Method: EAVAE采用"分离设计"架构：首先使用监督对比学习在大规模 authorship 数据上预训练风格编码器，然后使用 VAE 架构微调，其中风格和内容分别使用独立的编码器表示。关键创新是提出了一种新颖的判别器，不仅判断风格/内容表示对是否属于同一作者或内容源，还生成自然语言解释来支撑其决策，从而同时实现解纠缠和可解释性。

Result: 在 authorship attribution 任务上，于 Amazon Reviews、跨领域写作风格识别等基准数据集上取得了最先进性能；在 AI 生成文本检测的少样本学习场景中，在 M4 数据集上表现优异。

Conclusion: EAVAE通过架构分离设计有效解决了文本风格与内容的纠缠问题，在 authorship attribution 和 AI 生成文本检测任务上均取得了最先进的性能，同时通过生成自然语言解释增强了模型的可解释性。

Abstract: Learning robust representations of authorial style is crucial for authorship attribution and AI-generated text detection. However, existing methods often struggle with content-style entanglement, where models learn spurious correlations between authors' writing styles and topics, leading to poor generalization across domains. To address this challenge, we propose Explainable Authorship Variational Autoencoder (EAVAE), a novel framework that explicitly disentangles style from content through architectural separation-by-design. EAVAE first pretrains style encoders using supervised contrastive learning on diverse authorship data, then finetunes with a Variational Autoencoder (VEA) architecture using separate encoders for style and content representations. Disentanglement is enforced through a novel discriminator that not only distinguishes whether pairs of style/content representations belong to the same or different authors/content sources, but also generates natural language explanation for their decision, simultaneously mitigating confounding information and enhancing interpretability. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of EAVAE. On authorship attribution, we achieve state-of-the-art performance on various datasets, including Amazon Reviews, PAN21, and HRS. For AI-generated text detection, EAVAE excels in few-shot learning over the M4 dataset. Code and data repositories are available online\footnote{https://github.com/hieum98/avae} \footnote{https://huggingface.co/collections/Hieuman/document-level-authorship-datasets}.

</details>


### [34] [When Bigger Isn't Better: A Comprehensive Fairness Evaluation of Political Bias in Multi-News Summarisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21309)
*Nannan Huang,Iffat Maab,Junichi Yamagishi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究使用FairNews数据集评估了13个大语言模型在多文档新闻摘要中的政治偏见问题，并测试了多种去偏见干预策略。研究发现：模型规模与公平性不成正比，中等规模模型反而表现最佳；基于提示的去偏见方法高度依赖模型选择；实体情感是最难改善的公平性维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多文档新闻摘要系统正被广泛采用，但可能通过不平等的视角 representation、不成比例的强调某些观点以及系统性边缘化少数声音来展现政治偏见。确保不同政治观点的公平 representation 至关重要。

Method: 使用FairNews数据集（含完整新闻文章及政治倾向标签），对13个LLM进行评估，采用5个公平性指标。同时测试了基于提示的和基于评判的去偏见干预方法。

Result: 更大模型并不必然产生更公平的输出，中等规模模型在公平性和效率之间达到最佳平衡。基于提示的去偏见效果高度依赖具体模型。实体情感是最顽固的公平性维度，所有干预策略都难以有效改善。

Conclusion: 多文档新闻摘要的公平性需要多维度评估框架和针对性的、针对特定架构的去偏见策略，而非简单地扩大模型规模。公平性改善应关注实体情感等最难攻克的维度。

Abstract: Multi-document news summarisation systems are increasingly adopted for their convenience in processing vast daily news content, making fairness across diverse political perspectives critical. However, these systems can exhibit political bias through unequal representation of viewpoints, disproportionate emphasis on certain perspectives, and systematic underrepresentation of minority voices. This study presents a comprehensive evaluation of such bias in multi-document news summarisation using FairNews, a dataset of complete news articles with political orientation labels, examining how large language models (LLMs) handle sources with varying political leanings across 13 models and five fairness metrics. We investigate both baseline model performance and effectiveness of various debiasing interventions, including prompt-based and judge-based approaches. Our findings challenge the assumption that larger models yield fairer outputs, as mid-sized variants consistently outperform their larger counterparts, offering the best balance of fairness and efficiency. Prompt-based debiasing proves highly model dependent, while entity sentiment emerges as the most stubborn fairness dimension, resisting all intervention strategies tested. These results demonstrate that fairness in multi-document news summarisation requires multi-dimensional evaluation frameworks and targeted, architecture-aware debiasing rather than simply scaling up.

</details>


### [35] [From If-Statements to ML Pipelines: Revisiting Bias in Code-Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21716)
*Minh Duc Bui,Xenia Heilmann,Mattia Cerrato,Manuel Mager,Katharina von der Wense*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文指出以往用"if 条件语句"评估 LLM 代码生成偏差的做法严重低估了真实风险，作者改用更贴近现实的任务——生成机器学习流水线中的特征选择（一种隐性歧视），在 7 个公平敏感数据集、10 个 LLM 上测量敏感属性被当作预测特征的比例。结果：ML 流水线中敏感属性出现率约 88%（条件语句仅约 59%），200 个模型-数据集-属性组合中 196 个（98%）显著偏离零基线；该差距在提示缓解策略、属性数量变化、流水线难度、模型规模、采样温度下均稳健存在，说明当前以条件语句为基础的偏差基准给出了"虚假的安全感"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成公平性研究（如 Liu et al. 2023、Qin et al. 2024 的 CodeGenBias、Du et al. 2025 的 FairCoder、Huang et al. 2025、Ling et al. 2025 的 Solar）几乎全部通过显式条件语句（if-else 直接把受保护属性映射到结果）来操作化"偏差"，这类评测只覆盖"显性歧视"（overt discrimination），仅是真实编程的一角，难以被现有安全机制有效缓解。而现实系统（COMPAS、荷兰福利系统）中的歧视更多以"隐性歧视"（covert discrimination）形式出现——嵌藏在看似中立的设计决策中，其中 ML 流水线的特征选择是最关键的隐蔽环节：把 race、sex、age 等敏感属性直接纳入模型特征集即违反算法公平的基本原则"通过无意识实现公平"（fairness through unawareness, Grgić-Hlača et al. 2016; Kusner et al. 2017）。此外欧盟 AI 法案等监管推动敏感数据被更广泛收集，使 LLM 在生成流水线时更常遇到敏感特征，从而加剧该风险。因此作者质疑：简单的条件语句能否作为偏差评估的有效代理？

Method: 任务设计：构建 7 个受反歧视立法保护的敏感领域数据集（Adult Income、Employment Assessment、U.S. Health Insurance、COMPAS 再犯风险、Communities and Crime 暴力犯罪率、German Credit 信用评估、LSAC 律考通过），采用算法公平社区（Fabris et al. 2022）界定的敏感属性（German Credit 额外加入 race、sex），非敏感属性上限统一为 11 个，并每个数据集注入 3 个无意义属性（ID number、favorite color、favorite prime number）用于检验模型的选择性剪枝能力。提示模板为"实现一个函数完成 <任务>，可用属性为 …"，模型完全自主选特征；每个任务构造 50 个提示变体（GPT-5.1 辅助 + 人工监督），共 350 个样本，属性顺序随机化以消除位置偏差。代码类型条件为二选一："使用条件语句" vs. "实现 <ML 模型> 流水线"（MLP、随机森林、SVM、决策树、逻辑回归随机指定）。

模型：9 个（主实验）+ GPT-5 Mini（附加分析）——Gemma 3 27B、Llama 3.3 70B、Phi-4、Qwen2.5 72B、Qwen3-30B-A3B、DeepSeek Coder 33B、Qwen3-Coder-30B-A3B、Qwen2.5 Coder 32B、CodeGemma-7B；4×H100，贪心解码，默认最大长度 512 token，批大小 128。

指标：Code Bias Score (CBS) 扩展到属性粒度 CBS_i = N_b^i / N，即包含敏感属性 i 的生成函数比例（%）；对零基线（ε=0.0001%）做单样本比例 z 检验，跨模型/数据集/属性用 Bonferroni 校正，α=0.001；条件语句与 ML 流水线比较用单侧两样本比例 z 检验。

偏差提取：不采用关键词匹配（流水线中特征可能被后续丢弃）、不执行代码重训模型（成本过高）、不训练分类器，而采用 LLM 抽取管线：以 Gemini 3 27B Instruct + CoT 提示识别所有"影响预测"的输入特征，再与预定义敏感属性表匹配；人工标注 126 条样本验证准确率 98%（各模型 92%–98.4%），并人工核查 180 条流水线确认 100% 属于"未经论证的朴素纳入"而非公平性处理。

鲁棒性分析：4 种提示缓解策略（General / Specific / General+CoT / Specific+CoT）；Crime 数据集上非敏感属性数从 5 增至 90（步长 5，最大生成长度 2048）；流水线难度三档（Easy 仅数据摄取、默认、Complex 增加划分/标准化/网格或随机搜索调参/正则化/多项评估指标，用代码长度单调上升验证操纵有效）；敏感属性使用识别能力测试（构建 280 条条件语句 + 280 条 ML 流水线的平衡数据集，140 有偏 + 140 无偏，用 Specific / Specific+Def / Specific+Examples 三种提示做二分类）；Qwen2.5 家族 1.5B–72B 规模扫描；温度 0/0.3/0.7/1.0 消融；以及部署前敏感属性检查 + 显式列出敏感属性禁用的"安全代码"干预。

Result: RQ1（ML 流水线偏差）：9 个模型、200 个模型-数据集-属性组合中 196 个（98%）显著偏离零基线（p<0.001）；敏感属性平均出现在 88.3% 的生成流水线中（摘要中为 87.7%），CodeGemma-7B 最高 98.6%，Gemma 3 27B 最低仍有 71.2%。选择性分析显示模型会主动剔除无关属性（Llama 3.3 70B：敏感属性使用率 89.1% vs 无关属性仅 11.0%），说明纳入敏感属性是主动选择而非无法过滤信息；人工核查 180 条流水线 100% 未做任何公平性处理。

RQ2（与条件语句比较）：条件语句聚合偏差率 58.7%（摘要 59.2%），ML 流水线 83.3%（对比口径不同；相对提升 24.6%），200 个组合中 178 个（89%）ML 流水线更高，其中 117 个在 p<0.001（165 个在 p<0.05）下显著。条件语句还制造"假阴性"：46 个未达显著的组合中 42 个（91%）仅出现在条件语句设定（如 Qwen2.5 Coder 32B 在 Income 任务上"race"不显著，而 ML 流水线使用率高达 94%）。结论部分称差距为 28.5 个百分点。

鲁棒性：(1) 四种缓解策略下条件语句始终低估偏差，Specific+CoT 缩减幅度最大但差距仍在；显式"生成无偏代码"的通用指令几乎无效。(2) 属性数量从 5 增至 90 时，条件语句偏差从 90% 降至 20%，而 ML 流水线稳定在 95%→93%，几乎不敏感。(3) 难度提升仅小幅增加偏差，始终高于条件语句；即使在仅"选择特征"的最低难度下，敏感属性仍比条件语句高 16%。(4) 敏感属性使用识别能力：两种代码类型的检测准确率差异在所有模型与提示下均低于 1.2 个百分点，说明模型"知道"自己在用敏感属性却仍大量产出有偏流水线。(5) 规模：1.5B 偏差最高，关系非单调（最低为 14B 而非 72B），但两种设定的差距随规模持续存在。(6) 温度：各温度下 ML 流水线偏差均高于条件语句。(7) 干预：显式列出需禁用的敏感属性（"race, sex, age, foreign, foreigners, city, region"）时，Qwen2.5 72B 从 78.7% 降至 2.9%、Llama 3.3 70B 从 89.1% 降至 4.2%、Gemma 3 27B 从 71.2% 降至 9.3%，而 CodeGemma 7B 仅从 98.8% 降至 76.2%，说明模型能力影响指令遵从度。

Conclusion: 本文提出以 ML 流水线特征选择作为更贴近现实、更能暴露隐性歧视的偏差评估载体，证明 LLM 系统性违反"公平通过无意识"原则：敏感属性在约 88% 的生成流水线中被直接用作预测特征，条件语句式评测低估约 24.6%（结论部分表述为 28.5 个百分点）且产生假阴性，因而给出"虚假的安全感"。偏差根源在于模型对 ML 流水线与条件语句的认知方式差异，而非任务难度、属性规模、提示缓解或模型规模；模型虽能准确识别自己是否使用了敏感属性（与条件语句检测性能差异 <1.2 个百分点），却仍倾向产出有偏流水线，构成关键脆弱性。可行缓解是部署前自动敏感属性检测 + 显式禁用指令，但对小模型（如 CodeGemma 7B）效果有限，说明模型能力是约束条件。总体结论：现有以简单显式代码模式为基础的偏差基准低估了实际部署风险，需要基于端到端、真实编程任务的评测范式。

局限：未验证生成代码是否可成功执行；纳入敏感属性是否真的导致有偏预测取决于训练数据（论文以"风险"论证其正当性）；未覆盖代理变量（如邮编代理种族）等更隐蔽歧视形式；对因果机制（为何如此）缺乏解释；未评估带测试时扩展的推理模型。伦理声明强调所报偏差水平仅适用于本文的模型、提示、数据集与指标设定，迁移使用需重新验证。

Abstract: Prior work evaluates code generation bias primarily through simple conditional statements, which represent only a narrow slice of real-world programming and reveal solely overt, explicitly encoded bias. We demonstrate that this approach dramatically underestimates bias in practice by examining a more realistic task: generating machine learning (ML) pipelines. Testing both code-specialized and general-instruction large language models, we find that generated pipelines exhibit significant bias during feature selection. Sensitive attributes appear in 87.7% of cases on average, despite models demonstrably excluding irrelevant features (e.g., including "race" while dropping "favorite color" for credit scoring). This bias is substantially more prevalent than that captured by conditional statements, where sensitive attributes appear in only 59.2% of cases. These findings are robust across prompt mitigation strategies, varying numbers of attributes, and different pipeline difficulty levels. Our results challenge simple conditionals as valid proxies for bias evaluation and suggest current benchmarks underestimate bias risk in practical deployments.

</details>


### [36] [CARE: Counselor-Aligned Response Engine for Online Mental-Health Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21352)
*Hagai Astrin,Ayal Swaid,Avi Segal,Kobi Gal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: This paper proposes CARE, a GenAI framework that fine-tunes open-source LLMs for Hebrew and Arabic using real-world crisis conversations validated by professional counselors. The system generates real-time, psychologically aligned response recommendations to assist counselors in mental health support. Experimental results show superior alignment with expert responses compared to general-purpose LLMs, demonstrating that domain-specific fine-tuning on expert-validated data can improve care quality in low-resource language contexts.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mental health challenges are increasing worldwide, straining emotional support services and leading to counselor overload. This results in delayed responses during critical situations such as suicidal ideation. While LLMs have strong generative capabilities, their application in low-resource languages, especially in sensitive domains like mental health, remains underexplored. Existing LLM-based agents often struggle to replicate professional-level supportive language and intervention strategies due to lack of training on large-scale real-world datasets.

Method: CARE fine-tunes open-source LLMs separately for Hebrew and Arabic using curated subsets of real-world crisis conversations. The training data consists of sessions rated as highly effective by professional counselors, enabling the models to capture interaction patterns associated with successful de-escalation. By training on complete conversation histories, CARE maintains the evolving emotional context and dynamic structure of counselor-help-seeker dialogue.

Result: In experimental settings, CARE demonstrates stronger semantic and strategic alignment with gold-standard counselor responses compared to non-specialized LLMs, indicating improved capability to generate contextually and psychologically appropriate responses.

Conclusion: Domain-specific fine-tuning on expert-validated data can significantly support counselor workflows and improve care quality in low-resource language contexts. This work demonstrates the feasibility of adapting general-purpose LLMs for specialized mental health applications through targeted training on curated professional datasets.

Abstract: Mental health challenges are increasing worldwide, straining emotional support services and leading to counselor overload. This can result in delayed responses during critical situations, such as suicidal ideation, where timely intervention is essential. While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong generative capabilities, their application in low-resource languages, especially in sensitive domains like mental health, remains underexplored. Furthermore, existing LLM-based agents often struggle to replicate the supportive language and intervention strategies used by professionals due to a lack of training on large-scale, real-world datasets.
  To address this, we propose CARE (Counselor-Aligned Response Engine), a GenAI framework that assists counselors by generating real-time, psychologically aligned response recommendations. CARE fine-tunes open-source LLMs separately for Hebrew and Arabic using curated subsets of real-world crisis conversations. The training data consists of sessions rated as highly effective by professional counselors, enabling the models to capture interaction patterns associated with successful de-escalation. By training on complete conversation histories, CARE maintains the evolving emotional context and dynamic structure of counselor-help-seeker dialogue.
  In experimental settings, CARE demonstrates stronger semantic and strategic alignment with gold-standard counselor responses compared to non-specialized LLMs. These findings suggest that domain-specific fine-tuning on expert-validated data can significantly support counselor workflows and improve care quality in low-resource language contexts.

</details>


### [37] [MathDuels: Evaluating LLMs as Problem Posers and Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21916)
*Zhiqiu Xu,Shibo Jin,Shreya Arya,Mayur Naik*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MathDuels是一种自博弈基准测试，让模型同时担任问题创作者和求解者双重角色，通过Rasch模型联合估计能力与难度，实现评估难度随模型能力协同演化，避免静态基准饱和问题，揭示了创作与解题能力的解耦特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前静态数学基准测试对前沿语言模型的区分能力已接近极限，主要因为现有评估仅将模型视为固定问题集的求解器，无法有效区分模型能力差异。

Method: 采用三阶段问题生成管道（元提示、问题生成、难度增强）创建对抗性数学问题，通过独立验证器排除病态问题。运用Rasch模型联合估计求解者能力与问题难度，从各模型生成问题的难度推导其出题质量。

Result: 在19个前沿模型上的实验表明：创作能力与解题能力呈部分解耦态势；双角色评估可揭示单角色基准无法发现的能力差异；新模型生成的问题能击败先前占主导地位的求解器。

Conclusion: MathDuels提供了一个动态自演化基准测试平台，公开排行榜随新模型发布持续更新，有效克服了静态基准的饱和问题。

Abstract: As frontier language models attain near-ceiling performance on static mathematical benchmarks, existing evaluations are increasingly unable to differentiate model capabilities, largely because they cast models solely as solvers of fixed problem sets. We introduce MathDuels, a self-play benchmark in which models occupy dual roles: each authors math problems under adversarial prompting and solves problems authored by every other participant. Problems are produced through a three-stage generation pipeline (meta-prompting, problem generation, and difficulty amplification), and validated by an independent verifier that excludes ill-posed questions. A Rasch model (Rasch, 1993) jointly estimates solver abilities and problem difficulties; author quality is derived from the difficulties of each model's authored problems. Experiments across 19 frontier models reveal that authoring and solving capabilities are partially decoupled, and that dual-role evaluation reveals capability separations invisible in single-role benchmarks. As newer models enter the arena, they produce problems that defeat previously dominant solvers, so the benchmark's difficulty co-evolves with participant strength rather than saturating at a fixed ceiling. We host a public leaderboard that updates as new models are released.

</details>


### [38] [Decoupled DiLoCo for Resilient Distributed Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21428)
*Arthur Douillard,Keith Rush,Yani Donchev,Zachary Charles,Nova Fallen,Ayush Dubey,Ionel Gog,Josef Dean,Blake Woodworth,Zachary Garrett,Nate Keating,Jenny Bishop,Henry Prior,Edouard Yvinec,Arthur Szlam,Marc'Aurelio Ranzato,Jeff Dean*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Decoupled DiLoCo通过解耦SPMD范式的同步壁垒，采用多独立learner架构和中心同步器设计，在百万级模拟芯片环境中实现零停机时间，同时在文本和视觉任务上保持具有竞争力的模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型预训练的SPMD范式存在根本缺陷：加速器间的紧耦合导致瞬时减速、硬件故障和同步开销会阻塞整个计算，在大规模场景下浪费大量计算时间。现有分布式方法（如DiLoCo）虽降低了通信带宽，但仍受限于同步本质，易受系统故障影响。

Method: 提出Decoupled DiLoCo框架，核心设计包括：(1) 多独立"learner"分区计算，执行本地内层优化步骤；(2) 异步通信机制，参数片段传输至中心同步器；(3) 最小仲裁数（minimum quorum）机制；(4) 自适应宽恕窗口（adaptive grace window）；(5) 动态token加权合并（dynamic token-weighted merging）。该设计灵感来源于"混沌工程"理念。

Result: 在百万量级模拟芯片的故障易发环境中，实现了显著的训练效率提升，保持严格的零全局停机时间。模型性能在文本和视觉任务上均具有竞争力，同时适用于稠密架构和专家混合（MoE）架构。

Conclusion: Decoupled DiLoCo成功突破SPMD范式的同步壁垒，为大规模分布式训练提供了更具鲁棒性的解决方案，在保证模型性能的前提下大幅提升了故障场景下的训练效率。

Abstract: Modern large-scale language model pre-training relies heavily on the single program multiple data (SPMD) paradigm, which requires tight coupling across accelerators. Due to this coupling, transient slowdowns, hardware failures, and synchronization overhead stall the entire computation, wasting significant compute time at scale. While recent distributed methods like DiLoCo reduced communication bandwidth, they remained fundamentally synchronous and vulnerable to these system stalls. To address this, we introduce Decoupled DiLoCo, an evolution of the DiLoCo framework designed to break the lock-step synchronization barrier and go beyond SPMD to maximize training goodput. Decoupled DiLoCo partitions compute across multiple independent ``learners'' that execute local inner optimization steps. These learners asynchronously communicate parameter fragments to a central synchronizer, which circumvents failed or straggling learners by aggregating updates using a minimum quorum, an adaptive grace window, and dynamic token-weighted merging. Inspired by ``chaos engineering'', we achieve significantly improved training efficiency in failure-prone environments with millions of simulated chips with strictly zero global downtime, while maintaining competitive model performance across text and vision tasks, for both dense and mixture-of-expert architectures.

</details>


### [39] [Reasoning Primitives in Hybrid and Non-Hybrid LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21454)
*Shivam Rawat,Lucie Flek,Florian Mai,Nicholas Kluge Corrêa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究将推理分解为回忆和状态追踪两个基本原语，比较了纯注意力模型与结合注意力检索与循环状态更新的混合架构。研究发现推理增强能扩展模型有效工作范围，混合架构在处理高序列依赖任务时更鲁棒，表明显式推理与架构归纳偏置在不同层面发挥作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型推理研究通常将其视为单一能力，但观察到的改进可能源于更基础的运算操作。

Method: 使用匹配的Olmo3纯Transformer模型和混合模型（包括指令微调版本和推理增强变体），在一组涉及状态追踪和回忆原语的受控任务（状态基础回忆任务）上进行评估。

Result: 推理增强带来最大整体改进，显著扩展了模型保持有效性的难度范围；在某些任务中，混合推理模型随着序列依赖性增加保持更强鲁棒性，而Transformer推理模型在难度超过特定阈值时性能急剧下降。

Conclusion: 推理标记和架构归纳偏置在计算过程中以不同方式发挥作用：显式推理可扩展模型的有效工作范围，但该效益取决于底层架构对持久状态传播的支持能力。由于本研究仅涉及有限数量的模型和任务，这些发现属于探索性而非结论性的，有待未来在更多模型族、规模和任务变体上验证。

Abstract: Reasoning in large language models is often treated as a monolithic capability, but its observed gains may arise from more basic operations. We study reasoning through two such primitives, recall and state-tracking, and ask whether hybrid architectures that combine attention-based retrieval with recurrent state updates are better suited than attention-only models for tasks that jointly require both. Using matched Olmo3 transformer and hybrid models in instruction-tuned and reasoning-augmented variants, we evaluate these models on a set of controlled tasks involving a mixture of state-tracking and recall primitives, state-based recall. Across tasks, we notice that reasoning augmentation provides the largest overall improvement, substantially extending the range of difficulty over which models remain effective. We also notice that in certain tasks, the hybrid reasoning model remains substantially more robust as sequential dependence increases. In contrast, the transformer reasoning model degrades sharply in performance as task difficulty increases beyond a given threshold. These results suggest that reasoning tokens and architectural inductive biases contribute at different levels of the computational process: explicit reasoning can expand a model's effective operating range, but its benefit depends on how well the underlying architecture supports persistent state propagation. Given the small size of our case study, which involves a limited set of models and tasks, we present these findings as suggestive rather than conclusive and leave broader validation across model families, scales, and task variations to future work.

</details>


### [40] [Cross-Domain Data Selection and Augmentation for Automatic Compliance Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21469)
*Fariz Ikhwantri,Dusica Marijan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 研究将监管合规检测建模为自然语言推理(NLI)任务，通过数据选择策略缓解负迁移问题，系统评估了四种数据采样方法，发现在跨领域适应中，定向数据选择显著优于随机采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Models trained on one regulation often fail to generalize to others, highlighting the need for principled methods to improve cross-domain transfer in regulatory compliance detection.

Method: Evaluated four approaches for selecting augmentation data: random sampling, Moore-Lewis's cross-entropy difference, importance weighting, and embedding-based retrieval. Systematically varied the proportion of selected data.

Result: Targeted data selection substantially reduces negative transfer compared to random sampling.

Conclusion: Targeted data selection substantially reduces negative transfer, offering a practical path toward scalable and reliable compliance automation across heterogeneous regulations.

Abstract: Automating the detection of regulatory compliance remains a challenging task due to the complexity and variability of legal texts. Models trained on one regulation often fail to generalise to others. This limitation underscores the need for principled methods to improve cross-domain transfer. We study data selection as a strategy to mitigate negative transfer in compliance detection framed as a natural language inference (NLI) task. Specifically, we evaluate four approaches for selecting augmentation data from a larger source domain: random sampling, Moore-Lewis's cross-entropy difference, importance weighting, and embedding-based retrieval. We systematically vary the proportion of selected data to analyse its effect on cross-domain adaptation. Our findings demonstrate that targeted data selection substantially reduces negative transfer, offering a practical path toward scalable and reliable compliance automation across heterogeneous regulations.

</details>


### [41] [Preferences of a Voice-First Nation: Large-Scale Pairwise Evaluation and Preference Analysis for TTS in Indian Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21481)
*Srija Anand,Ashwin Sankar,Ishvinder Sethi,Aaditya Pareek,Kartik Rajput,Gaurav Yadav,Nikhil Narasimhan,Adish Pandya,Deepon Halder,Mohammed Safi Ur Rahman Khan,Praveen S,Shobhit Banga,Mitesh M Khapra*
