Method: 1) 改编 AttnLRP：采用其改进的 Gradient×Input 近似计算方式，并把梯度分配（均匀再分配规则）扩展到 DNABERT-2 特有的 ALiBi（query/key 与距离偏置三者均分）与 GLU（门控分支与内容分支均分）组件。2) 提出 4 种（去）聚合策略以桥接粒度：求和 (a)、均值 (b) 的 token→核苷酸聚合，以及直接传递 (c)、等分 (d) 的核苷酸级还原；其中 (a)(d) 保持相关性总量守恒，(b)(c) 不守恒但避免稀释重要位点。3) 数据：基因组基准中的 Human Non-TATA Promoters（251 bp，36,131 样本）与 Drosophila Enhancers（中位长 2,142 bp，6,914 样本）。4) 模型：DNABERT-2（BPE+ALiBi）微调两个分类任务，与基准 CNN 及改造后的 DeepSTARR（输入长扩展到 3,061 bp）对比。5) 指标：连续 Jaccard 相似度 (CJ) 用于跨模型对齐；Gini 指数与熵衡量稀疏性与复杂度；忠实性用 MIF/LIF 扰动（替换为 N/UNK、随机核苷酸、互补碱基）后预测分数变化衡量；定位用 TF-MoDISco 提取 PWM 并通过 TOMTOM 与 JASPAR 比对。

Result: 定性上 AttnLRP 归因呈现明显的非随机连续模式；大量相关性集中在 CLS/SEP 特殊 token 上（序列越长越显著），故排除并重归一化后再分析。相似度上，非守恒策略 (b)(c) 的 CJ 高于守恒策略 (a)(d)；非 TATA 启动子上 CJ 多为正（中度一致），果蝇增强子上接近零（一致度低，因序列长、信号噪声比低），负类一致度更低。稀疏性/复杂度上，token 级熵天然低于核苷酸级；短序列上 CNN 更稀疏（Gini 0.51 vs 0.42），长序列上 DNABERT-2 更稀疏（0.64 vs 0.50），总体两者相当。忠实性上，按 MIF 扰动 UNK 或互补碱基时 DNABERT-2 的解释普遍给出比 CNN 更大的预测分数下降，随机扰动在 50% 时 CNN 略高；LIF 扰动两者变化均很小，说明低相关位点确实不重要。定位上，TF-MoDISco 从 DNABERT-2 解释提取的每个模式都能匹配已知 motif：非 TATA 启动子聚焦 GC-box（MA0528.1），与正类 GC 含量 62% vs 负类 48% 一致，DNABERT-2 的模式更集中于少数核苷酸；果蝇增强子两模型均收敛到同一 top motif MA1841.1（与增强子抑制相关）。

Conclusion: AttnLRP 经 ALiBi/GLU 适配后可为 DNABERT-2 提供与 CNN 上 LRP 相当可靠、忠实的解释，且能定位于已知生物学注释，说明基于 Transformer 的 gLM 与 CNN 一样能支持生物学洞见与假设生成；跨架构归因的可比性通过（去）聚合策略得以建立。局限与展望：仅研究了 DNABERT-2 与两个数据集，结论向其他 gLM、不同序列长度/物种/任务的可迁移性仍待验证，未来将扩展到更多模型、数据集并考察 AttnLRP 之外的其他解释方法。

Abstract: Explaining deep neural network predictions on genome sequences enables biological insight and hypothesis generation-often of greater interest than predictive performance alone. While explanations of convolutional neural networks (CNNs) have been shown to capture relevant patterns in genome sequences, it is unclear whether this transfers to more expressive Transformer-based genome language models (gLMs). To answer this question, we adapt AttnLRP, an extension of layer-wise relevance propagation to the attention mechanism, and apply it to the state-of-the-art gLM DNABERT-2. Thereby, we propose strategies to transfer explanations from token and nucleotide level. We evaluate the adaption of AttnLRP on genomic datasets using multiple metrics. Further, we provide an extensive comparison between the explanations of DNABERT-2 and a baseline CNN. Our results demonstrate that AttnLRP yields reliable explanations corresponding to known biological patterns. Hence, like CNNs, gLMs can also help derive biological insights. This work contributes to the explainability of gLMs and addresses the comparability of relevance attributions across different architectures.

</details>


### [194] [Fairness under uncertainty in sequential decisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21711)
*Michelle Seng Ah Lee,Kirtan Padh,David Watson,Niki Kilbertus,Jatinder Singh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了顺序决策场景中的公平机器学习问题，指出模型不确定性、反馈不确定性和预测不确定性在不同群体间分布不均会导致系统性不公平。文章提出了一种统一的不确定性分类框架，通过反事实逻辑和强化学习形式化建模，并验证了不确定性感知探索策略能够改善弱势群体的结果方差同时保持机构目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公平机器学习虽然在监督学习中研究充分，但许多实际应用是在线顺序决策系统，历史排斥和选择性反馈导致代表性不足群体数据稀少，决策者面临未观察反事实的不确定性。现有方法忽视不确定性在群体间的非均匀分布，导致对弱势群体的系统性伤害累积。

Method: 提出不确定性三分类框架（模型不确定性、反馈不确定性、预测不确定性）；基于反事实逻辑和强化学习对模型不确定性和反馈不确定性进行形式化建模；通过模拟数据实验验证不同偏差水平下不公平性的产生机制；设计不确定性感知探索策略测试其对公平性指标的影响。

Result: 不平衡的不确定性和选择性反馈会产生显著差异；不确定性感知探索策略能够改变公平性指标；可在保持机构预期效用目标的同时，降低弱势群体的结果方差。

Conclusion: 该框架为从业者提供了诊断、审计和治理公平风险的工具。当不公平性源于不确定性而非偶然噪声时，纳入不确定性考量对实现公平有效的决策至关重要。

Abstract: Fair machine learning (ML) methods help identify and mitigate the risk that algorithms encode or automate social injustices. Algorithmic approaches alone cannot resolve structural inequalities, but they can support socio-technical decision systems by surfacing discriminatory biases, clarifying trade-offs, and enabling governance. Although fairness is well studied in supervised learning, many real ML applications are online and sequential, with prior decisions informing future ones. Each decision is taken under uncertainty due to unobserved counterfactuals and finite samples, with dire consequences for under-represented groups, systematically under-observed due to historical exclusion and selective feedback. A bank cannot know whether a denied loan would have been repaid, and may have less data on marginalized populations.
  This paper introduces a taxonomy of uncertainty in sequential decision-making -- model, feedback, and prediction uncertainty -- providing shared vocabulary for assessing systems where uncertainty is unevenly distributed across groups. We formalize model and feedback uncertainty via counterfactual logic and reinforcement learning, and illustrate harms to decision makers (unrealized gains/losses) and subjects (compounding exclusion, reduced access) of policies that ignore the unobserved space. Algorithmic examples show it is possible to reduce outcome variance for disadvantaged groups while preserving institutional objectives (e.g. expected utility). Experiments on data simulated with varying bias show how unequal uncertainty and selective feedback produce disparities, and how uncertainty-aware exploration alters fairness metrics. The framework equips practitioners to diagnose, audit, and govern fairness risks. Where uncertainty drives unfairness rather than incidental noise, accounting for it is essential to fair and effective decision-making.

</details>


### [195] [Transferable Physics-Informed Representations via Closed-Form Head Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21761)
*Jian Cheng Wong,Isaac Yin Chung Lai,Pao-Hsiung Chiu,Chin Chun Ooi,Abhishek Gupta,Yew-Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Pi-PINN框架，通过在共享嵌入空间中学习可迁移的物理信息表示，结合闭式伪逆头适配，实现对新PDE实例的快速求解，无需额外训练数据即可达到比典型PINN快100-1000倍、误差低10-100倍的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前PINN方法在缺乏训练样本时难以有效预测或求解新的PDE问题，泛化能力不足。同时，多任务学习损失与物理约束损失之间的协同作用尚未被充分研究，限制了PINN的设计优化。

Method: 提出基于快速伪逆的Pi-PINN框架：1）在共享嵌入空间学习可迁移的物理信息表示；2）通过最小二乘最优伪逆在PDE约束下进行闭式头适配，实现快速求解；3）联合优化多任务学习损失与物理损失函数。

Result: 在泊松方程、亥姆霍兹方程和 Burgers 方程上的实验表明：Pi-PINN在无数据条件下可生成比典型PINN快100-1000倍的预测；在仅两个训练样本下，预测相对误差比纯数据驱动模型低10-100倍。

Conclusion: 可迁移表示与闭式头适配的结合能够显著提升PINN在PDE族和科学工程应用中的效率和泛化能力，为构建高效、可扩展的物理信息学习方法提供了新范式。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) have garnered significant interest for their potential in solving partial differential equations (PDEs) that govern a wide range of physical phenomena. By incorporating physical laws into the learning process, PINN models have demonstrated the ability to learn physical outcomes reasonably well. However, current PINN approaches struggle to predict or solve new PDEs effectively when there is a lack of training examples, indicating they do not generalize well to unseen problem instances. In this paper, we present a transferable learning approach for PINNs premised on a fast Pseudoinverse PINN framework (Pi-PINN). Pi-PINN learns a transferable physics-informed representation in a shared embedding space and enables rapid solving of both known and unknown PDE instances via closed-form head adaptation using a least-squares-optimal pseudoinverse under PDE constraints. We further investigate the synergies between data-driven multi-task learning loss and physics-informed loss, providing insights into the design of more performant PINNs. We demonstrate the effectiveness of Pi-PINN on various PDE problems, including Poisson's equation, Helmholtz equation, and Burgers' equation, achieving fast and accurate physics-informed solutions without requiring any data for unseen instances. Pi-PINN can produce predictions 100-1000 times faster than a typical PINN, while producing predictions with 10-100 times lower relative error than a typical data-driven model even with only two training samples. Overall, our findings highlight the potential of transferable representations with closed-form head adaptation to enhance the efficiency and generalization of PINNs across PDE families and scientific and engineering applications.

</details>


### [196] [An effective variant of the Hartigan $k$-means algorithm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21798)
*François Clément,Stefan Steinerberger*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: k-means聚类问题的算法改进研究。对比Lloyd算法(1957)和Hartigan算法(1975)，发现Hartigan算法表现更优(通常提升5%-10%)。本文进一步提出Hartigan方法的微小变体，可额外提升2%-5%，且该改进随维度或k值增大而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hartigan算法虽已证明优于经典的Lloyd算法，但仍有优化空间。研究旨在探索是否存在进一步提升聚类算法性能的微小修改。

Method: 对Hartigan算法进行微小变体调整。通过实验对比原Hartigan算法与改进版本在各种参数条件下的性能表现。

Result: Hartigan算法的微小变体可额外获得2%-5%的性能提升。该改进效果随维度(dimension)或聚类数(k)增加而增强。

Conclusion: 对经典聚类算法的微小改进即可带来显著性能提升，且算法优化潜力随问题规模增大而更加明显。该发现为聚类算法的进一步研究提供了新方向。

Abstract: The k-means problem is perhaps the classical clustering problem and often synonymous with Lloyd's algorithm (1957). It has become clear that Hartigan's algorithm (1975) gives better results in almost all cases, Telgarsky-Vattani note a typical improvement of $5\%$ -- $10\%$. We point out that a very minor variation of Hartigan's method leads to another $2\%$ -- $5\%$ improvement; the improvement tends to become larger when either dimension or $k$ increase.

</details>


### [197] [Quotient-Space Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21809)
*Yixian Xu,Yusong Wang,Shengjie Luo,Kaiyuan Gao,Tianyu He,Di He,Chang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文为扩散生成模型在商空间上的应用建立了形式化框架，并将其应用于遵循SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成。相比传统等变扩散模型，该框架简化了学习难度并保证了采样器的正确性，在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上超越了以往的对称性处理方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散生成模型在科学领域展现了强大能力，但在处理具有对称性的任务时存在挑战。某些问题的目标分布本质上定义在关于群作用的商空间上，传统方法需要学习群作用对应的分量，或者依赖启发式对齐策略但缺乏恰当的采样器。

Method: 建立了在一般商空间上进行扩散建模的形式化框架，并应用于遵循特殊欧几里得群SE(3)对称性的分子结构生成。该框架通过约减学习群作用对应分量的必要性，简化了学习难度。

Result: 经验验证表明，该方法在小分子和蛋白质结构生成任务上均有效。原则性的商空间扩散模型在性能上优于以往的对称性处理方法，且采样器能够保证恢复目标分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的商空间扩散模型为处理科学数据对称性提供了新的原则性框架，理论上简化了学习过程，实践中提供了恰当的采样器保证，在分子结构生成任务中展现了优越性能。

Abstract: Diffusion-based generative models have reformed generative AI, and have enabled new capabilities in the science domain, for example, generating 3D structures of molecules. Due to the intrinsic problem structure of certain tasks, there is often a symmetry in the system, which identifies objects that can be converted by a group action as equivalent, hence the target distribution is essentially defined on the quotient space with respect to the group. In this work, we establish a formal framework for diffusion modeling on a general quotient space, and apply it to molecular structure generation which follows the special Euclidean group $\text{SE}(3)$ symmetry. The framework reduces the necessity of learning the component corresponding to the group action, hence simplifies learning difficulty over conventional group-equivariant diffusion models, and the sampler guarantees recovering the target distribution, while heuristic alignment strategies lack proper samplers. The arguments are empirically validated on structure generation for small molecules and proteins, indicating that the principled quotient-space diffusion model provides a new framework that outperforms previous symmetry treatments.

</details>


### [198] [GFlowState: Visualizing the Training of Generative Flow Networks Beyond the Reward](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21830)
*Florian Holeczek,Andreas Hinterreiter,Alex Hernandez-Garcia,Marc Streit,Christina Humer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GFlowState 是一个面向生成流网络（GFlowNet）训练过程的可视分析系统。它通过四个协同联动的视图——样本排名（bump chart）、状态投影（六边形分箱 + UMAP）、轨迹有向无环图（DAG View）与转移热力图（Transition Heatmap）——将 GFlowNet 采样轨迹的结构、采样概率与奖励的对应关系以及训练中的概率演化暴露出来，帮助开发者调试训练、发现欠探索区域并检测模式崩塌。系统以 Python + Plotly Dash 实现，样本与图结构存入 SQL 数据库，并通过四个可插拔接口支持自定义环境集成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GFlowNet 作为按奖励函数比例采样的生成式框架，已在分子、材料（电催化剂、抗体）等科学发现任务中展现出生成多样高价值候选的能力，但其训练动力学仍难以解释与诊断：现有工具（如 TensorBoard、Weights & Biases）只能跟踪损失、奖励等聚合指标，无法揭示模型如何探索样本空间、如何构造采样轨迹、以及采样概率如何随训练演化；轨迹状态空间呈组合爆炸并形成 DAG，超出人工检视与常规监控的极限。同时，已有面向强化学习、序列/语言模型、CNN 的可视化系统与 GFlowNet 的学习范式结构紧耦合，无法直接迁移。作者与 Mila 的 GFlowNet 开发者通过迭代访谈，识别出三类核心分析任务：轨迹分析（Traj）、生成样本分析（Samp）、训练动态分析（Dyn），共 9 项子任务，并据此提出定制化可视化分析方案的需求。

Method: 1）任务识别：面向模型开发者（WHY: debugging and improving models），通过与 Mila 研究者的反复协作反馈，归纳出 Traj 1-2（跟踪逐步采样行为/决策、识别导致样本生成的因素）、Samp 1-4（验证采样与奖励成比例、定位高低奖励/损失区域、识别离群样本、与验证集参考分布比较）、Dyn 1-3（分析训练中转移概率的漂移、分析样本空间探索动态、检测模式崩塌）。2）系统设计四个视图：① Sample Ranking，以 bump chart 展示按奖励/损失/自定义指标排序的 Top-N 样本随迭代的排名变化，线条竖直变动指示新探索、水平平坦段提示模式崩塌；② State Projection，对样本与验证集做维度降维（网格环境为二维，复杂环境用 UMAP），投影后以六边形分箱聚合，颜色可编码奖励-采样概率相关性、训练指标（奖励/损失）、验证集覆盖度（基于优势比缩放至 [-1,1]，0 表示与全局比例一致，+1/-1 表示仅含验证集/仅含生成样本）；③ DAG View，将所有采样轨迹合并为 DAG，先做预处理——线性链（入度出度均为 1）折叠为单条转移、相同转移合并并按聚合指标着色，支持从根节点自底向上交互式展开与折叠，并处理 DAG 的多父节点问题；④ Transition Heatmap，横轴为迭代、纵轴为按指标（转移概率、概率方差、转移频率）排名的转移，悬停显示源/目标状态与转移概率演化折线，支持前向/后向转移。3）交互与工作流：中心迭代区间滑块统一过滤所有视图；任一视图中的样本/转移选择会跨视图高亮并在 DAG View 中显示完整轨迹；悬停显示可定制的状态表示。4）实现：Python + Plotly Dash，训练样本与节点/边两表存入 SQL 以支持长训练与大规模 DAG 的重复查询；环境集成仅需实现四个函数（状态到唯一字符串、特征表示、单状态可视表示、多状态汇总表示）。

Result: 通过网格环境（20×20、四个角模式、10,000 次迭代）与晶体环境（Pt/Pd 组成、立方空间群 225/229、密度奖励、50,000 次迭代、覆盖全空间的验证集）两个案例研究，验证了系统的有效性。在网格案例中，专家借助 Sample Ranking 迅速定位约 4,500 迭代时模型发现右上高奖励模式、约 5,000 迭代发现其余两个模式；State Projection 显示高损失集中于网格中心（因该低奖励区域采样稀少、难以建模）；Transition Heatmap 揭示高排名转移多为 4,500 迭代之后才被采样、对应网格边缘附近转移；DAG View 显示通往左下模式的转移概率随训练下降，印证模型正转向多样化采样以更好地刻画真实奖励地形。在晶体案例中，专家发现某个 Pt2/空间群 225/晶格长度≈2 的结构主导高奖励区且很早被发现；早期 State Projection 的优势比显示大量样本空间尚未覆盖，后期模型泛化明显改善；非聚合投影呈现按组成分簇、晶格长度决定密度与奖励的清晰结构。专家一致认为交互式轨迹 DAG 探索是最有价值的组件，能显著加速轨迹调试并可借助领域知识检查生成状态的物理合理性，同时强调子环境间依赖（先生成空间群再约束组成）分析的必要性；并指出奖励与前向转移概率的相关性是评估模型性能的关键指标。此外还收集了三位其他研究者对晶体环境的定性反馈。

Conclusion: GFlowState 作为一个模型专用的可视分析原型，弥补了 GFlowNet 可解释性工具的空白：它将采样轨迹的 DAG 结构、样本空间各局部的相关性与验证集覆盖度指标、以及训练全程的转移概率演化以交互方式呈现出来，支持开发者定位欠探索区域、诊断训练失败来源、检测模式崩塌并评估 GFlowNet 质量。两个跨领域案例研究与专家反馈表明，交互式轨迹探索与局部化的"奖励-前向转移概率"相关性分析是标准实验跟踪工具无法提供的关键能力，可加速 GFlowNet 的实际开发。局限与未来工作包括：在 DAG 转移上额外显示动作以增强可解释性、引入用户引导的聚类、支持连续环境（以连续形式记录数据以便训练后动态调整离散化精度）、提供未截断图并按需截断以支持训练中流式分析、以及通过验证集的反向轨迹重要性采样估计转移概率以改进相关性度量在稀疏迭代中的可靠性。作者指出，随着 GFlowNet 在科学发现中获得更多关注，模型专用的可视分析工具将使 GFlowNet 的可解释性与实际应用得到增强。

Abstract: We present GFlowState, a visual analytics system designed to illuminate the training process of Generative Flow Networks (GFlowNets or GFNs). GFlowNets are a probabilistic framework for generating samples proportionally to a reward function. While GFlowNets have proved to be powerful tools in applications such as molecule and material discovery, their training dynamics remain difficult to interpret. Standard machine learning tools allow metric tracking but do not reveal how models explore the sample space, construct sample trajectories, or shift sampling probabilities during training. Our solution, GFlowState, allows users to analyze sampling trajectories, compare the sample space relative to reference datasets, and analyze the training dynamics. To this end, we introduce multiple views, including a chart of candidate rankings, a state projection, a node-link diagram of the trajectory network, and a transition heatmap. These visualizations enable GFlowNet developers and users to investigate sampling behavior and policy evolution, and to identify underexplored regions and sources of training failure. Case studies demonstrate how the system supports debugging and assessing the quality of GFlowNets across application domains. By making the structural dynamics of GFlowNets observable, our work enhances their interpretability and can accelerate GFlowNet development in practice.

</details>


### [199] [A Scale-Adaptive Framework for Joint Spatiotemporal Super-Resolution with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21903)
*Max Defez,Filippo Quarenghi,Mathieu Vrac,Stephan Mandt,Tom Beucler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种尺度自适应的时空超分辨率框架，通过将超分辨率分解为确定性条件均值预测与残差扩散模型，并调整三个关键超参数（扩散噪声β、时间上下文L、可选质量守恒f），实现同一架构在不同空间（1-25倍）和时间（1-6倍）超分辨率因子间的复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时空联合超分辨率模型通常针对特定的空间和时间超分辨率因子设计，缺乏跨不同空间分辨率和时间采样率的可迁移性，限制了实际气候应用的灵活性。

Method: 提出一种尺度自适应框架，将时空超分辨率分解为两部分：1）基于注意力机制的确定性条件均值预测；2）残差条件扩散模型。此外引入可选的质量守恒（输入输出降水总量相同）变换以保留聚合总量。尺度适应性通过调整三个因子依赖的超参数实现：扩散噪声幅度β（因子越大β越大以增加多样性）、时间上下文长度L（设置以维持各帧率下可比的注意力范围）、以及可选的质量守恒函数f（在大因子下收缩以限制极端值放大）。

Result: 在法国降水再分析数据（Comephore）上验证，同一架构可覆盖空间1-25倍、时间1-6倍的超分辨率因子范围。

Conclusion: 通过在法国降水再分析数据（Comephore）上的验证，所提框架能够跨空间1-25倍、时间1-6倍的超分辨率因子工作，证明了同一架构和调参配方在不同尺度间的可复用性。

Abstract: Deep-learning video super-resolution has progressed rapidly, but climate applications typically super-resolve (increase resolution) either space or time, and joint spatiotemporal models are often designed for a single pair of super-resolution (SR) factors (upscaling spatial and temporal ratio between the low-resolution sequence and the high-resolution sequence), limiting transfer across spatial resolutions and temporal cadences (frame rates). We present a scale-adaptive framework that reuses the same architecture across factors by decomposing spatiotemporal SR into a deterministic prediction of the conditional mean, with attention, and a residual conditional diffusion model, with an optional mass-conservation (same precipitation amount in inputs and outputs) transform to preserve aggregated totals. Assuming that larger SR factors primarily increase underdetermination (hence required context and residual uncertainty) rather than changing the conditional-mean structure, scale adaptivity is achieved by retuning three factor-dependent hyperparameters before retraining: the diffusion noise schedule amplitude beta (larger for larger factors to increase diversity), the temporal context length L (set to maintain comparable attention horizons across cadences) and optionally a third, the mass-conservation function f (tapered to limit the amplification of extremes for large factors). Demonstrated on reanalysis precipitation over France (Comephore), the same architecture spans super-resolution factors from 1 to 25 in space and 1 to 6 in time, yielding a reusable architecture and tuning recipe for joint spatiotemporal super-resolution across scales.

</details>


### [200] [Low-Rank Adaptation Redux for Large Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21905)
*Bingcong Li,Yilang Zhang,Georgios B. Giannakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从信号处理视角重新审视LoRA（低秩适配）方法，系统梳理了其在参数高效微调领域的技术机制，将研究进展归纳为架构设计、高效优化和应用三个维度，并探讨了信号处理与深度学习交叉的开放研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LoRA在基础模型微调中取得了显著成功并产生了大量变体，但关于如何选择架构设计、优化技术和部署策略仍缺乏系统性指导。

Method: 将LoRA与经典低秩建模工具和逆问题理论相联系，通过信号处理(SP)的视角分析现代适配器设计，聚焦于各方法的技术机制而非简单枚举和实验对比。

Result: 研究进展分为三个互补轴：(1)架构设计—基于SVD分解、秩增强和跨层张量化；(2)高效优化—涉及初始化、交替求解器、规范不变优化和参数化感知方法；(3)应用—涵盖预训练、后训练到推理部署的完整生命周期。

Conclusion: 提出信号处理与深度学习交叉的开放研究方向：经典SP工具可为PEFT方法设计提供原则性框架，而大规模深度学习带来的独特挑战也为信号处理社区提供新的研究课题。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) has emerged as the de facto standard for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of foundation models, enabling the adaptation of billion-parameter networks with minimal computational and memory overhead. Despite its empirical success and rapid proliferation of variants, it remains elusive which architectural choices, optimization techniques, and deployment constraints should guide practical method selection. This overview revisits LoRA through the lens of signal processing (SP), bridging modern adapter designs with classical low-rank modeling tools and inverse problems, as well as highlighting how SP principles can inform principled advances of fine-tuning approaches. Rather than providing a comprehensive enumeration and empirical comparisons of LoRA variants, emphasis is placed on the technical mechanisms underpinning these approaches to justify their effectiveness. These advances are categorized into three complementary axes: architectural design, efficient optimization, and pertinent applications. The first axis builds on singular value decomposition (SVD)-based factorization, rank-augmentation constructions, and cross-layer tensorization, while the second axis deals with initialization, alternating solvers, gauge-invariant optimization, and parameterization-aware methods. Beyond fine-tuning, emerging applications of LoRA are accounted across the entire lifecycle of large models, ranging from pre- and post-training to serving/deployment. Finally, open research directions are outlined at the confluence of SP and deep learning to catalyze a bidirectional frontier: classical SP tools provide a principled vocabulary for designing principled PEFT methods, while the unique challenges facing modern deep learning, especially the overwhelming scale and prohibitive overhead, also offer new research lines benefiting the SP community in return.

</details>


### [201] [The Sample Complexity of Multicalibration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21923)
*Natalie Collina,Jiuyao Lu,Georgy Noarov,Aaron Roth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文解决了批量（batch）设定下多重校准（multicalibration）的极小极大样本复杂度问题。对于均值 ECE 度量，在组族规模 |G| ≤ ε^{-κ}（固定 κ>0）的全部多项式预算区间内，最优样本复杂度为 Θ̃(ε^{-3})（下界对随机化预测器同样成立，上界由在线到批量的归约给出）；加权 L_p 度量（1≤p≤2）为 Θ̃(ε^{-3/p})；并可推广到"正则"可引出性质（均值、expectile、有界密度分位数）。同时指出 κ=0 时退化为 Θ̃(ε^{-2})，存在尖锐阈值现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多重校准自 Hebert-Johnson 等 (2018) 提出以来虽有近十年的算法工作，但其最优样本复杂度（作为目标误差 ε 的函数）始终未知：既有上界从 ε^{-7}、ε^{-4} 到最新的 ε^{-3} 互不一致；下界方面除均值估计给出的平凡 Ω(ε^{-2}) 外，仅有 Gibbs & Tibshirani (2025) 针对更弱概念"校准多准确率"、且仅对确定性预测器的 Ω(ε^{-5/2})。此前不存在对随机化预测器成立的非平凡下界，因此"随机化是否能把样本复杂度降回 ε^{-2}"这一问题悬而未决。此外，多重校准在非均值性质（expectile、分位数）上的样本复杂度下界也完全空白。本文旨在给出精确的极小极大刻画，并厘清批处理与在线设定下多重校准与边缘校准的差异。

Method: 方法上采用"下界 = 编码论构造 + Fano 信息论"与"上界 = 在线到批量归约"两条主线。下界核心：定义"正则可引出性质"（Definition 11），要求存在单参数族 {D_t} 满足 (1) Γ(D_t)=t；(2) 识别函数期望 M_Γ(v,t) 与 (v−t) 同号且满足二次下界 (v−t)M_Γ(v,t) ≥ c_Γ(v−t)²；(3) KL 二次界 D_KL(D_t‖D_{t'}) ≤ C(t−t')²（用于 Fano）。硬实例分两部分：(a) 硬组族 G_m——用低相关码（Lemma 16，Hoeffding 概率方法）构造 dyadic 分解下各块近乎正交的短符号向量，把每个前缀区间分解为不相交 dyadic 区间，再以低相关码表示块指示函数，从而用 O(polylog m) 个组近似表示全部 m 个符号阈值函数（L∞ 误差 ≤ 1/4，系数有界 2L）；(b) 硬分布族 H_m——取 Gilbert–Varshamov 型打包码 Θ_m（距离 ≥ d/8，log|Θ_m| = Ω(d)），把每个码字 θ 编成单调"阶梯图" t_θ（奇数位为固定梯级、偶数位按比特 θ_j 抬升 γ_m），得到 L₁ 分离 ≥ γ_m/16 与 KL ≤ C γ_m² 的分布族。证明链：多校准 ⇒ 预测误差 ≤ O(log m)·多校准误差（Proposition 26，用阈值函数逼近把 v−t_θ(i) 的符号模式分解到组上）；低误差 ⇒ 精确解码 θ（Proposition 27，用 L₁ 分离做最近邻）；精确解码 ⇒ 样本量 ≥ Ω(m/γ_m²)=Ω(m³)（Fano 不等式，Lemma 28）。L_p (1≤p≤2) 下界由 Hölder 不等式从 ECE 下界直接导出。上界方面：均值 ECE 用 Noarov 等 (2025) 的在线 O(T^{2/3}) 多校准算法经 Azuma–Hoeffding 型在线到批量归约（对偶性线性化内层、K=Θ(T^{1/3})）得 T=Õ(ε^{-3}log|G|)；一般 L_p 用 Hu 等 (2025) 的在线 swap 多校准算法，配以方差自适应 Freedman 集中不等式（Lemma 36，含 dyadic peeling）处理 |B|^p/π^{p−1} 的非线性与"轻桶"问题（阈值 τ=Θ(L/T)），得 T=Õ(ε^{-3/p})；p≥2 时为 ε^{-(p+1)/p}。

Result: (1) 主结果：对每个固定 κ>0，组族规模 |G| ≤ ε^{-κ} 时，均值 ECE 多重校准的极小极大样本复杂度为 Θ̃(ε^{-3})，硬实例极简（[m] 上的一维 Bernoulli 回归、单调均值函数、m=Θ((1/ε)log(1/ε))），组族仅需 O(log⁴(1/ε)) 个组；(2) 下界对随机化预测器成立（对任意随机化预测器经量化后仅损失常数因子），上界由随机化预测器经在线到批量归约实现；(3) 加权 L_p 度量（1≤p≤2）最优指数为 3/p，即 Θ̃(ε^{-3/p})；(4) 抽象下界模板可推广到"正则"可引出性质，与 Hu 等 (2025) 的在线上界结合，得到 expectile 与有界密度分位数的匹配界 Θ̃(ε^{-3/p})；(5) 阈值现象：κ=0（固定规模组族）时样本复杂度仍为 Θ̃(ε^{-2})，因为固定 |G| 的组成员模式把定义域划分为至多 2^{|G|} 个单元格，逐格取经验均值即可；(6) 既有工作速率经统一折算（Appendix B）：Hebert-Johnson 等 ε^{-7}、Gupta 等 ε^{-4}、Haghtalab 等 ε^{-4}、Gopalan 等 ε^{-8}、Globus-Harris 等 ε^{-10}、Noarov 等 ε^{-3}。

Conclusion: 结论与展望：批处理下均值 ECE 多重校准的极小极大样本复杂度与在线对抗设定（Collina 等 2026b 的 Θ̃(T^{-1/3})）指数一致，而边缘校准则批处理为 Θ̃(ε^{-2})、在线不可达（Qiao–Valiant 2021；Dagan 等 2025），这清晰分离了两类校准的统计行为。开放问题包括：(i) 随机化是否必要——确定性预测器能否达到同一极小极大速率（作者提出"预先固定随机性"的去随机化思路，但原子点处会失效）；(ii) p>2 的 L_p 多重校准的紧下界（Hölder 转换在此区间有损）；(iii) 样本复杂度对 ε 与组族规模 M 的联合依赖，特别是现有 Õ((log|G|)/ε³) 上界的对数依赖能否改进为次对数 f(|G|)=o(log|G|)，以及固定小 |G| 下 Õ(2^{|G|}/ε²) 平凡界与 ε^{-3/p} 行为之间的插值；(iv) 结构参数（如 VC 维）版本。

Abstract: We study the minimax sample complexity of multicalibration in the batch setting. A learner observes $n$ i.i.d. samples from an unknown distribution and must output a (possibly randomized) predictor whose population multicalibration error, measured by Expected Calibration Error (ECE), is at most $\varepsilon$ with respect to a given family of groups. For every fixed $κ> 0$, in the regime $|G|\le \varepsilon^{-κ}$, we prove that $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-3})$ samples are necessary and sufficient, up to polylogarithmic factors. The lower bound holds even for randomized predictors, and the upper bound is realized by a randomized predictor obtained via an online-to-batch reduction. This separates the sample complexity of multicalibration from that of marginal calibration, which scales as $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$, and shows that mean-ECE multicalibration is as difficult in the batch setting as it is in the online setting, in contrast to marginal calibration which is strictly more difficult in the online setting. In contrast we observe that for $κ= 0$, the sample complexity of multicalibration remains $\widetildeΘ(\varepsilon^{-2})$ exhibiting a sharp threshold phenomenon.
  More generally, we establish matching upper and lower bounds, up to polylogarithmic factors, for a weighted $L_p$ multicalibration metric for all $1 \le p \le 2$, with optimal exponent $3/p$. We also extend the lower-bound template to a regular class of elicitable properties, and combine it with the online upper bounds of Hu et al. (2025) to obtain matching bounds for calibrating properties including expectiles and bounded-density quantiles.

</details>


### [202] [Fine-Tuning Regimes Define Distinct Continual Learning Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21927)
*Paul-Tiberiu Iordache,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出：持续学习（CL）基准评测中通常固定的"微调机制"（即可训练参数子空间）本身应被视为关键评估变量。作者将不同微调机制形式化为固定可训练子空间上的投影优化，证明改变可训练深度会改变当前任务拟合信号与知识保持信号的有效构成，进而系统地证明：仅改变可训练深度（ResNet-18 最后一个残差块 → 全参微调）即可在 5 个数据集（CIFAR-100、MNIST、Fashion MNIST、KMNIST、QMNIST）× 11 个任务顺序上颠倒 5 种标准 CL 方法（online EWC、LwF、SI、GEM、DER）的相对排名（数据集级平均 Kendall's τ 仅 0.49–0.79）；同时更深（更宽松）的机制伴随更大更新幅度、更高遗忘及二者更强的耦合。据此呼吁"机制感知"的 CL 评测协议，把可训练深度作为与算法、任务顺序并列的显式实验因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习方法（正则化类 online EWC/SI、蒸馏类 LwF、梯度投影类 GEM、动态架构类 DER）各自以不同方式与"可训练参数子空间"交互，但现有文献几乎普遍在单一固定微调设置（全参数更新）下比较方法，且任务顺序敏感性通常被当作噪声而非结构化变量处理。这导致两个缺口：(1) 缺乏对"适应机制（adaptation regime）"作为独立评估轴的系统研究；(2) 缺乏对任务顺序与可训练深度交互效应的联合分析。作者的核心论点是：方法比较的结论可能仅在特定微调机制下成立，因此排名并非方法的内在属性而可能是配置依赖的。

Method: 1) 理论框架：将适应机制定义为固定可训练坐标子集 S 与正交投影算子 P_S，一阶更新为 θ ← θ − η P_S ∇J_t(θ)，其中 J_t = L_t + λΩ_t 覆盖"当前任务拟合"与"过去知识保持"两种力量；将投影梯度分解为 g_t^(S)=P_S∇L_t 与 r_t^(S)=P_S∇Ω_t，定义相互作用量 Γ_t = ⟨g_t, r_t⟩（为正表示两信号兼容，为负表示竞争）；基于 L-光滑性给出投影更新下的进度界 J(θ⁺) ≤ J(θ) − η(1−Lη/2)‖P_S∇J‖²，说明决定局部进展的是投影更新而非全梯度。2) 实验设置：任务增量（task-incremental）场景，共享骨干 + 全局 T×C 分类器，已知任务边界；骨干为随机初始化的 ResNet-18；5 种微调机制（仅最后 1 个 block、最后 2/3/6 个 block、全部 8 个 block 全参微调）；5 种方法（online EWC、LwF、SI、GEM、DER）；5 个数据集（CIFAR-100 划 T=10、C=10；4 个 MNIST 系数据集 T=5、C=2）；每个数据集 11 个任务顺序（1 个规范顺序 + 10 个随机采样），所有方法/机制共用同一组顺序；超参数全数据集/全机制固定（Adam、lr 1e-3、余弦退火至 1e-5、batch 256 等），并公开代码与配置文件。3) 评估：平均准确率、平均遗忘度；核心诊断量为不同机制间方法排名的成对 Kendall's τ（按数据集在 11 个顺序上平均）；并在 LwF（及扩展至 SI、online EWC、GEM、DER）上测量投影梯度幅度 ‖P_S∇J‖ 与遗忘的耦合及机制间 τ 变化。

Result: (1) 排名不稳定：Fashion MNIST 上各机制准确率曲线差异大且标准差高，机制间成对 τ 低，个别任务顺序出现低一致性甚至高一致性的极端；(2) 跨数据集一致：平均 τ 从 Fashion MNIST 的 0.49 到 KMNIST 的 0.79，最严格与最宽松机制之间分歧最大；(3) 机制层面：更深机制 → 投影梯度幅度更大、平均遗忘更高，且梯度幅度与遗忘的 Kendall's τ 随机制放宽而增强；(4) 方法层面差异：SI 呈严格单调关系，online EWC 方向相似但中间机制存在局部反转，GEM 因梯度投影机制抑制更新幅度而范数跨度极窄（存在一次局部倒挂），DER 总体递增但 Last 3 Blocks 遗忘高于 Last 6 Blocks（logit 重放约束与梯度更新冲突），全参微调均位于右上角极端；(5) 数值表给出各数据集×方法×机制的平均准确率与遗忘（如 CIFAR-100 全参微调下 DER 0.80±0.003、LwF 0.80、online EWC 0.65、SI 0.63、GEM 0.71；MNIST 全参微调下 DER/LwF 达 1.00、online EWC 0.89）；(6) 局限：超参未按机制单独调优、仅沿可训练深度一个轴变化、仅限任务增量、仅 ResNet-18、投影量的实测覆盖不完整。

Conclusion: CL 的比较性结论对所选微调机制高度敏感：即使固定架构、超参与任务顺序，仅改变可训练参数子空间（可训练深度）即可实质颠倒 well-established 方法的相对排名。机制之所以起作用，是因为它同时改变当前任务拟合信号与保持信号的可行方向及二者的相互作用（Γ），从而改变"有效优化信号"；更宽松的机制暴露更多可塑性与干扰方向，表现为更大的更新幅度、更高遗忘以及二者更强的耦合。因此 CL 基准应把微调机制（可训练深度）作为与算法选择、任务顺序并列的一等实验因子，报告单机制下的排名结论不具跨配置泛化性。后续工作应扩展到其他架构（CNN/Transformer）、按机制独立调参、扩展到类增量/域增量/任务无关场景，并考察更丰富的子空间形式（低秩更新、adapter、稀疏掩码、结构化剪枝）。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) studies how models acquire tasks sequentially while retaining previously learned knowledge. Despite substantial progress in benchmarking CL methods, comparative evaluations typically keep the fine-tuning regime fixed. In this paper, we argue that the fine-tuning regime, defined by the trainable parameter subspace, is itself a key evaluation variable. We formalize adaptation regimes as projected optimization over fixed trainable subspaces, showing that changing the trainable depth alters the effective update signal through which both current task fitting and knowledge preservation operate. This analysis motivates the hypothesis that method comparisons need not be invariant across regimes. We test this hypothesis in task incremental CL, five trainable depth regimes, and four standard methods: online EWC, LwF, SI, and GEM. Across five benchmark datasets, namely MNIST, Fashion MNIST, KMNIST, QMNIST, and CIFAR-100, and across 11 task orders per dataset, we find that the relative ranking of methods is not consistently preserved across regimes. We further show that deeper adaptation regimes are associated with larger update magnitudes, higher forgetting, and a stronger relationship between the two. These results show that comparative conclusions in CL can depend strongly on the chosen fine-tuning regime, motivating regime-aware evaluation protocols that treat trainable depth as an explicit experimental factor.

</details>


### [203] [Temporal Taskification in Streaming Continual Learning: A Source of Evaluation Instability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21930)
*Nicolae Filat,Ahmed Hussain,Konstantinos Kalogiannis,Elena Burceanu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Temporal taskification (how continuous data streams are partitioned into discrete tasks) significantly impacts streaming CL benchmark conclusions, independent of the learner or data stream itself. Shorter taskifications lead to noisier patterns and higher sensitivity to boundary changes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Streaming CL typically converts continuous streams into discrete tasks via temporal partitioning, but this taskification step is not a neutral preprocessing choice—it fundamentally shapes the CL regime and benchmark conclusions. Different valid splits of the same stream can induce different evaluation outcomes.

Method: Introduced a taskification-level framework based on plasticity and stability profiles, including a profile distance measure between taskifications and Boundary-Profile Sensitivity (BPS) to diagnose sensitivity to boundary perturbations. Evaluated four CL methods (continual finetuning, Experience Replay, EWC, LwF) on network traffic forecasting (CESNET-Timeseries24) while varying only temporal taskification across 9-, 30-, and 44-day splits.

Result: Across different taskification granularities, substantial changes were observed in forecasting error, forgetting, and backward transfer. Shorter taskifications induce noisier distribution-level patterns, larger structural distances, and higher BPS, indicating greater sensitivity to boundary perturbations.

Conclusion: Temporal taskification should be considered a first-class evaluation variable in streaming CL benchmarks, alongside the learner and the data stream, to ensure robust and reliable evaluation conclusions.

Abstract: Streaming Continual Learning (CL) typically converts a continuous stream into a sequence of discrete tasks through temporal partitioning. We argue that this temporal taskification step is not a neutral preprocessing choice, but a structural component of evaluation: different valid splits of the same stream can induce different CL regimes and therefore different benchmark conclusions. To study this effect, we introduce a taskification-level framework based on plasticity and stability profiles, a profile distance between taskifications, and Boundary-Profile Sensitivity (BPS), which diagnoses how strongly small boundary perturbations alter the induced regime before any CL model is trained. We evaluate continual finetuning, Experience Replay, Elastic Weight Consolidation, and Learning without Forgetting on network traffic forecasting with CESNET-Timeseries24, keeping the stream, model, and training budget fixed while varying only the temporal taskification. Across 9-, 30-, and 44-day splits, we observe substantial changes in forecasting error, forgetting, and backward transfer, showing that taskification alone can materially affect CL evaluation. We further find that shorter taskifications induce noisier distribution-level patterns, larger structural distances, and higher BPS, indicating greater sensitivity to boundary perturbations. These results show that benchmark conclusions in streaming CL depend not only on the learner and the data stream, but also on how that stream is taskified, motivating temporal taskification as a first-class evaluation variable.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [204] [AtomicRAG: Atom-Entity Graphs for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20844)
*Yanning Hou,Duanyang Yuan,Sihang Zhou,Xiaoshu Chen,Ke Liang,Siwei Wang,Xinwang Liu,Jian Huang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出AtomicRAG框架，通过将知识表示为原子化的事实单元（knowledge atoms）而非粗粒度的文本块，并结合个性化PageRank与相关性过滤，实现更精确可靠的知识索引与检索，显著提升检索准确性和推理鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GraphRAG方法存在两个核心问题：1）将文本块作为知识表示基本单元，导致多个原子事实被刚性分组，限制了对多样化查询场景的适应能力；2）基于三元组的实体链接对关系抽取错误敏感，容易产生缺失或错误的推理路径，降低检索准确性。

Method: 提出原子-实体图（Atom-Entity Graph）架构：1）将知识存储为"知识原子"，即独立、自包含的事实单元，支持灵活重组；2）实体间的边仅表示关系是否存在，不依赖具体关系类型；3）结合个性化PageRank算法与相关性过滤，保持实体连接的准确性。

Result: 在5个公开基准数据集上的实验表明，AtomicRAG算法在检索准确性和推理鲁棒性方面均优于强基线RAG方法。

Conclusion: 知识原子化表示与简化的图结构设计能有效解决传统GraphRAG的局限性，为多样化查询场景提供更灵活、更可靠的知识检索支持。

Abstract: Recent GraphRAG methods integrate graph structures into text indexing and retrieval, using knowledge graph triples to connect text chunks, thereby improving retrieval coverage and precision. However, we observe that treating text chunks as the basic unit of knowledge representation rigidly groups multiple atomic facts together, limiting the flexibility and adaptability needed to support diverse retrieval scenarios. Additionally, triple-based entity linking is sensitive to relation-extraction errors, which can lead to missing or incorrect reasoning paths and ultimately hurt retrieval accuracy. To address these issues, we propose the Atom-Entity Graph, a more precise and reliable architecture for knowledge representation and indexing. In our approach, knowledge is stored as knowledge atoms, namely individual, self-contained units of factual information, rather than coarse-grained text chunks. This allows knowledge elements to be flexibly reassembled without mutual interference, thereby enabling seamless alignment with diverse query perspectives. Edges between entities simply indicate whether a relationship exists. By combining personalized PageRank with relevance-based filtering, we maintain accurate entity connections and improve the reliability of reasoning. Theoretical analysis and experiments on five public benchmarks show that the proposed AtomicRAG algorithm outperforms strong RAG baselines in retrieval accuracy and reasoning robustness. Code: https://github.com/7HHHHH/AtomicRAG.

</details>


### [205] [Caesar: Deep Agentic Web Exploration for Creative Answer Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20855)
*Jason Liang,Elliot Meyerson,Risto Miikkulainen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Caesar（Cognizant AI Lab + UT Austin）是一个两阶段智能体架构：Phase 1 在网页上做"深度图式遍历"，用 NetworkX 知识图 G=(V,E) + 导航栈 + 向量知识库 + 情节记忆双记忆，边探索边抽取"洞见"；Phase 2 用"对抗式精炼"递归追问、生成多版草稿、动态演化挑战问题，最后生成式合并为最终 artifact 并可选 ELI5 化。在 5 类创造力挑战 × 3 种输出约束的盲评（LLM-as-a-Judge）中，Caesar 在全部设置下以大幅 Cliff's Delta（≥0.76，论文称 13–23% 提升）击败 Gemini 3 / Sonnet 4.5 / GPT-5.2 的 Deep Research 与浅层检索；等算力对照（T=250 + GPT-5-mini）仍领先，23 人 112 场 A/B 人工评测中 56.25% 偏好 Caesar。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 Web 智能体与深度研究架构（ReAct、GPT-Researcher、WebThinker、WebResearcher、线性/单步 RAG）把互联网视为扁平的、断连的文档序列，优化的是"对已知问题给出最高概率答案"的收敛式检索，导致"导航失忆"、内容冗余循环与派生式总结（derivative summarization）。作者认为技术进步来自知识的 novel synthesis 而非事实累积（类比人类研究者构建"心智地图"寻找非显然连接），因此智能体必须从"被动图书馆员"升级为能做联想式探究的"主动探索者"。论文把创造性操作化为 Simonton(2012) 的三元组 New（新颖/罕见）、Useful（可行/对齐）、Surprising（局部颠覆/横向跃迁），并据此构造 5 个针对 LLM 已知失败模式（参数偏置、知识冲突、推理深度、RLHF 对齐税、归纳-溯因/模式坍缩）的挑战。

Method: Phase 1 深度网页探索（Algorithm 1，Perceive-Think-Act 循环）：(a) 引导——把挑战展开为辅助检索词、搜索结果拼成合成 HTML 作为根节点 v0，并由 GenerateRole 动态重写系统提示生成领域专属角色 ρr（缓解 RLHF 安全偏置的通用回答）；(b) Perceive——curl_cffi 模拟 Chrome 指纹取 HTML/PDF，Trafilatura/PyPDF2 抽取正文与出链，按域边界、失败链接（Lf）过滤；(c) Think——检索拓扑邻域 Nc（k=3 跳、|Nc|≤30）作为短时记忆，在 (Pc, Nc, Q, ρr) 条件下生成洞见 Ic，要求指出新内容如何"支撑或挑战"邻域洞见而非摘要；Ic 双重存储（挂在图节点 + 写入 ChromaDB 向量库 KB）；(d) Act——策略 πc 融合 KB 语义检索与情节记忆 M（Mem0+Neo4j，记录导航动作/回溯/推理轨迹）构成复合上下文 Kc，先选高层元策略 a_m∈{Explore, Backtrack, WebSearch}，再由 LLM 作为判别器对候选链接排序，并受 |S|>1、搜索预算 S_m、|Lc|>0 等动态约束，保证栈式 DFS/回溯/横向搜索。Phase 2 对抗式 artifact 合成（Algorithm 2，N=3 轮）：递归洞察发现 GenerateInsightQA（30 次迭代，问题 q_t 由上一轮答案 a_t 的缺口自动生成，形成逻辑链而非事实袋）→ 递归草稿生成 GenerateDraft（条件 C_k={I_k, A_{k-1}}，同时产出引用映射 B_k）→ 对抗挑战精炼 RefineChallenge（分析 A_k 的叙事弱点/矛盾，生成正交的 Q_k）→ 生成式合并 MergeDrafts（整合全部草稿与引用）→ 与主循环解耦的 ELI5 后处理。工程栈：Python、NetworkX、ChromaDB + text-embedding-3-large（400/80 分块，top-k 50/rerank 10）、curl_cffi、Trafilatura/PyPDF2、GPT-5.2（探索期低推理 effort、合成期高 effort）、Mem0/Neo4j；超参 T=1000 探索步、N=3 精炼轮、τ_Think/Act=0.9、τ_Draft/Merge=0.1。

Result: 主结果：Caesar 在 3 位评委（Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-5.2、Gemini 3 Pro，各 3 次重复、共 9 样本）、5 挑战、3 输出约束上全面领先。完整答案 26.96（New 9.11 / Useful 8.87 / Surp. 8.98）vs Gemini 3 Deep 23.78（+3.18）；无约束 ELI5 25.69 vs Sonnet 4.5 Deep 21.87；450 词 ELI5 25.13 vs 20.40；所有对比 Cliff's Delta ≥0.76，多数为 1.00（严格支配）。分类别看，优势集中在关联/结构类任务（Cross-Domain +5.89、Constrained +3.33、Counterfactual +2.88），在偏参数内省的推测类任务收窄（Meta +2.44、Open-Ended +1.34）。稳健性：剔除 GPT 评委（因 Caesar 底座为 GPT-5.2）后 Caesar 仍居首（27.27/25.94/25.10）；评委自偏好偏差为 Gemini +1.35、Claude +0.98（仅完整答案）；长度-得分相关 r=0.47，但深层档内为 -0.13，属 Simpson 悖论，说明优势来自内容而非篇幅；等算力对照（T=250、GPT-5-mini，匹配 Gemini Deep ~$5/题）Caesar 26.16 仍高于 Gemini 3 Deep 24.37（δ=0.76）、Sonnet 21.00、GPT-5.2 16.16；23 名人类 112 场配对盲评偏好 Caesar 63:49（56.25%，OR 1.29），且偏好格局与 LLM 评委一致（C3 20:2、C2 18:5 偏好 Caesar，C1 5:18、C5 7:15 偏好基线）。消融：去掉知识图（改 flat top-T 搜索）23.76 vs 25.11（δ=0.52）；邻域降为单跳（k=1、5 节点）23.69 vs 25.45（δ=0.52），二者效应在 Draft 1 与最终合并上最强（δ=0.56/0.68 与 0.52）；探索步数 1000→500→250 使总分 24.92→22.74→21.84（δ=0.92）；对抗精炼 A3 提升 Surprising(+1.76)/New 但拉低 Useful(7.04)，生成式合并把 Useful 拉回 8.28（发散-收敛组合有效）；Hub 节点洞见分 24.43 显著高于 Leaf 21.88（δ=1.00）。

Conclusion: Caesar 通过"图式导航控制 + 对抗式精炼"把自主网页搜索从静态检索推进到主动发现：消融显示知识图的主要价值在于宏观导航（引导到结构相邻但语义正交的信息）与更宽的拓扑上下文，深度遍历才能获得罕见洞见，而对抗精炼能跳出通用摘要的吸引盆。局限包括：token/时延成本高（Phase 1 约 $40–60/千步，Phase 2 <$3/轮）、引导阶段可能陷入 SEO 内容簇形成递归低价值循环、对简单事实检索类挑战属过度设计、在依赖参数化想象的 Open-Ended Synthesis 上被 Sonnet 4.5 Deep 反超或持平；评测仅 5 个挑战、LLM-as-a-Judge 存在系统性盲区、闭源深度研究基线不可精确复现。未来方向为多智能体协作群、ARC-AGI 上的程序合成验证、按挑战复杂度自适应校准探索深度。总体上，该工作以"探索与合成解耦 + 路径本身作为洞见来源"为核心主张，给出了可复现的实现栈、消融证据与较完整的偏差/成本分析。

Abstract: To advance from passive retrieval to creative discovery of new ideas, autonomous agents must be capable of deep, associative synthesis. However, current agentic frameworks prioritize convergent search, often resulting in derivative summaries that lack creativity. Caesar is an agentic LLM architecture designed to bridge the gap between information gathering and synthesis of new insights. Unlike existing agents that treat the web as a flat sequence of disconnected documents, Caesar leverages an extensive knowledge graph to foster associative reasoning, thus enabling the discovery of non-obvious connections between disparate concepts. It consists of two components: (1) exploration driven by a dynamic context-aware policy, and (2) synthesis controlled by an adversarial draft refinement loop that actively seeks novel perspectives rather than confirming established priors. Caesar demonstrates the ability to generate artifacts and answers characterized by high novelty and structural coherence, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art LLM research agents in tasks requiring creativity.

</details>


### [206] [CaST-POI: Candidate-Conditioned Spatiotemporal Modeling for Next POI Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20845)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CaST-POI模型，解决传统POI推荐方法中用户历史表示与候选POI无关的问题。核心思想是用户历史应基于待评估的候选POI进行差异化解读，通过候选条件注意力机制动态整合用户轨迹，并引入候选相对时空偏置捕获细粒度移动模式。在三个基准数据集上验证了方法的有效性，尤其在大候选池场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有POI推荐方法通常从用户历史轨迹中计算单一用户表示，并将其均匀应用于所有候选POI的打分。这种候选无关（Candidate-Agnostic）范式存在明显缺陷：历史访问的相关性本质上取决于当前评估的候选POI，忽视了两者之间的关系导致推荐精度受限。

Method: CaST-POI包含两个核心组件：1) 候选条件序列阅读器（Candidate-Conditioned Sequence Reader）：将候选POI作为查询（Query）动态注意力机制从用户历史中提取相关信息；2) 候选相对时空偏置（Candidate-Relative Temporal and Spatial Biases）：建模历史访问与每个候选POI之间的细粒度时空关系，捕获用户移动模式。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，CaST-POI一致性地超越现有最优方法，在多个评估指标上取得实质性提升。特别地，在大候选池场景下优势更为显著。

Conclusion: 候选条件化的建模思路能有效区分同一用户历史对不同候选POI的相关程度，是提升下一POI推荐性能的有效途径。该工作为序列推荐中的用户兴趣建模提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Next Point-of-Interest (POI) recommendation plays a crucial role in location-based services by predicting users' future mobility patterns. Existing methods typically compute a single user representation from historical trajectories and use it to score all candidate POIs uniformly. However, this candidate-agnostic paradigm overlooks that the relevance of historical visits inherently depends on which candidate is being evaluated. In this paper, we propose CaST-POI, a candidate-conditioned spatiotemporal model for next POI recommendation. Our key insight is that the same user history should be interpreted differently when evaluating different candidate POIs. CaST-POI employs a candidate-conditioned sequence reader that uses candidates as queries to dynamically attend to user history. In addition, we introduce candidate-relative temporal and spatial biases to capture fine-grained mobility patterns based on the relationships between historical visits and each candidate POI. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets demonstrate that CaST-POI consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, yielding substantial improvements across multiple evaluation metrics, with particularly strong advantages under large candidate pools. Code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/CaST-POI.git.

</details>


### [207] [ADS-POI: Agentic Spatiotemporal State Decomposition for Next Point-of-Interest Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20846)
*Zhenyu Yu,Chunlei Meng,Yangchen Zeng,Mohd Yamani Idna Idris,Shuigeng Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: ADS-POI提出一种用于下一个POI推荐的空间-时间状态分解框架，通过多个并行演化的潜在子状态分别建模用户行为中的异质信号（如日常移动模式、短期意图、时间规律性），并通过上下文条件聚合机制形成决策状态进行预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将用户历史压缩为单一潜在表示，容易纠缠异质信号（如日常移动模式、短期意图、时间规律性），限制了状态演化的灵活性，降低了模型适应不同决策上下文的能力。

Method: ADS-POI框架包含两个核心设计：(1) 多并行演化子状态：每个用户用多个具有独立空间-时间转换动态的潜在子状态表示；(2) 上下文条件聚合机制：子状态选择性聚合形成用于预测的决策状态，使不同行为成分能以不同速率演化，同时保持空间-时间上下文下的协调。

Result: 在Foursquare和Gowalla的三个真实数据集上，ADS-POI在全排名评估协议下始终优于强基线方法。将用户行为分解为多个空间-时间感知状态能够带来更有效且更鲁棒的下一个POI推荐效果。

Conclusion: 通过将用户行为解耦为多个空间-时间感知的子状态，ADS-POI有效解决了现有方法中异质信号纠缠的问题，显著提升了下一个POI推荐的准确性和鲁棒性。

Abstract: Next point-of-interest (POI) recommendation requires modeling user mobility as a spatiotemporal sequence, where different behavioral factors may evolve at different temporal and spatial scales. Most existing methods compress a user's history into a single latent representation, which tends to entangle heterogeneous signals such as routine mobility patterns, short-term intent, and temporal regularities. This entanglement limits the flexibility of state evolution and reduces the model's ability to adapt to diverse decision contexts. We propose ADS-POI, a spatiotemporal state decomposition framework for next POI recommendation. ADS-POI represents a user with multiple parallel evolving latent sub-states, each governed by its own spatiotemporal transition dynamics. These sub-states are selectively aggregated through a context-conditioned mechanism to form the decision state used for prediction. This design enables different behavioral components to evolve at different rates while remaining coordinated under the current spatiotemporal context. Extensive experiments on three real-world benchmark datasets from Foursquare and Gowalla demonstrate that ADS-POI consistently outperforms strong state-of-the-art baselines under a full-ranking evaluation protocol. The results show that decomposing user behavior into multiple spatiotemporally aware states leads to more effective and robust next POI recommendation. Our code is available at https://github.com/YuZhenyuLindy/ADS-POI.git.

</details>


### [208] [Revisiting Content-Based Music Recommendation: Efficient Feature Aggregation from Large-Scale Music Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20847)
*Yizhi Zhou,Jia-Qi Yang,De-Chuan Zhan,Da-Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TASTE是一个多模态音乐推荐数据集和基准框架，整合音频和文本模态，利用自监督音乐编码器提取音频特征，并提出MuQ-token方法高效融合多层音频特征，验证了内容驱动方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有音乐推荐系统主要依赖协同过滤，未能充分利用音频特征，在冷启动场景下表现欠佳；现有数据集缺乏丰富的多模态信息（原始音频和文本元数据）；当前评估框架不足以支持多模态方法的全面评测。

Method: 提出TASTE数据集与基准框架，整合音频和文本两种模态；利用大规模自监督音乐编码器提取音频表示；开发MuQ-token方法实现多层音频特征的高效融合。

Result: 提取的音频表示在候选召回和CTR预测任务中展现出显著价值；MuQ-token方法在多种设置下均优于其他特征融合技术。

Conclusion: 验证了内容驱动方法的有效性，构建了一个可复用的多模态研究基础，为未来音乐推荐研究提供支持。

Abstract: Music Recommendation Systems (MRSs) are a cornerstone of modern streaming platforms. Existing recommendation models, spanning both recall and ranking stages, predominantly rely on collaborative filtering, which fails to exploit the intrinsic characteristics of audio and consequently leads to suboptimal performance, particularly in cold-start scenarios. However, existing music recommendation datasets often lack rich multimodal information, such as raw audio signals and descriptive textual metadata. Moreover, current recommender system evaluation frameworks remain inadequate, as they neither fully leverage multimodal information nor support a diverse range of algorithms, especially multimodal methods. To address these limitations, we propose TASTE, a comprehensive dataset and benchmarking framework designed to highlight the role of multimodal information in music recommendation. Our dataset integrates both audio and textual modalities. By leveraging recent large-scale self-supervised music encoders, we demonstrate the substantial value of the extracted audio representations across recommendation tasks, including candidate recall and CTR. In addition, we introduce the \textbf{MuQ-token} method, which enables more efficient integration of multi-layer audio features. This method consistently outperforms other feature integration techniques across various settings. Overall, our results not only validate the effectiveness of content-driven approaches but also provide a highly effective and reusable multimodal foundation for future research. Code is available at https://github.com/zreach/TASTE

</details>


### [209] [MATRAG: Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation for Explainable Recommendations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20848)
*Sushant Mehta*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper introduces MATRAG, a multi-agent framework combining knowledge graph-augmented retrieval with LLM-based recommendation to deliver explainable and transparent personalized suggestions. The system uses four specialized agents for user modeling, item analysis, reasoning, and explanation generation, achieving significant improvements in recommendation accuracy (12.7% Hit Rate, 15.3% NDCG) and explanation quality (87.4% expert trust rating) across three benchmark datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing LLM-based recommendation systems face critical challenges in transparency, knowledge grounding, and providing coherent explanations that foster user trust.

Method: MATRAG employs four specialized agents: (1) User Modeling Agent for dynamic preference profiling, (2) Item Analysis Agent for semantic feature extraction from knowledge graphs, (3) Reasoning Agent for synthesizing collaborative and content-based signals, and (4) Explanation Agent for natural language justification generation. The framework integrates a transparency scoring mechanism to quantify explanation faithfulness and relevance.

Result: MATRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance across three benchmark datasets (Amazon Reviews, MovieLens-1M, Yelp), with 12.7% improvement in Hit Rate and 15.3% improvement in NDCG over leading baselines. Human evaluation confirms that 87.4% of generated explanations are rated as helpful and trustworthy by domain experts.

Conclusion: MATRAG establishes new benchmarks for transparent, agentic recommendation systems, offering a scalable architecture for deploying LLM-based recommenders in production. The combination of multi-agent collaboration with knowledge graph retrieval effectively addresses the transparency and explainability challenges in existing LLM-based recommendation approaches.

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM)-based recommendation systems have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in understanding user preferences and generating personalized suggestions. However, existing approaches face critical challenges in transparency, knowledge grounding, and the ability to provide coherent explanations that foster user trust. We introduce MATRAG (Multi-Agent Transparent Retrieval-Augmented Generation), a novel framework that combined multi-agent collaboration with knowledge graph-augmented retrieval to deliver explainable recommendations. MATRAG employs four specialized agents: a User Modeling Agent that constructs dynamic preference profiles, an Item Analysis Agent that extracts semantic features from knowledge graphs, a Reasoning Agent that synthesizes collaborative and content-based signals, and an Explanation Agent that generates natural language justifications grounded in retrieved knowledge. Our framework incorporates a transparency scoring mechanism that quantifies explanation faithfulness and relevance. Extensive experiments on three benchmark datasets (Amazon Reviews, MovieLens-1M, and Yelp) demonstrate that MATRAG achieves state-of-the-art performance, improving recommendation accuracy by 12.7\% (Hit Rate) and 15.3\% (NDCG) over leading baselines, while human evaluation confirms that 87.4\% of generated explanations are rated as helpful and trustworthy by domain experts. Our work establishes new benchmarks for transparent, agentic recommendation systems and provides actionable insights for deploying LLM-based recommenders in production environments.

</details>


### [210] [SPIRE: Structure-Preserving Interpretable Retrieval of Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20849)
*Mike Rainey,Umut Acar,Muhammed Sezer*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种针对半结构化文档（如HTML）的结构感知检索pipeline，通过将文档表示为可寻址的子文档，并定义路径、剪枝、全局/局部上下文等原语，在保持结构信息的同时实现高效检索和上下文保留的证据抽取。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前检索增强生成(RAG)系统在处理HTML等半结构化文档时存在结构失配问题：现有模型依赖扁平序列接口，文档常被线性化为固定大小块，导致段落结构、列表、表格等重要信息丢失，难以返回既精确又保留可解释上下文的引用证据。

Method: 提出结构感知检索pipeline，核心包括：(1) 子文档表示：将文档片断保留结构身份；(2) 文档原语：路径与路径集、剪枝提取子文档、全局上下文化（添加非局部支架如标题、表结构）和局部上下文化（在结构邻域内扩展）；(3) 基于嵌入的候选生成器：索引句子级子文档；(4) 查询时文档感知聚合：复用共享结构上下文；(5) 上下文过滤阶段：使用局部上下文化视图重新排序。

Result: 在HTML问答基准测试中，结构感知方法在固定预算下产生了更高质量、更多样化的引用，性能优于强基线方法，同时保持了可扩展性。

Conclusion: 通过保留文档结构并对检索选择进行上下文化，可实现更优的检索质量；该方法为半结构化文档的RAG系统提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation over semi-structured sources such as HTML is constrained by a mismatch between document structure and the flat, sequence-based interfaces of today's embedding and generative models. Retrieval pipelines often linearize documents into fixed-size chunks before indexing, which obscures section structure, lists, and tables, and makes it difficult to return small, citation-ready evidence without losing the surrounding context that makes it interpretable.
  We present a structure-aware retrieval pipeline that operates over tree-structured documents. The core idea is to represent candidates as subdocuments: precise, addressable selections that preserve structural identity while deferring the choice of surrounding context. We define a small set of document primitives--paths and path sets, subdocument extraction by pruning, and two contextualization mechanisms. Global contextualization adds the non-local scaffolding needed to make a selection intelligible (e.g., titles, headers, list and table structure). Local contextualization expands a seed selection within its structural neighborhood to obtain a compact, context-rich view under a target budget. Building on these primitives, we describe an embedding-based candidate generator that indexes sentence-seeded subdocuments and a query-time, document-aware aggregation step that amortizes shared structural context. We then introduce a contextual filtering stage that re-scores retrieved candidates using locally contextualized views.
  Across experiments on HTML question-answering benchmarks, we find that preserving structure while contextualizing selections yields higher-quality, more diverse citations under fixed budgets than strong passage-based baselines, while maintaining scalability.

</details>


### [211] [Association Is Not Similarity: Learning Corpus-Specific Associations for Multi-Hop Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20850)
*Jason Dury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出Association-Augmented Retrieval (AAR)，一种轻量级的转导式重排序方法，通过对比学习训练小型MLP（4.2M参数）学习段落间的关联关系。在HotpotQA上将候选段落召回率提升8.6个百分点，在困难问题上提升28.5个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集检索系统通过嵌入相似性对段落进行排名，但在多跳问题场景中，需要通过共享推理链进行关联的段落。现有密集检索方法无法有效捕捉段落间的关联关系，导致在需要多步推理的问题上表现不佳。

Method: 使用对比学习在共现标注上训练小型MLP学习嵌入空间中的段落关联；推理时通过双向关联评分对初始检索候选集进行重排序。转导式设置（评估集关联在训练时可见）。

Result: HotpotQA上Recall@5从0.831提升至0.916（+8.6）；困难问题提升+28.5；MuSiQue转导式设置+10.1；归纳式设置（未见关联）无显著提升；下游QA任务EM提升+6.4；效率：每查询+3.7ms，单GPU训练<2分钟。

Conclusion: AAR主要捕捉语料库特定的共现关系而非可迁移模式。消融实验表明：仅用语义相似但无关联的段落对训练会降低性能，打乱关联对会导致严重性能下降。该方法轻量高效，无需LLM索引。

Abstract: Dense retrieval systems rank passages by embedding similarity to a query, but multi-hop questions require passages that are associatively related through shared reasoning chains. We introduce Association-Augmented Retrieval (AAR), a lightweight transductive reranking method that trains a small MLP (4.2M parameters) to learn associative relationships between passages in embedding space using contrastive learning on co-occurrence annotations. At inference time, AAR reranks an initial dense retrieval candidate set using bi-directional association scoring. On HotpotQA, AAR improves passage Recall@5 from 0.831 to 0.916 (+8.6 points) without evaluation-set tuning, with gains concentrated on hard questions where the dense baseline fails (+28.5 points). On MuSiQue, AAR achieves +10.1 points in the transductive setting. An inductive model trained on training-split associations and evaluated on unseen validation associations shows no significant improvement, suggesting that the method captures corpus-specific co-occurrences rather than transferable patterns. Ablation studies support this interpretation: training on semantically similar but non-associated passage pairs degrades retrieval below the baseline, while shuffling association pairs causes severe degradation. A downstream QA evaluation shows retrieval gains translate to +6.4 exact match improvement. The method adds 3.7ms per query, trains in under two minutes on a single GPU, and requires no LLM-based indexing.

</details>


### [212] [Robust Test-time Video-Text Retrieval: Benchmarking and Adapting for Query Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20851)
*Bingqing Zhang,Zhuo Cao,Heming Du,Yang Li,Xue Li,Jiajun Liu,Sen Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对视频-文本检索(VTR)模型在真实世界查询偏移下的鲁棒性问题，提出系统性解决方案。核心发现是查询偏移会加剧"hubness现象"（少数gallery项目不成比例地吸引查询）。创新点包括：1）构建了包含12种扰动类型、5种严重程度的综合基准；2）提出HAT-VTR测试时适应框架，通过Hubness Suppression Memory和多粒度损失实现鲁棒性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代视频-文本检索模型在基准测试上表现优异，但在真实世界的查询偏移场景下性能急剧下降。现有针对图像的鲁棒性解决方案无法有效处理视频中复杂的时空动态变化，亟需专门针对视频域迁移的测试时适应方法。

Method: 1）构建包含12种视频扰动类型、5种严重程度级别的综合基准用于系统评估；2）提出HAT-VTR测试时适应框架，包含两个核心组件：Hubness Suppression Memory（用于细化相似度分数）和Multi-granular losses（用于强制时间特征一致性）。

Result: 实验表明HAT-VTR显著提升模型鲁棒性，在多种查询偏移场景下持续优于先前方法，增强了模型在真实应用中的可靠性。

Conclusion: 通过揭示hubness现象在视频检索中的关键作用，并提出有效的测试时适应策略，为VTR模型应对真实世界分布偏移提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Modern video-text retrieval (VTR) models excel on in-distribution benchmarks but are highly vulnerable to real-world query shifts, where the distribution of query data deviates from the training domain, leading to a sharp performance drop. Existing image-focused robustness solutions are inadequate to handle this vulnerability in video, as they fail to address the complex spatio-temporal dynamics inherent in these shifts. To systematically evaluate this vulnerability, we first introduce a comprehensive benchmark featuring 12 distinct types of video perturbations across five severity degrees. Analysis on this benchmark reveals that query shifts amplify the hubness phenomenon, where a few gallery items become dominant "hubs" that attract a disproportionate number of queries. To mitigate this, we then propose HAT-VTR (Hubness Alleviation for Test-time Video-Text Retrieval), as our baseline test-time adaptation framework designed to directly counteract hubness in VTR. It leverages two key components: a Hubness Suppression Memory to refine similarity scores, and multi-granular losses to enforce temporal feature consistency. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HAT-VTR substantially improves robustness, consistently outperforming prior methods across diverse query shift scenarios, and enhancing model reliability for real-world applications.

</details>


### [213] [DenoiseRank: Learning to Rank by Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20852)
*Ying Wang,Preslav Nakov,Shangsong Liang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出DenoiseRank，首次从生成式视角解决学习排序问题，利用扩散模型的去噪过程预测查询-文档的相关性分布，在基准数据集上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统学习排序模型均从判别式角度构建，缺乏生成式方法的探索。该研究旨在开辟LTR的新视角，利用深度生成模型处理排序任务。

Method: 提出DenoiseRank模型，采用扩散模型框架。前向过程对相关标签添加噪声，逆向过程对查询-文档进行去噪以准确预测其分布。

Result: 在基准数据集上的广泛实验证明了DenoiseRank的有效性。

Conclusion: DenoiseRank是首个生成式LTR方法，也是首个应用于排序任务的扩散模型，为生成式排序任务提供了基准。

Abstract: Learning to rank (LTR) is one of the core tasks in Machine Learning. Traditional LTR models have made great progress, but nearly all of them are implemented from discriminative perspective. In this paper, we aim at addressing LTR from a novel perspective, i.e., by a deep generative model. Specifically, we propose a novel denoise rank model, DenoiseRank, which noises the relevant labels in the diffusion process and denoises them on the query documents in the reverse process to accurately predict their distribution. Our model is the first to address traditional LTR from generative perspective and is a diffusion method for LTR. Our extensive experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrated the effectiveness of DenoiseRank, and we believe it provides a benchmark for generative LTR task.

</details>


### [214] [A Systematic Study of Biomedical Retrieval Pipeline Trade-offs in Performance and Efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20853)
*Hayk Stepanyan,Matthew McDermott*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: An empirical study providing practical guidance for designing biomedical retrieval systems at scale, evaluating corpus selection, chunking, and indexing choices across multiple datasets and query types, with recommendations for optimal configurations balancing performance and efficiency.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Researchers building biomedical and clinical NLP retrieval systems lack practical guidance for pipeline design decisions at scale.

Method: Empirical study across multiple public biomedical datasets with diverse query types (exam questions, conversational medical queries, community questions, non-question formulations), evaluating pipeline settings including corpus selection, chunk granularity, and vector index configurations; assessed using LLM-as-a-judge with human validation through win-rate comparison.

Result: Corpus aggregation outperforms other approaches for absolute retrieval quality; MedRAG/pubmed emerges as the Pareto-optimal singleton corpus under graph-based (HNSW) indexing; appropriate chunking strategies and FAISS indexing configurations offer favorable speed-efficiency trade-offs.

Conclusion: Researchers should consider corpus aggregation for best absolute quality; MedRAG/pubmed serves as the Pareto-optimal singleton corpus with HNSW indexing; optimal chunking strategies and FAISS indexing choices provide the best speed-efficiency trade-offs.

Abstract: Retrieval systems are increasingly used in biomedical and clinical natural language processing applications, yet practical guidance for researchers building such systems is limited. In this work, we provide such guidance through an empirical study of how retrieval pipeline design choices affect performance and efficiency at scale.
  In particular, we examine retrieval over a variety of existing, public biomedical text datasets, leveraging a variety of disparate types of queries, including exam-style questions, conversational medical queries, community-asked questions, and non-question formulations across various retrieval pipeline settings spanning corpus selection, chunk granularity, and vector index configuration. Retrieval results are judged using a robust, win-rate comparison assessment via an LLM-as-a-judge setting with human validation.
  Across these experiments, we identify several points of concrete guidance for reviewers, including the superiority of corpus aggregation for absolute retrieval quality, and the emergence of MedRAG/pubmed as the Pareto-optimal singleton corpus under graph-based (HNSW) indexing, appropriate chunking strategies, and FAISS indexing choices that offer the best trade-offs in speed and efficiency.

</details>


### [215] [ERA: Evidence-based Reliability Alignment for Honest Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20854)
*Sunguk Shin,Meeyoung Cha,Byung-Jun Lee,Sungwon Park*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出ERA框架，通过将置信度估计从标量概率转向显式证据分布，增强RAG系统的弃权行为。该方法利用Dirichlet分布建模内外知识的独立信念质量，并借助Dempster-Shafer理论量化信息源间的几何不一致性，从而区分认知不确定性和随机不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可靠性方法依赖标量置信度，无法显式区分RAG混合场景中的认知不确定性和数据固有模糊性。当模型参数知识与检索信息发生冲突时，缺乏有效的机制来指导系统何时应弃权而非给出不可靠回答。

Method: 提出两个核心组件：(1) 情境证据量化 - 通过Dirichlet分布将内部和外部知识建模为独立信念质量；(2) 知识冲突量化 - 利用Dempster-Shafer理论(DST)严格测量信息源间的几何不一致性。两组件协同实现认知不确定性与随机不确定性的解耦。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在标准基准和精选的泛化数据集上显著优于基线方法，在答案覆盖率和弃权之间实现了更优的权衡，并展现出更优的校准性能。

Conclusion: 通过从标量概率转向证据分布的范式转变，ERA框架能有效增强RAG系统的可靠性决策能力，为处理知识冲突场景提供了理论基础和实用方案。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) grounds language models in factual evidence but introduces critical challenges regarding knowledge conflicts between internalized parameters and retrieved information. However, existing reliability methods, typically relying on scalar confidence, fail to explicitly distinguish between epistemic uncertainty and inherent data ambiguity in such hybrid scenarios. In this paper, we propose a new framework called ERA (Evidence-based Reliability Alignment) to enhance abstention behavior in RAG systems by shifting confidence estimation from scalar probabilities to explicit evidence distributions. Our method consists of two main components: (1) Contextual Evidence Quantification, which models internal and external knowledge as independent belief masses via the Dirichlet distribution, and (2) Quantifying Knowledge Conflict, which leverages Dempster-Shafer Theory (DST) to rigorously measure the geometric discordance between information sources. These components are used to disentangle epistemic uncertainty from aleatoric uncertainty and modulate the optimization objective based on detected conflicts. Experiments on standard benchmarks and a curated generalization dataset demonstrate that our approach significantly outperforms baselines, optimizing the trade-off between answer coverage and abstention with superior calibration.

</details>


### [216] [CRED-1: An Open Multi-Signal Domain Credibility Dataset for Automated Pre-Bunking of Online Misinformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20856)
*Alexander Loth,Martin Kappes,Marc-Oliver Pahl*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CRED-1 是一个开放、可复现的域名级可信度数据集，融合 OpenSources.co 与 Iffy.news 两个开放许可来源列表，并附加域名年龄（RDAP）、网站流行度（Tranco Top-1M）、事实核查频次（Google Fact Check Tools API）与威胁情报（Google Safe Browsing API）四类计算型增强信号，覆盖 2,672 个被标记为 fake/unreliable/mixed/conspiracy/satire 的域名，每个域名附 0.0–1.0 的综合可信度分。目标场景是部署在端侧的隐私友好型浏览器扩展，在内容分发阶段完成客户端“预辟谣”（pre-bunking）。整条流水线仅用 Python 标准库实现，完全可复现，数据与代码以 CC BY 4.0 释出并归档于 Zenodo（DOI 10.5281/zenodo.18769460）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线错误信息的治理实践长期依赖 NewsGuard、MBFC API 等商业专有数据库，存在三重问题：一是不可获取、不可审计，二是成本高昂、难以在浏览器等端侧场景中实时调用，三是仅停留在“事后核查/事后拦截”环节。已有专家调研指出“内容投递环节”是错误信息杀伤链中的关键干预点，但缺乏可嵌入、可离线、隐私友好的域名级可信度先验（prior）。此外，主流事实核查研究多聚焦声明级（claim-level）判定，缺少系统性的域名级 ground truth 作为自动化事实核查流水线的补充信号。本文作者同时观察到学界/业界常用的一些启发式假设在错误信息场景下并不成立（如“新注册域名更可能是虚假站点”“被安全浏览标记即高风险”），因此需要用公开数据对多信号融合的可信度评分模型进行实证构建与验证。

Method: 方法为两阶段全自动流水线，均用仅依赖标准库（json、csv、urllib、zipfile）的独立 Python 脚本实现。阶段一（build_dataset.py）获取并合并两个开放许可来源：OpenSources.co（CC BY 4.0，Zimdars 编纂的 825 个域名分类列表）与 Iffy.news Index（MIT 许可，2,040 个 MBFC 低/极低事实性评级域名，含事实评级、政治倾向与数值可信度分）；经规范化（小写化、去 www. 前缀、去尾部斜杠）与去重后得到 2,672 个唯一域名，193 个（7.2%）双源重叠，重叠时取可信度更低的类别。阶段二（enrich_dataset.py）逐域计算四类增强信号：①域名年龄——通过公共 RDAP 查询注册日期，成功解析 2,325 个（87%），中位 14.0 年；②流行度——匹配 Tranco Top-1M 榜单，命中 704 个（26.3%），其中 56 个进入 Top 10,000；③事实核查频次——调用 Google Fact Check Tools API 查询 ClaimReview 声明数，67 个域（2.5%）至少命中一条，共 332 条声明；④威胁情报——调用 Google Safe Browsing Lookup API 做二进制标记，全部 2,672 个中仅 2 个被标记。评分模型为五信号加权融合：S = w_cat·s_cat + w_iffy·s_iffy + w_fc·s_fc + w_tranco·s_tranco + w_age·s_age + w_fill·s_cat，基础权重分别为 0.50/0.15/0.15/0.05/0.05，缺失信号的权重以 w_fill 回退到类别分；各分量为：类别查表（fake=0.0、conspiracy=0.1、unreliable=0.2、satire=0.3、mixed=0.5、reliable=1.0）、Iffy 原始分、s_fc = max(0, 1−log10(claims)/1.7)、s_tranco = max(0, 1−log10(rank)/6)、s_age = min(1, age/20)；Safe Browsing 命中域设硬上限 S=0.05。输出两种格式：紧凑 JSON（145 KB，字段 s/c/n/r/a，供端侧嵌入）与完整 CSV（18 字段，含原始信号与各评分分量，按分数升序）。复现仅需免费 Google Cloud API key（GOOGLE_API_KEY 或 macOS Keychain），全流程约 30 分钟，仓库 CODEBOOK.md 提供 SHA-256 校验和。

Result: 数据集共 2,672 个域名，六类分布为 mixed 1,335（50.0%）、unreliable 589（22.0%）、fake 493（18.4%）、conspiracy 153（5.7%）、satire 94（3.5%）、reliable 仅 8（0.3%）。综合分范围 0.000–0.962，均值 0.299，标准差 0.170，呈双峰分布：846 个（31.7%）低于 0.2，1,335 个（50.0%）落在 0.4–0.6，0.6–0.8 区间为空，仅 8 个高于 0.8。增强信号覆盖率差异显著：Iffy 分 76.3%、RDAP 年龄 87.0%、Tranco 26.3%、事实核查声明 2.5%、Safe Browsing 0.07%。四项关键实证观察：(1)域名年龄并非强错误信息信号，中位 14.0 年说明大量错误信息站点长期存在，与安全检测中“新注册即高危”的启发式相悖；(2)绝大多数域名无任何事实核查记录，被核查最多者为 trump.news（52 条）、thegatewaypundit.com（26）、naturalnews.com（22）、infowars.com（8）、breitbart.com（7）；(3)错误信息与恶意软件基本不相交（仅 2/2,672 被标记），表明其在技术合法性边界内运作；(4)双源重叠提供独立佐证，n 字段支持按置信度过滤。作者同时明确列出局限：英语偏差、时间有效性（v1.0 快照至 2026 年 2 月）、负信号设计（缺席≠可靠）、上游依赖与继承偏差。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提供了一个开放、透明、可复现、免付费的域名级可信度数据集与可复现流水线，证明仅凭公开数据即可构造多信号可信度评分，无需专有数据库或人工标注。其价值定位在于：作为错误信息研究的 ground truth、作为浏览器扩展/内容过滤器的可嵌入资源（145 KB JSON 支持端侧、隐私友好、无服务端依赖）、作为自动化事实核查流水线的域名级先验补充，并在内容投递环节实现自动化 pre-bunking。核心洞见是：域名年龄、流行度、事实核查频次、恶意软件标记等常见代理信号在错误信息场景下解释力有限且稀疏，因此以多源类别共识为主导（w_cat=0.50）、并对缺失信号做类别回退的加权融合方案，比单启发式规则更稳健；这一设计也使数据集成为持续更新的负信号参考基线，并支持研究者检验或反驳主流的“域龄/黑名单”类假设。

Abstract: This article presents CRED-1, an open, reproducible domain-level credibility dataset combining two openly-licensed source lists (OpenSources.co and Iffy.news) with four computed enrichment signals: domain age (WHOIS/RDAP), web popularity (Tranco Top-1M), fact-check frequency (Google Fact Check Tools API), and threat intelligence (Google Safe Browsing API). The dataset covers 2,672 domains categorized as fake, unreliable, mixed, conspiracy, or satire, each assigned a composite credibility score between 0.0 and 1.0. CRED-1 is designed for on-device deployment in privacy-preserving browser extensions to enable client-side pre-bunking of misinformation at the content delivery stage. The entire pipeline is implemented in Python using only standard library modules and is fully reproducible from publicly available sources. The dataset and pipeline code are released under CC~BY~4.0 and archived on Zenodo.

</details>


### [217] [DiagramBank: A Large-scale Dataset of Diagram Design Exemplars with Paper Metadata for Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20857)
*Tingwen Zhang,Ling Yue,Zhen Xu,Shaowu Pan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: DiagramBank解决AI科学家系统中论文生成流程的瓶颈问题——自动生成高质量科学图表（teaser figure）。该数据集包含89,422张从顶级学术论文中提取的原理图，配套丰富的上下文信息（摘要、标题、图表引用对），支持多模态检索和范例驱动的图表生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI科学家系统能够自动撰写科学手稿和代码，但在生成出版级科学图表（如teaser figure）方面仍是主要瓶颈。Teaser figure作为战略性视觉界面，需要概念性综合和规划，将复杂逻辑流程转化为引导直觉、激发好奇心的图形。现有AI系统通常忽略或使用劣质替代方案。

Method: 1) 自动筛选管道：从顶级学术论文中提取图表及其文内引用；2) CLIP过滤：区分原理图与标准数据图表/自然图像；3) 上下文配对：为每个图表配对摘要、标题、图表引用对等多粒度信息；4) 检索增强生成：提供可检索索引格式和RAG代码库用于范例条件合成。

Result: 构建了包含89,422张原理图的大规模数据集，来自顶级学术出版物，每条数据配有丰富的上下文信息，支持不同查询粒度下的信息检索。

Conclusion: DiagramBank公开于HuggingFace和GitHub，为多模态检索和teaser figure的范例条件合成提供了基准数据和工具，推动AI科学家系统的端到端论文生成能力。

Abstract: Recent advances in autonomous ``AI scientist'' systems have demonstrated the ability to automatically write scientific manuscripts and codes with execution. However, producing a publication-grade scientific diagram (e.g., teaser figure) is still a major bottleneck in the ``end-to-end'' paper generation process. For example, a teaser figure acts as a strategic visual interface and serves a different purpose than derivative data plots. It demands conceptual synthesis and planning to translate complex logic workflow into a compelling graphic that guides intuition and sparks curiosity. Existing AI scientist systems usually omit this component or fall back to an inferior alternative. To bridge this gap, we present DiagramBank, a large-scale dataset consisting of 89,422 schematic diagrams curated from existing top-tier scientific publications, designed for multimodal retrieval and exemplar-driven scientific figure generation. DiagramBank is developed through our automated curation pipeline that extracts figures and corresponding in-text references, and uses a CLIP-based filter to differentiate schematic diagrams from standard plots or natural images. Each instance is paired with rich context from abstract, caption, to figure-reference pairs, enabling information retrieval under different query granularities. We release DiagramBank in a ready-to-index format and provide a retrieval-augmented generation codebase to demonstrate exemplar-conditioned synthesis of teaser figures. DiagramBank is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/zhangt20/DiagramBank with code at https://github.com/csml-rpi/DiagramBank.

</details>


### [218] [Mixture of Sequence: Theme-Aware Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Sequence Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20858)
*Xiao Lin,Zhicheng Tang,Weilin Cong,Mengyue Hang,Kai Wang,Yajuan Wang,Zhichen Zeng,Ting-Wei Li,Hyunsik Yoo,Zhining Liu,Xuying Ning,Ruizhong Qiu,Wen-yen Chen,Shuo Chang,Rong Jin,Huayu Li,Hanghang Tong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为MoS（序列混合）的模型无关的MoE框架，用于解决长序列推荐中的用户兴趣转移问题。通过主题感知路由机制过滤无关信息，并利用多尺度融合机制捕获多维度特征，在保持SOTA性能的同时实现了高效计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长序列建模中存在关键挑战：用户兴趣常发生显著转移，导致序列中引入大量不相关甚至误导性的信息。论文通过实证分析发现了一种反复出现的行为模式——"会话跳跃"（session hopping）：用户在短时间跨度内兴趣保持稳定，但在会话间剧烈转移，且可能在多个会话后再次出现。

Method: MoS框架包含两个核心机制：1) 主题感知路由机制：自适应学习用户序列的潜在主题，将序列组织为多个连贯的子序列，每个子序列仅包含对齐特定主题的会话；2) 多尺度融合机制：利用三种类型的专家分别捕获全局序列特征、短期用户行为和主题特定语义模式。

Result: 实验结果表明，MoS在与其他MoE方法相比引入了更少的FLOPs的同时，始终保持SOTA性能表现。

Conclusion: MoS通过主题感知路由机制和多尺度融合机制的协同作用，在减少FLOPs的同时实现了SOTA性能，证明了其在效用与效率之间的优秀平衡能力。该框架具有模型无关性，可灵活集成到现有序列推荐模型中。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation has rapidly advanced in click-through rate prediction due to its ability to model dynamic user interests. A key challenge, however, lies in modeling long sequences: users often exhibit significant interest shifts, introducing substantial irrelevant or misleading information. Our empirical analysis corroborates this challenge and uncovers a recurring behavioral pattern in long sequences (\textit{session hopping}): user interests remain stable within short temporal spans (\textit{sessions}) but shift drastically across sessions and may reappear after multiple sessions. To address this challenge, we propose the Mixture of Sequence (MoS) framework, a model-agnostic MoE approach that achieves accurate predictions by extracting theme-specific and multi-scale subsequences from noisy raw user sequences. First, MoS employs a theme-aware routing mechanism to adaptively learn the latent themes of user sequences and organizes these sequences into multiple coherent subsequences. Each subsequence contains only sessions aligned with a specific theme, thereby effectively filtering out irrelevant or even misleading information introduced by user interest shifts in session hopping. In addition, to alleviate potential information loss, we introduce a multi-scale fusion mechanism, which leverages three types of experts to capture global sequence characteristics, short-term user behaviors, and theme-specific semantic patterns. Together, these two mechanisms endow MoS with the ability to deliver accurate recommendations from multi-faceted and multi-scale perspectives. Experimental results demonstrate that MoS consistently achieves the SOTA performance while introducing fewer FLOPs compared with other MoE counterparts, providing strong evidence of its excellent balance between utility and efficiency. The code is available at https://github.com/xiaolin-cs/MoS.

</details>


### [219] [KGiRAG: An Iterative GraphRAG Approach for Responding Sensemaking Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20859)
*Isabela Iacob,Melisa Marian,Gheorghe Cosmin Silaghi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出名为 KGiRAG 的迭代式、反馈驱动的 GraphRAG 架构：在知识图谱上做边级语义检索生成答案，用 LLM-as-a-judge 评估回答的忠实性与完整性，若不达标则扩展上下文（沿图多走一层边）并重新生成，最多 4 轮。在 HotPotQA 500 题子集上，KGiRAG+NER 的忠实性 0.95（与微软 GraphRAG 持平）、完整性 0.86、相关性 0.64、余弦相似度 0.325，整体优于 RARR 和静态基线，说明迭代检索+质量门控能有效降低幻觉并提升答案质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在生成超出其先验知识的复杂"sensemaking 查询"（需跨多文档综合信息的段落级回答）时容易产生幻觉；经典向量式 RAG 受上下文窗口限制难以整合多个相互关联的概念。虽然已有 GraphRAG 方法（微软 GraphRAG、GRAG 等）能利用知识图谱结构化证据，但它们多为一站式静态检索，缺少对生成结果质量的自动评估与反馈，难以系统性地抑制幻觉。此外，一次性从图上抓取大量上下文会带来高昂计算成本和过长的上下文，损害 LLM 判断质量。

Method: 架构假设已存在覆盖领域知识的知识图谱（KG），并预先按微软 GraphRAG 流程做社区检测得到社区摘要与嵌入。流程：(1) 问题处理——可选 NER 抽取命名实体，实验显示 NER 有效故保留，同时对问题做向量化；(2) 边级语义检索——用 all-MiniLM-L6-v2 将问题与图社区嵌入做余弦相似度检索，取回相关文本单元与社区摘要，构成"fringe"；(3) Prompt 生成——证据按语义相关性重排、去重，分为结构化事实三元组与非结构化文本单元，拼接为含问题、上下文和生成指令的提示（要求仅依据上下文作答，信息不足须显式声明、不得编造）；(4) 答案生成与评估——用 Mixtral-8x7B-Instruct-v0.1 生成答案，并以 BERTScore/余弦相似度及 LLM-as-a-judge 的 faithfulness、completeness、relevance 打分，忠实性与完整性均需 ≥0.8 才接受，否则触发反馈；(5) 上下文扩展——从已选实体出发在图上再走一条边，发现新实体与关系并扩入上下文；(6) 最多迭代 4 轮强制终止。实验对比四种配置：KGiRAG+NER、KGiRAG（无 NER 消融）、微软 GraphRAG、RARR（后两者用 GPT-4o mini），统一在微软 GraphRAG 基准数据中 HotPotQA 过滤子集抽样的 500 题上评测，并统计各轮次解决数量与指标演化。

Result: KGiRAG+NER：faithfulness 0.95±0.019、completeness 0.86±0.030、relevance 0.64±0.042、BERTScore 0.79±0.002、余弦相似度 0.325±0.027；KGiRAG（无 NER）：0.78/0.69/0.76/0.81/0.52；微软 GraphRAG：0.95/0.62/0.23/0.77/0.075；RARR：0.90/0.41/0.59/0.797/0.33。结论：KGiRAG+NER 在忠实性上与微软 GraphRAG 持平，完整性与相关性显著更优，余弦相似度大幅提升，BERTScore 略高；分数分布更稳定，说明幻觉减少。迭代统计显示首轮仅约一半问题达标，NER 模块使最终轮未解决查询数减少约 40%；忠实性在第 3 轮达到 0.95 峰值，故设 4 轮为终止条件。NER 有明显增益（忠实性 0.78→0.95），但无 NER 版本在 BERTScore 与余弦相似度上略高，揭示 NER 可能带来上下文收窄的权衡。

Conclusion: KGiRAG 表明：图结构化知识与 RAG 管线结合可提升 sensemaking 查询的回答质量；相比单次静态检索，"质量评估门控 + 上下文逐轮扩展"的迭代式策略能产出语义质量更高、相关性与完整性更好且幻觉更少的答案，同时通过按需加深图遍历（而非一次性大量抓取）更好地平衡质量与计算成本。作者明确指出结果仍属初步：需在更多带明确 ground truth 的数据集上做实验、需要针对各组件的全面消融、并尝试更强大的 LLM 及替代的答案接受判据（如基于指标不再提升的软性停止规则）。

Abstract: Recent literature highlights the potential of graph-based approaches within large language model (LLM) retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) pipelines for answering queries of varying complexity, particularly those that fall outside the LLM's prior knowledge. However, LLMs are prone to hallucination and often face technical limitations in handling contexts large enough to ground complex queries effectively. To address these challenges, we propose a novel iterative, feedback-driven GraphRAG architecture that leverages response quality assessment to iteratively refine outputs until a sound, well-grounded response is produced. Evaluating our approach with queries from the HotPotQA dataset, we demonstrate that this iterative RAG strategy yields responses with higher semantic quality and improved relevance compared to a single-shot baseline.

</details>


### [220] [RealRoute: Dynamic Query Routing System via Retrieve-then-Verify Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20860)
*Jiahe Liu,Qinkai Yu,Jingcheng Niu,Xi Zhu,Zirui He,Zhen Xiang,Fan Yang,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: RealRoute提出了一种从"预测式路由"到"检索-验证"范式的转变，通过并行、跨源的证据检索和动态验证器来解决异构知识源上的RAG路由难题，显著提升多跳推理任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统采用"LLM-as-a-Router"进行预测式路由，但在处理异构知识源（私有数据库、全文语料库、API等）时，由于源边界模糊，容易导致路由错误，影响答案的完整性和准确性。

Method: RealRoute提出Retrieve-then-Verify机制：1) 并行、跨源无关的检索策略确保证据完整性；2) 动态验证器对多源结果进行交叉检查；3) 综合生成基于事实的答案。系统提供可视化界面展示实时重路由过程和跨知识孤岛的验证链。

Result: 在多跳RAG推理任务中，RealRoute显著优于预测式路由基线方法。

Conclusion: RealRoute通过验证范式而非预测范式，有效解决了异构知识源场景下的路由难题，为RAG系统提供了更可靠的事实 grounding 能力。系统已开源。

Abstract: Despite the success of Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) in grounding LLMs with external knowledge, its application over heterogeneous sources (e.g., private databases, global corpora, and APIs) remains a significant challenge. Existing approaches typically employ an LLM-as-a-Router to dispatch decomposed sub-queries to specific sources in a predictive manner. However, this "LLM-as-a-Router" strategy relies heavily on the semantic meaning of different data sources, often leading to routing errors when source boundaries are ambiguous. In this work, we introduce RealRoute System, a framework that shifts the paradigm from predictive routing to a robust Retrieve-then-Verify mechanism. RealRoute ensures \textit{evidence completeness through parallel, source-agnostic retrieval, followed by a dynamic verifier that cross-checks the results and synthesizes a factually grounded answer}. Our demonstration allows users to visualize the real-time "re-routing" process and inspect the verification chain across multiple knowledge silos. Experiments show that RealRoute significantly outperforms predictive baselines in the multi-hop Rag reasoning task. The RealRoute system is released as an open-source toolkit with a user-friendly web interface. The code is available at the URL: https://github.com/Joseph1951210/RealRoute.

</details>


### [221] [Deep Interest Mining with Cross-Modal Alignment for SemanticID Generation in Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20861)
*Yagchen Zeng*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对生成式推荐(GR)中基于语义ID(SID)的三重退化问题（信息退化、语义退化、模态扭曲），提出集成DCIM、CMSA和QARM三大创新的统一框架，利用VLM对齐多模态特征，通过深度兴趣挖掘保留上下文信息，并采用强化学习机制筛选高质量SID，在多个基准上实现最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐依赖语义ID将万亿级数据压缩为可学习的词汇序列，但现有方法存在严重缺陷：两阶段压缩导致语义信息损失，多模态特征对齐不足，且缺乏区分高质量与低质量SID的后验机制，限制了推荐系统的性能上限。

Method: 提出统一框架包含三个核心创新：(1) CMSA利用视觉-语言模型将非文本模态对齐到统一文本语义空间；(2) DCIM通过深度兴趣挖掘机制捕获广告上下文中的高层语义信息，引入重构监督保留关键上下文；(3) QARM采用强化学习框架与质量感知奖励机制，在后验阶段优选语义丰富的SID并抑制低质量生成。

Result: 实验表明该方法在多个基准测试中一致优于现有最先进的SID生成方法，消融实验验证了各组件的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过VLM驱动的跨模态对齐、深度上下文兴趣挖掘和强化学习质量控制的三重创新，有效解决了生成式推荐中语义ID的三重退化问题，为构建高质量、可扩展的生成式推荐系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Generative Recommendation (GR) has demonstrated remarkable performance in next-token prediction paradigms, which relies on Semantic IDs (SIDs) to compress trillion-scale data into learnable vocabulary sequences.
