However, existing methods suffer from three critical limitations: (1) Information Degradation: the two-stage compression pipeline causes semantic loss and information degradation, with no posterior mechanism to distinguish high-quality from low-quality SIDs; (2) Semantic Degradation: cascaded quantization discards key semantic information from original multimodal features, as the embedding generation and quantization stages are not jointly optimized toward a unified objective; (3) Modality Distortion: quantizers fail to properly align text and image modalities, causing feature misalignment even when upstream networks have aligned them. To address these challenges, we propose a novel framework integrating three key innovations: Deep Contextual Interest Mining (DCIM), Cross-Modal Semantic Alignment (CMSA), and Quality-Aware Reinforcement Mechanism (QARM). First, we leverage Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to align non-textual modalities into a unified text-based semantic space, mitigating modality distortion. Second, we introduce a deep interest mining mechanism that captures high-level semantic information implicitly present in advertising contexts, encouraging SIDs to preserve critical contextual information through reconstruction-based supervision. Third, we employ a reinforcement learning framework with quality-aware rewards to encourage semantically rich SIDs while suppressing low-quality ones in the posterior stage. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach consistently outperforms state-of-the-art SID generation methods, achieving superior performance on multiple benchmarks. Ablation studies further validate the effectiveness of each proposed component

</details>


### [222] [Following the Eye-Tracking Evidence: Established Web-Search Assumptions Fail in Carousel Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21019)
*Jingwei Kang,Maarten de Rijke,Harrie Oosterhuis*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究发现网页搜索界面的用户行为假设（如F模式、检查假设）不能直接应用于流媒体轮播界面。轮播界面用户表现出独特的L模式检查行为，且忽视标题直接关注内容项，提示现有点击模型和评估指标需要重新评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮播界面是流媒体服务的事实标准，但用户行为研究不足。之前的工作假设网页搜索界面的行为模式（如F模式和检查假设）也适用于轮播界面，但这一假设未被实证验证。

Method: 通过分析来自眼动追踪研究的交互和检查数据集，验证轮播界面上的用户行为假设。

Result: （1）F模式仅适用于垂直检查，不适用于水平滑动；在点击条件下用户检查遵循轮播界面特有的L模式；（2）检查假设在轮播界面上不成立；（3）用户通常忽略轮播标题而直接关注内容项。

Conclusion: 现有用户行为模型（尤其是检查模式）无法从网页搜索界面迁移到轮播推荐场景。基于轮播界面的用户行为缺乏可靠研究基础，现有的评估指标和点击模型可能需要重新审视。

Abstract: Carousel interfaces have been the de-facto standard for streaming media services for over a decade. Yet, there has been very little research into user behavior with such interfaces, which thus remains poorly understood. Due to this lack of empirical research, previous work has assumed that behaviors established in single-list web-search interfaces, such as the F-pattern and the examination hypothesis, also apply to carousel interfaces, for instance when designing click models or evaluation metrics. We analyze a recently-released interaction and examination dataset resulting from an eye-tracking study performed on carousel interfaces to verify whether these assumptions actually hold.
  We find that (i)~the F-pattern holds only for vertical examination and not for horizontal swiping; additionally, we discover that, when conditioned on a click, user examination follows an L-pattern unique to carousel interfaces; (ii)~click-through-rates conditioned on examination indicate that the well-known examination hypothesis does not hold in carousel interfaces; and (iii)~contrary to the assumptions of previous work, users generally ignore carousel headings and focus directly on the content items. Our findings show that many user behavior assumptions, especially concerning examination patterns, do not transfer from web search interfaces to carousel recommendation settings. Our work shows that the field lacks a reliable foundation on which to build models of user behavior with these interfaces. Consequently, a re-evaluation of existing metrics and click models for carousel interfaces may be warranted.

</details>


### [223] [Automated Extraction of Pharmacokinetic Parameters from Structured XML Scientific Articles: Enhancing Data Accessibility at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21063)
*Remya Ampadi Ramachandran,Lisa A. Tell,Sidharth Rai,Nuwan Millagaha Gedara,Hossein Sholehrasa,Jim E. Riviere,Majid Jaberi-Douraki*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文讨论了药理学领域中定量药代动力学(PK)数据的自动化表格检测与提取的迫切需求。现有PK数据分散存储于XML、HTML、PDF等格式中，手动提取效率低且易出错。FARAD机构虽已手动提取数据40余年，但面对每日大量发表的文献，亟需基于AI的表格检测与提取算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 药理学R&D面临获取集中化、标准化PK数据的困难。定量PK参数分散于多种在线资源中，以复杂表格形式呈现。手动提取数据工作量大、容易出错，加之人员短缺问题，自动化提取成为必然需求。

Method: 论文提出需要开发能够精确处理表格结构的AI算法，重点关注：1) 表格检测；2) 基于行列表头信息的单元格内容提取；3) 符合人类阅读习惯的信息理解方式。

Result: 研究指出表格信息的提取难度与表格结构复杂度密切相关。需构建能准确识别列/行表头、保持表格结构完整性的自动化系统。

Conclusion: 开发能够处理复杂表格布局的AI表格检测与提取算法是当前药理学数据提取领域的核心任务，对于提高PK数据获取效率、支持药物研发具有重要意义。

Abstract: In the field of pharmacology, there is a notable absence of centralized, comprehensive, and up-to-date repositories of PK data. This poses a significant challenge for R&D as it can be a time-consuming and challenging task to collect all the required quantitative PK parameters from diverse scientific publications. This quantitative PK information is predominantly organized in tabular format, mostly available as XML, HTML, or PDF files within various online repositories and scientific publications, including supplementary materials. This makes tables one of the crucial components and information elements of scientific or regulatory documents as they are commonly utilized to present quantitative information. Extracting data from tables is typically a labor-intensive process, and alternative automated machine learning models may struggle to accurately detect and extract the relevant data due to the complex nature and diverse layouts of tabular data. The difficulty of information extraction and reading order detection is largely dependent on the structural complexity of the tables. Efforts to understand tables should prioritize capturing the content of table cells in a manner that aligns with how a human reader naturally comprehends the information. FARAD has been manually extracting tabular data and other information from literature and regulatory agencies for over 40 years. However, there is now an urgent need to automate this process due to the large volume of publications released daily. The accuracy of this task has become increasingly challenging, as manual extraction is tedious and prone to errors, especially given the staffing shortages we are currently facing. This necessitates the development of AI algorithms for table detection and extraction that are able to precisely handle cells organized according to the table structure, as indicated by column and/or row header information.

</details>


### [224] [Multilingual and Domain-Agnostic Tip-of-the-Tongue Query Generation for Simulated Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21096)
*Xuhong He,To Eun Kim,Maik Fröbe,Jaime Arguello,Bhaskar Mitra,Fernando Diaz*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了首个大规模多语言舌尖现象(ToT)检索基准数据集，涵盖中文、日文、韩文和英文四种语言，每种语言包含5,000条查询。提出了基于LLM的查询模拟框架，系统研究了提示语言和源文档语言对模拟查询质量的影响，为非英语语言的ToT数据集构建提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的舌尖现象(ToT)检索基准测试主要集中于英语，限制了其在多语言信息检索领域的应用。构建多语言ToT测试集对于支持跨语言信息访问至关重要。

Method: 采用基于LLM的查询模拟框架构建多语言ToT测试集，系统研究了两大关键因素：1) 提示语言（prompt language）；2) 源文档语言（source document language）。通过计算模拟查询与真实用户查询之间的系统排序相关性来验证合成查询的保真度。

Result: 研究表明，有效的ToT模拟需要语言感知的设计选择：非英语语言源通常很重要，但当非英语源信息不足以生成查询时，英语Wikipedia可作为有益补充。实验验证了所提方法在多语言场景下的有效性。

Conclusion: 本研究发布了四个多语言ToT测试集（每种语言5,000条查询，涵盖多个领域），这是首个大规模多语言ToT基准测试，并为构建超越英语的现实ToT数据集提供了实践指导。

Abstract: Tip-of-the-Tongue (ToT) retrieval benchmarks have largely focused on English, limiting their applicability to multilingual information access. In this work, we construct multilingual ToT test collections for Chinese, Japanese, Korean, and English, using an LLM-based query simulation framework. We systematically study how prompt language and source document language affect the fidelity of simulated ToT queries, validating synthetic queries through system rank correlation against real user queries. Our results show that effective ToT simulation requires language-aware design choices: non-English language sources are generally important, while English Wikipedia can be beneficial when non-English sources provide insufficient information for query generation. Based on these findings, we release four ToT test collections with 5,000 queries per language across multiple domains. This work provides the first large-scale multilingual ToT benchmark and offers practical guidance for constructing realistic ToT datasets beyond English.

</details>


### [225] [PAPERMIND: Benchmarking Agentic Reasoning and Critique over Scientific Papers in Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21304)
*Yanjun Zhao,Tianxin Wei,Jiaru Zou,Xuying Ning,Yuanchen Bei,Lingjie Chen,Simmi Rana,Wendy H. Yang,Hanghang Tong,Jingrui He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: PAPERMIND是一个用于评估科研论文综合推理能力的基准测试平台，涵盖农业、生物、化学、计算机科学、医学、物理和经济7个领域，包含4类互补任务（多模态定位、实验解读、跨源证据推理和批判性评估），旨在诊断大模型在整合科学推理方面的能力差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准测试通常孤立评估科学理解能力，难以全面衡量模型整合文本与视觉信息、解读实验证据、跨源综合信息和批判性评估科学主张的综合能力。构建一个能够评估科学论文理解中相互关联的认知能力的统一基准具有重要意义。

Method: PAPERMIND基于7个领域的真实科学论文构建，包含4个互补任务族：多模态定位（连接文本与视觉信息）、实验解读（理解和评估实验证据）、跨源证据推理（综合多个来源的信息）和批判性评估（评估科学主张的有效性）。

Result: 对开源和闭源多模态大语言模型的广泛实验表明，模型在各类任务上均存在一致的性能差距，揭示了当前模型在整合科学推理和批判性评估方面面临持续性挑战。

Conclusion: PAPERMIND能够诊断难以通过孤立任务评估来发现的整合科学推理行为，为未来改进多模态大语言模型的科学推理能力提供了有价值的评估工具。基准数据集已公开于GitHub。

Abstract: Understanding scientific papers requires more than answering isolated questions or summarizing content. It involves an integrated reasoning process that grounds textual and visual information, interprets experimental evidence, synthesizes information across sources, and critically evaluates scientific claims. However, existing benchmarks typically assess these abilities in isolation, making it difficult to evaluate scientific paper understanding as a unified set of interacting cognitive abilities. In this work, we introduce PAPERMIND, a benchmark designed to evaluate integrated and agent-oriented scientific reasoning over research papers. PAPERMIND is constructed from real scientific papers across seven domains, including agriculture, biology, chemistry, computer science, medicine, physics, and economics. It comprises four complementary task families that collectively operationalize distinct cognitive facets of scientific paper reasoning, including multimodal grounding, experimental interpretation, cross-source evidence reasoning, and critical assessment. By analyzing model behavior across multiple tasks, PAPERMIND enables a diagnostic evaluation of integrated scientific reasoning behaviors that are difficult to assess through isolated task evaluations. Extensive experiments on both opensource and closed-source multimodal LLMs reveal consistent performance gaps across tasks, highlighting persistent challenges in integrated scientific reasoning and critique. Our benchmark and dataset are available at https:// github.com/Yanjun-Zhao/PaperMind.

</details>


### [226] [WPGRec: Wavelet Packet Guided Graph Enhanced Sequential Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21305)
*Peilin Liu,Zhiquan Ji,Gang Yan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: WPGRec提出了一种统一时频与图增强的序列推荐框架，通过平稳小波包变换生成等长平移不变子带序列，在子带层面进行图传播以对齐多分辨率时序建模与图结构信息，并采用能量和谱平坦度感知的门控融合模块自适应聚合有用子带、抑制噪声成分。实验表明该方法在多个数据集上优于基线模型，尤其在稀疏和行为复杂的数据集上效果显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有频域方法存在局限性：全局频谱操作易纠缠局部瞬态与长距离依赖；基于滤波器的子波处理在多尺度分解与重构中存在时间错位和边界伪影问题；且协同信号通常通过尺度不一致的辅助模块注入，未能有效联合建模时序动态与结构依赖关系。

Method: WPGRec包含三个核心组件：1）采用全树非抽样平稳小波包变换生成等长、平移不变的子带序列；2）在子带层面执行交互图传播，注入高阶协同信息同时保持跨分辨率的时间对齐；3）设计能量与谱平坦度感知的门控融合模块，自适应聚合信息子带并抑制噪声类分量。

Result: 在四个公开基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，WPGRec始终优于序列推荐和基于图的基线模型，在稀疏和行为复杂数据集上优势尤为明显，验证了尺度一致的图结构注入和自适应子带融合的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将多分辨率时序建模与图传播在匹配尺度上对齐，并结合自适应子带融合机制，WPGRec有效解决了传统频域方法的时间错位问题，显著提升了序列推荐性能。

Abstract: Sequential recommendation aims to model users' evolving interests from noisy and non-stationary interaction streams, where long-term preferences, short-term intents, and localized behavioral fluctuations may coexist across temporal scales. Existing frequency-domain methods mainly rely on either global spectral operations or filter-based wavelet processing. However, global spectral operations tend to entangle local transients with long-range dependencies, while filter-based wavelet pipelines may suffer from temporal misalignment and boundary artifacts during multi-scale decomposition and reconstruction. Moreover, collaborative signals from the user-item interaction graph are often injected through scale-inconsistent auxiliary modules, limiting the benefit of jointly modeling temporal dynamics and structural dependencies. To address these issues, we propose Wavelet Packet Guided Graph Enhanced Sequential Recommendation (WPGRec), a unified time-frequency and graph-enhanced framework that aligns multi-resolution temporal modeling with graph propagation at matching scales. WPGRec first applies a full-tree undecimated stationary wavelet packet transform to generate equal-length, shift-invariant subband sequences. It then performs subband-wise interaction-graph propagation to inject high-order collaborative information while preserving temporal alignment across resolutions. Finally, an energy- and spectral-flatness-aware gated fusion module adaptively aggregates informative subbands and suppresses noise-like components. Extensive experiments on four public benchmarks show that WPGRec consistently outperforms sequential and graph-based baselines, with particularly clear gains on sparse and behaviorally complex datasets, highlighting the effectiveness of band-consistent structure injection and adaptive subband fusion for sequential recommendation.

</details>


### [227] [From Tokens to Concepts: Leveraging SAE for SPLADE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21511)
*Yuxuan Zong,Mathias Vast,Basile Van Cooten,Laure Soulier,Benjamin Piwowarski*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出用稀疏自编码器(SAE)学习的语义概念潜在空间替代传统词汇表来解决SPLADE模型的词汇依赖问题，在保持检索性能的同时提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SPLADE等稀疏检索模型依赖底层词汇表，存在多义词和同义词问题，且难以支持多语言和多模态场景。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器(SAE)学习的语义概念潜在空间替换传统骨干词汇表，构建SAE-SPLADE模型，并探索训练方法和分析模型差异。

Result: SAE-SPLADE在域内和域外任务上均达到与SPLADE相当的检索性能，同时具有更高的效率。

Conclusion: SAE-SPLADE成功将语义概念潜在空间与稀疏检索结合，证明了在保持效果的同时提升效率的可行性。

Abstract: Learned Sparse IR models, such as SPLADE, offer an excellent efficiency-effectiveness tradeoff. However, they rely on the underlying backbone vocabulary, which might hinder performance (polysemicity and synonymy) and pose a challenge for multi-lingual and multi-modal usages. To solve this limitation, we propose to replace the backbone vocabulary with a latent space of semantic concepts learned using Sparse Auto-Encoders (SAE). Throughout this paper, we study the compatibility of these 2 concepts, explore training approaches, and analyze the differences between our SAE-SPLADE model and traditional SPLADE models. Our experiments demonstrate that SAE-SPLADE achieves retrieval performance comparable to SPLADE on both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks while offering improved efficiency.

</details>


### [228] [Pre-trained LLMs Meet Sequential Recommenders: Efficient User-Centric Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21536)
*Nikita Severin,Danil Kartushov,Vladislav Urzhumov,Vladislav Kulikov,Oksana Konovalova,Alexey Grishanov,Anton Klenitskiy,Artem Fatkulin,Alexey Vasilev,Andrey Savchenko,Ilya Makarov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种知识蒸馏方法，将预训练LLM生成的用户画像文本知识迁移到顺序推荐系统，在不增加推理成本的情况下提升模型语义理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有顺序推荐系统在捕获用户语义方面存在局限，而LLM虽具备推理能力但实时推理成本过高，无法直接应用于实际服务场景。

Method: 提出一种知识蒸馏方法，利用预训练LLM生成文本用户画像，将其知识蒸馏到顺序推荐模型中。该方法无需在服务时调用LLM，也无需修改模型架构或微调LLM。

Result: 方法在保持传统顺序模型推理效率的同时，有效融合了LLM的语义理解能力。

Conclusion: 该工作为将LLM能力高效融入推荐系统提供了一条可行路径，兼顾了模型性能与推理效率。

Abstract: Sequential recommender systems have achieved significant success in modeling temporal user behavior but remain limited in capturing rich user semantics beyond interaction patterns. Large Language Models (LLMs) present opportunities to enhance user understanding with their reasoning capabilities, yet existing integration approaches create prohibitive inference costs in real time. To address these limitations, we present a novel knowledge distillation method that utilizes textual user profile generated by pre-trained LLMs into sequential recommenders without requiring LLM inference at serving time. The resulting approach maintains the inference efficiency of traditional sequential models while requiring neither architectural modifications nor LLM fine-tuning.

</details>


### [229] [Counterfactual Multi-task Learning for Delayed Conversion Modeling in E-commerce Sales Pre-Promotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21675)
*Xin Song,Kaiyuan Li,Jinxin Hu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出CM-DCM模型，通过多任务架构、用户行为门控模块和反事实因果方法，解决促销前期用户转化率预测难题，显著提升延迟转化和直接转化的预测效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CVR预测研究忽视了促销前期这一关键时段的用户行为特征；当前延迟反馈建模方法未考虑促销前转化行为的分布偏移特点，延迟转化主要集中发生在促销当天而非连续时间窗口。

Method: 提出Counterfactual Multi-task Delayed Conversion Model (CM-DCM)，包含三个核心创新：(1)多任务架构联合建模直接转化和延迟转化；(2)个性化用户行为门控模块应对促销前期数据稀疏问题；(3)反事实因果方法建模加购至延迟转化的转移概率。

Result: 离线实验中CM-DCM在促销前场景下优于基线方法；在线A/B测试在大促期间广告收入、延迟转化GMV和整体GMV均获得显著提升。

Conclusion: CM-DCM有效解决了促销前期CVR预测的延迟转化问题，验证了多任务学习与因果推断结合在该场景的有效性，为电商大促营销策略提供了技术支撑。

Abstract: Sales promotions, as short-term incentives to stimulate product purchases, play a pivotal role in modern e-commerce marketing strategies. During promotional events, user behavior patterns exhibit distinct characteristics compared to regular periods. In the pre-promotion phase, users typically engage in product search and browsing without immediate purchases, adding items to carts in anticipation of promotional discounts. This behavior leads to delayed conversions, resulting in significantly lower conversion rates (CVR) before the promotion day. Although existing research has made progress in CVR prediction for promotion days using historical data, it largely overlooks the critical pre-promotion period. And delayed feedback modeling has been extensively studied, current approaches fail to account for the unique distribution shifts in conversion behavior before promotional events, where delayed conversions predominantly occur on the promotion day rather than over continuous time windows. To address these limitations, we propose the Counterfactual Multi-task Delayed Conversion Model (CM-DCM), which leverages historical pre-promotion data to enhance CVR prediction for both delayed and direct conversions. Our model incorporates three key innovations: (i) A multi-task architecture that jointly models direct and delayed conversions using historical pre-promotion data; (ii) A personalized user behavior gating module to mitigate data sparsity issues during brief pre-promotion periods; (iii) A counterfactual causal approach to model the transition probability from add-to-cart (ATC) to delayed conversion. Extensive experiments demonstrate that CM-DCM outperforms baselines in pre-promotion scenarios. Online A/B tests during major promotional events showed significant improvements in advertising revenue, delayed conversion GMV, and overall GMV, validating the effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [230] [Multistakeholder Impacts of Profile Portability in a Recommender Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21750)
*Anas Buhayh,Elizabeth McKinnie,Clement Canel,Robin Burke*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本研究探讨推荐系统中算法多元主义的政策含义，特别是当用户切换推荐算法时数据可移植性的影响。研究发现数据可移植性政策对不同推荐算法下的用户效用产生不同效果，为设计公平推荐生态系统提供了政策建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注算法层面的优化（如多目标模型、重排序），而对推荐生态系统的结构性改变研究不足；同时，算法多元主义（中间件）虽有益处，但用户切换算法时的数据迁移问题尚未被充分探讨。

Method: 基于仿真的方法，研究数据可移植性政策对用户模型和多方利益相关者结果的影响。

Result: 数据可移植性场景对不同推荐算法下的用户效用产生差异化影响；相关政策对生态系统各方参与者有重要影响。

Conclusion: 数据可移植性政策对不同推荐算法下用户效用的影响存在差异，本研究为设计公平推荐生态系统和相关政策制定提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Optimizing outcomes for multiple stakeholders in recommender systems has historically focused on algorithmic interventions, such as developing multi-objective models or re-ranking results from existing algorithms. However, structural changes to the recommendation ecosystem itself remain understudied. This paper explores the implications of algorithmic pluralism (also known as "middleware" in the governance literature), in which recommendation algorithms are decoupled from platforms, enabling users to select their preferred algorithm. Prior simulation work demonstrates that algorithmic choice benefits niche consumers and providers. Yet this approach raises critical questions about user modeling in the context of data portability: when users switch algorithms, what happens to their data? Noting that multiple data portability regulations have emerged to strengthen user data ownership and control. We examine how such policies affect user models and stakeholders' outcomes in recommendation setting. Our findings reveal that data portability scenarios produce varying effects on user utility across different recommendation algorithms. We highlight key policy considerations and implications for designing equitable recommendation ecosystems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Biomedical systems biology workflow orchestration and execution with PoSyMed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20906)
*Simon Süwer,Zoe Chervontseva,Kester Bagemihl,Jan Baumbach,Olga Tsoy,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PoSyMed是一个开源模块化平台，旨在解决生物信息学软件复用困难、工作流适配耗时的问题。该平台通过形式化工具描述、容器化构建与执行、持久化工作流状态和对话式用户界面，整合大语言模型作为有界语义助手，帮助用户识别工具、提议工作流步骤和支持参数化，从而提升生物医学分析的 reproducibility、traceability 和透明度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着科学软件的快速发展，生物信息学研究面临实用障碍：工具分布碎片化、文档不一致、依赖复杂、执行环境难以复现。这导致即使是有经验的用户，复用已发表工具和适配工作流也耗时且技术门槛高。

Method: PoSyMed采用后端中心化平台架构，结合形式化工具描述、受控的基于容器的构建和执行流程、持久化工作流状态，以及基于对话的用户界面。LLM被整合为有界语义助手，而非自主决策者，在类型化、验证和人类监督的执行环境中辅助工具识别、工作流步骤提议和参数支持。

Result: 平台已在代表性生物软件场景中验证，覆盖工作流支持、交互设计和平台可扩展性三个维度。

Conclusion: PoSyMed旨在单一平台内提升生物医学分析的reproducibility、traceability和透明度，平台已在https://apps.cosy.bio/posymed公开发布。

Abstract: The rapid growth of scientific software has created practical barriers for bioinformatics research. Although powerful statistical, artificial intelligence (AI)-based methods are now widely available, their effective use is often hindered by fragmented distribution, inconsistent documentation, complex dependencies, and difficult-to-reproduce execution environments. As a result, reusing published tools and workflow adaptation to own date remains technically demanding and time-intensive, even for experienced users. Here, we present PoSyMed, an open and modular platform for the controlled integration, composition, and execution of bioinformatics tools and workflows. PoSyMed combines a backend-centered platform architecture with formal tool descriptions, controlled container-based build and execution processes, persistent workflow state, and a dialogue-based user interface. Large language models (LLM) are integrated not as autonomous decision-makers, but as human-computer interface with bounded semantic assistants that help identify tools, propose workflow steps, and support parameterization within a typed, validated, and human-supervised execution environment. PoSyMed is designed to improve reproducibility, traceability, and transparency in practical biomedical analysis within one platform. We describe the system architecture and evaluate its behavior across representative biological software scenarios with respect to workflow support, interaction design, and platform extensibility. PoSyMed is publicly available at https://apps.cosy.bio/posymed.

</details>


### [232] [Learning Reasoning World Models for Parallel Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20926)
*Gautam Singh,Arjun Guha,Bhavya Kailkhura,Harshitha Menon*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出并行代码世界模型(PCWMs)，通过预测工具执行结果来替代昂贵的外部工具调用。实验证明该方法能显著提升模型在并行代码相关的竞争检测、性能分析等任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在串行代码生成方面表现出色，但在并行代码方面仍存在困难，原因在于训练数据相对稀缺。现有解决方案依赖编码智能体与外部工具交互，但工具调用成本高昂且有时不切实际（例如对于部分编写的代码）。

Method: 设计了一种新的探索和数据生成流程，对多样化的并行编程问题和候选实现进行采样，通过工具执行以记录数据竞争和性能分析结果，并从中综合出将源代码与工具执行结果因果关联的回顾性推理轨迹，用于微调训练PCWMs。

Result: 7B参数世界模型在竞争结果预测上从64.3%提升至72.8%；8B参数模型在性能分析任务上从49.3%提升至58.6%；在数据竞争修复任务中，7B世界模型相比自我反馈提升2.7%-9.1%，14B世界模型提升6.1%-11.1%。

Conclusion: 推理模型有潜力成为并行代码智能体中外部工具调用的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Large language models have shown remarkable ability in serial code generation, but they still struggle with parallel code for which training data is comparatively scarce. A common remedy is to use coding agents that interact with external tools, but tool calls can be costly and sometimes impractical, e.g., for partially written code. We propose Parallel-Code World Models (PCWMs), reasoning LLMs that aim to predict tool outcomes directly from parallel source code. To train PCWMs, we design a novel exploration and data generation pipeline that samples diverse parallel-coding problems and candidate implementations across multiple domains, then executes them via tools to record data races and performance profiles. From these, we synthesize hindsight reasoning traces that causally connect source code to observed tool outcomes. Fine-tuning on the resulting data yields noticeable gains, with a 7B-parameter world model improving from 64.3% to 72.8% accuracy in race-outcome prediction, while an 8B-parameter model improves in a performance profiling task from 49.3% to 58.6% accuracy. Furthermore, when open-weight models were tasked with fixing data races, world-model feedback improved their race-fixing rates relative to self-feedback by 2.7%-9.1% using our 7B-parameter world model and by 6.1%-11.1% using our 14B-parameter world model. Our results suggest that reasoning models have the potential to serve as practical substitutes for external tool calls in parallel-coding agents.

</details>


### [233] [Residual Risk Analysis in Benign Code: How Far Are We? A Multi-Model Semantic and Structural Similarity Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21051)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出残余风险评分(RRS)框架,通过代码语言模型和AST分析检测补丁后代码中可能残留的安全风险。研究发现约61%的高RRS代码对存在13类残余问题,证明代码级相似性可有效用于补丁后安全评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件安全依赖于有效的漏洞检测和修复,但确定补丁是否完全消除风险仍是未被充分探索的挑战。现有漏洞基准测试通常将修复后的函数视为固有良性,忽视了残留安全风险的可能性。

Method: 1. 从PrimeVul基准数据集分析 vulnerable-benign 函数对; 2. 使用多个代码语言模型(Code LMs)捕获语义相似性; 3. 使用基于Tree-sitter的AST分析捕获结构相似性; 4. 提出RRS框架,整合基于嵌入的语义相似性、局部AST结构相似性和跨模型一致性。

Result: 良性函数在语义和结构上通常与其对应漏洞版本高度相似,表明存在潜在残留风险。约61%的高RRS代码对表现出13种不同类别的残留问题(如空指针解引用、不安全内存分配),并通过Cppcheck、Clang-Tidy和Facebook-Infer等先进静态分析工具验证。

Conclusion: 代码级相似性为优先排序补丁后检查提供了实用信号,能够在实际开源软件流程中实现更可靠和可扩展的安全评估。

Abstract: Software security relies on effective vulnerability detection and patching, yet determining whether a patch fully eliminates risk remains an underexplored challenge. Existing vulnerability benchmarks often treat patched functions as inherently benign, overlooking the possibility of residual security risks. In this work, we analyze vulnerable-benign function pairs from the PrimeVul, a benchmark dataset using multiple code language models (Code LMs) to capture semantic similarity, complemented by Tree-sitter-based abstract syntax tree (AST) analysis for structural similarity. Building on these signals, we propose Residual Risk Scoring (RRS), a unified framework that integrates embedding-based semantic similarity, localized AST-based structural similarity, and cross-model agreement to estimate residual risk in code. Our analysis shows that benign functions often remain highly similar to their vulnerable counterparts both semantically and structurally, indicating potential persistence of residual risk. We further find that approximately $61\%$ of high-RRS code pairs exhibit $13$ distinct categories of residual issues (e.g., null pointer dereferences, unsafe memory allocation), validated using state-of-the-art static analysis tools including Cppcheck, Clang-Tidy, and Facebook-Infer. These results demonstrate that code-level similarity provides a practical signal for prioritizing post-patch inspection, enabling more reliable and scalable security assessment in real-world open-source software pipelines.

</details>


### [234] [Structural Quality Gaps in Practitioner AI Governance Prompts: An Empirical Study Using a Five-Principle Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21090)
*Christo Zietsman*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"五原则"框架来评估 AI 治理提示（governance prompt）是否"结构完整"，并对 34 个来自 GitHub 的 AGENTS.md 文件做了实证评估：总体均值仅 2.81/5，37% 的文件—模型配对低于结构完整性阈值，缺失最多的是"数据分类"与"评估量规"两项原则。作者还发现 AGENTS.md 约定存在一个此前未被记录的"工件分类缺口"（同一文件可能既是治理正文、又是指向其他文件的指针、或二者混合，社区未达成共识），并指出一个 404 断链案例。研究结论是：这些原则可直接转化为静态分析检查（治理提示 linter），在提示编写时即实施"左移"治理。文章自陈不足包括评分主观性、跨模型校准分歧（Gemini 系统性偏高）、构造效度（不评估内容正确性与时效性）、以及外部效度仅限于开源仓库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体治理越来越依赖自然语言提示（系统提示、治理简报、仓库级指令文件）来约束智能体行为，这些提示实质上是"可执行规约"，定义了智能体的任务、范围与质量标准。然而与传统软件需求规约不同，社区已有成熟的需求质量理论（IEEE 830 的完备性/一致性/无歧义性/可测试性、EARS 记法、QuARS 语言缺陷检测），却没有任何系统框架用于判断治理提示本身是否结构完备——实践者无标准地撰写、无验证步骤地部署，只能在智能体行为出错后才发现问题。作者指出这类失败本质是"规约失败"而非技术失败（Gartner 预测 40% 以上 agentic AI 项目将在 2027 年底前被取消；IBM 报告显示 shadow AI 出现在 20% 的数据泄露中，平均额外成本 67 万美元），并认为需求工程社区具备理论储备与实证方法来处理这一新的需求工件类别。

Method: 1）框架构建：提出五原则评估框架，理论依据分别来自 Rice 定理（可判定性→成功定义/可验证性）、Curry-Howard 对应（规约即类型声明→评估量规与质量门）、贝叶斯认识论（信念修正需先验/证据/更新规则→数据分类）。五原则为 P1 成功定义、P2 评估量规、P3 范围边界、P4 数据分类、P5 质量门，每项附可操作的检验问题（如"两个独立智能体阅读后是否会就'完成是什么样'达成一致"）。
2）评分协议：每原则采用 0 / 0.5 / 1.0 三级整体评分，总分 0–5；≥4.0 为结构完备，3.0–3.9 为功能可用但存在缺口，<3.0 为结构不完备。逐分值给出示例（附录 A）。
3）语料构建：在 GitHub 公共代码索引检索 AGENTS.md（2026 年 4 月），过滤条件为：含超过十行实质治理内容（纯自动生成/占位指令剔除）、文件在检索前六个月内至少一次提交（活跃度代理）、排除疑似工具生成、重复内容去重；重定向类文件例外保留以做"重定向解析"分析。最终得到 34 个仓库的 34 个文件。
4）评估流程：三个独立 LLM 评估者（Claude Opus 4.6、OpenAI Codex/gpt-5.4、Google Gemini）各自通读全文后独立打分，每个分值附一句话理由形成审计轨迹；评估时屏蔽仓库 star/贡献者等流行度信号以避免晕轮效应；对纯指针文件分别按"独立文档"与"重定向解析后"两种方式计分。
5）分析：总分分布、原则强度排序、评估者间收敛与分歧模式、评分原型聚类、以及效度威胁分析（内部效度、评分校准、构造效度、外部效度、生态效度）。

Result: 总体：34 文件的三模型平均总分为 2.81/5；102 个文件—模型配对中有 38 个（37%）低于 2.5 分的结构完整性阈值；无任何文件被三个评估者同时给出满分 5.0，最高分文件为作者自建的 nuphirho.dev（均值 4.67，已披露作者身份）。
原则强度排序（三模型均值，n=34）：P5 质量门 0.70 > P3 范围边界 0.60 = P2 评估量规 0.60 > P1 成功定义 0.57 > P4 数据分类 0.34。P4 是最弱原则，且被三模型一致判定为零分比例最高；P1 存在天花板效应，多数文件停在"部分符合"（暗示成功但未操作化）。范围边界得分高但多为"只定义正向范围、未定义禁止行为与越界处置"，构成部分得分聚集带。
评估者分歧：Gemini 均值 3.68、Codex 2.51、Claude 2.24，且 Gemini 始终偏高（系统性、方向性分歧，非噪声）；低于 2.5 的比例为 Gemini 15%、Codex 47%、Claude 50%。三方在定性结构上一致（同一最弱原则、同一主导原型、同一 P1 天花板效应），分歧集中在"部分符合给多少分"的校准。
结构原型三类：(1) 操作用手册（最常见：给出范围与部分质量门如 lint/test 命令，但缺少成功定义、量规与数据分类，代表 Langflow、Grafana、Prisma、Biome）；(2) 受限执行者（增加部分量规或点名具体工具/检查，代表 Apache Airflow、Ansible、Next.js）；(3) 最小指针（<10 行，仅指向 CLAUDE.md/CONTRIBUTING.md，独立评分五原则全为 0，解析后为 2.0–3.5，代表 VS Code、FastMCP、Prisma）。第四个特例：mark3labs/mcp-go 指向的 openspec/AGENTS.md 返回 404，属"断链"（声明治理存在但实际不存在）而非架构选择。无任何文件构成第五类"完整规约"原型。
工件分类缺口：AGENTS.md 在野外承担三种互不兼容的架构角色（治理正文／指针／混合），社区未就其定位达成共识（agentsmd/agents.md issue #66 自 2025 年 9 月提出重定向问题，至 2026 年 4 月仍未解决），这被作者视为质量评估的前置性障碍。

Conclusion: 实践者撰写的 AI 治理提示存在系统性结构缺口，表现为把治理提示当作"操作指令"而非"规约"来理解：可观测的工程惯例（lint 配置、测试命令、CI 流水线）天然映射到范围边界与质量门，而数据分类与评估量规缺乏既有实践对应物，因此最常缺席。五原则可被直接操作化为对提示文本的静态检查（完成标准句式、禁止/升级声明、自验证与证据格式、量规式枚举、分类处理声明），构成"治理提示 linter"的技术规格，从而把治理检查左移到编写时刻。作者主张当下即可将五原则用于组织现有治理提示的手工审阅。理论层面，本文仅主张五原则是结构完备性的必要条件，其充分性、权重设定与跨评估者校准均属开放问题；五原则与作者另一项关于可执行 BDD 规约充当 AI 代码评审质量门的研究合并，构成 AI 辅助开发中"治理层—实现验证层"的分层规约质量模型。
值得注意的内部一致性问题：摘要与结果节对 37% 的口径表述不一致（"文件—模型配对" vs "文件"）；一致性分析处称"P3 最强"，与表 1 中 P5（0.70）排名最高相矛盾。此外语料为 2026 年 4 月自 GitHub 公共索引采集的 34 个文件，样本量小、限定单一约定与开源语料，且评分依赖 LLM 评估者并存在显著校准分歧，故量化结论的稳健性有限，结论更宜作为结构性模式证据而非精确度量。

Abstract: AI governance programmes increasingly rely on natural language prompts to constrain and direct AI agent behaviour. These prompts function as executable specifications: they define the agent's mandate, scope, and quality criteria. Despite this role, no systematic framework exists for evaluating whether a governance prompt is structurally complete. We introduce a five-principle evaluation framework grounded in computability theory, proof theory, and Bayesian epistemology, and apply it to an empirical corpus of 34 publicly available AGENTS.md governance files sourced from GitHub. Our evaluation reveals that 37% of evaluated file-model pairs score below the structural completeness threshold, with data classification and assessment rubric criteria most frequently absent. These results suggest that practitioner-authored governance prompts exhibit consistent structural patterns that automated static analysis could detect and remediate. We discuss implications for requirements engineering practice in AI-assisted development contexts, identify a previously undocumented artefact classification gap in the AGENTS.md convention, and propose directions for tool support.

</details>


### [235] [A Ground-Truth-Based Evaluation of Vulnerability Detection Across Multiple Ecosystems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21111)
*Peter Mandl,Paul Mandl,Martin Häusl,Maximilian Auch*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本研究对跨多个软件生态系统的自动化漏洞检测工具进行了实证评估，利用OSV数据库构建了映射到具体包版本的高质量真实漏洞数据集。研究开发了开源工具以支持数据集的可重复构建，发现不同漏洞检测系统间存在系统性差异，强调了透明数据集构建对可重复安全研究的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前自动化漏洞检测工具的评估面临挑战，主要由于漏洞数据来源的结构异构性、标识符方案不一致以及版本范围规范的模糊性。这些问题阻碍了对漏洞检测工具的系统性比较和可靠评估。

Method: 基于OSV数据库构建了精心策划的真实漏洞数据集，明确将漏洞映射到具体的软件包版本；开发了开源工具自动从当前OSV数据库重建数据集；跨多个软件生态系统对漏洞检测系统进行系统性比较。

Result: 该评估揭示了不同漏洞检测系统之间存在系统性差异，证明了透明的数据集构建方法对可重复的实证安全研究具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 透明的数据集构建对可重复的实证安全研究至关重要。该研究强调了系统性比较漏洞检测系统的必要性，并为未来的研究和工具评估提供了可复现的方法论基础。

Abstract: Automated vulnerability detection tools are widely used to identify security vulnerabilities in software dependencies. However, the evaluation of such tools remains challenging due to the heterogeneous structure of vulnerability data sources, inconsistent identifier schemes, and ambiguities in version range specifications. In this paper, we present an empirical evaluation of vulnerability detection across multiple software ecosystems using a curated ground-truth dataset derived from the Open Source Vulnerabilities (OSV) database. The dataset explicitly maps vulnerabilities to concrete package versions and enables a systematic comparison of detection results across different tools and services. Since vulnerability databases such as OSV are continuously updated, the dataset used in this study represents a snapshot of the vulnerability landscape at the time of the evaluation. To support reproducibility and future studies, we provide an open-source tool that automatically reconstructs the dataset from the current OSV database using the methodology described in this paper. Our evaluation highlights systematic differences between vulnerability detection systems and demonstrates the importance of transparent dataset construction for reproducible empirical security research.

</details>


### [236] [Hidden Dependencies and Component Variants in SBOM-Based Software Composition Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21278)
*Shawn Rasheed,Max McPhee,Lisa Patterson,Stephen MacDonell,Jens Dietrich*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文研究了SBOM中两类不匹配问题：隐藏的代码级依赖和组件克隆变体，并证明这些问题会导致主流漏洞扫描器产生不一致的漏洞报告和VEX声明处理结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件供应链攻击日益增多，SBOM作为漏洞管理工具的有效性依赖于其对组件身份和实际依赖关系的准确表示。

Method: 通过实验对比，识别了两类不匹配模式（隐藏的代码级依赖和组件克隆变体），并测试了主流SBOM漏洞扫描器在处理这些不匹配时的表现。

Result: 这些不匹配模式导致了漏洞报告的不一致性和VEX声明处理的不一致，暴露了现有SBOM扫描器的局限性。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前SBOM生产和消费中的局限性，呼吁采用更丰富的依赖表示和组件身份识别方案。

Abstract: Software Bills of Material (SBOMs) have emerged as an important technology for vulnerability management amid rising supply-chain attacks. They represent component relationships within a software product and support software composition analysis (SCA) by linking components to known vulnerabilities. However, the effectiveness of SBOM-based analysis depends on how accurately SBOMs represent component identities and actual dependencies in software. This paper studies two mismatch patterns: hidden code-level dependencies that are not represented as component-level dependencies, and component variants (clones) that cannot be identified consistently by scanners. We show that these mismatches can lead to inconsistent vulnerability reporting and inconsistent handling of VEX statements across popular SBOM-based vulnerability scanners. These results highlight limitations in current SBOM production and consumption and motivate richer dependency representation and component identity.

</details>


### [237] [Conjecture and Inquiry: Quantifying Software Performance Requirements via Interactive Retrieval-Augmented Preference Elicitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21380)
*Wang Shi Hai,Chen Tao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出IRAP方法，通过交互式检索增强偏好获取，将自然语言描述的软件性能需求量化为数学函数。实验表明该方法在四个真实数据集上显著优于10种最先进方法，交互轮次仅需5轮即可达到最高40倍提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件性能需求以自然语言文档化，将其量化为数学形式至关重要。然而需求中的模糊性及人类认知的不确定性导致高度不确定的歧义解释，使得自动化量化成为未解决且具有挑战性的问题。

Method: 提出IRAP方法，通过交互式检索增强偏好获取（interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation）将性能需求量化为数学函数。该方法显式地从问题特定知识中检索和推理偏好，同时引导与利益相关者的渐进交互，降低认知负担。

Result: 在4个真实数据集上针对10种最先进方法进行实验，IRAP在所有情况下均表现出优越性，交互轮次少至5轮即可实现最高40倍提升。

Conclusion: IRAP通过融合领域知识检索与交互式偏好获取，有效解决了性能需求自动量化问题，验证了方法在精度和效率上的显著优势。

Abstract: Since software performance requirements are documented in natural language, quantifying them into mathematical forms is essential for software engineering. Yet, the vagueness in performance requirements and uncertainty of human cognition have caused highly uncertain ambiguity in the interpretations, rendering their automated quantification an unaddressed and challenging problem. In this paper, we formalize the problem and propose IRAP, an approach that quantifies performance requirements into mathematical functions via interactive retrieval-augmented preference elicitation. IRAP differs from the others in that it explicitly derives from problem-specific knowledge to retrieve and reason the preferences, which also guides the progressive interaction with stakeholders, while reducing the cognitive overhead. Experiment results against 10 state-of-the-art methods on four real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority of IRAP on all cases with up to 40x improvements under as few as five rounds of interactions.

</details>


### [238] [Assessing the Impact of Requirement Ambiguity on LLM-based Function-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21505)
*Di Yang,Xinou Xie,Xiuwen Yang,Ming Hu,Yihao Huang,Yueling Zhang,Weikai Miao,Ting Su,Chengcheng Wan,Geguang Pu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了Orchid，首个专门针对模糊需求的代码生成基准，包含1,304个涵盖四种模糊类型的任务。通过系统性实验发现：模糊性会降低所有LLM性能，高级模型受影更大；LLM对同一模糊需求产生不同实现，且无法自主识别和解决模糊性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实软件开发中存在普遍的需求模糊性问题，源于自然语言的固有模糊性和利益相关者的不同解读。然而现有基准通常假设需求清晰明确，缺乏对LLM在真实模糊需求下表现的实证研究。LLM虽能从精确规范生成代码，但需求模糊性成为可靠自动代码生成的主要障碍。

Method: 构建了一个包含1,304个函数级任务的代码生成基准，覆盖四种模糊性类型（词汇模糊、句法模糊、语义模糊和模糊性）；在此基础上对LLM进行了系统性实证评估。

Result: 模糊性会一致性地降低所有被评估LLM的性能，高级模型受影响尤为显著；LLM对于相同的模糊需求常产生功能不同的实现；LLM缺乏自主识别或解决模糊性的能力。

Conclusion: Orchid基准揭示了清晰需求与模糊需求之间存在显著的性能差距，强调了下一代自动化软件工程工具亟需开发能够感知和处理需求模糊性的技术。

Abstract: Software requirement ambiguity is ubiquitous in real-world development, stemming from the inherent imprecision of natural language and the varying interpretations of stakeholders. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generating code from precise specifications, such ambiguity poses a significant obstacle to reliable automated code generation. Existing benchmarks typically assume clear and unambiguous requirements, leaving an empirical gap in understanding how LLMs behave when faced with the inherent uncertainty of real-world software requirements. In this paper, we introduce Orchid, the first code generation benchmark specifically designed with ambiguous requirements. It comprises 1,304 function-level tasks covering four distinct types of ambiguity: lexical, syntactic, semantic, and vagueness. Leveraging this dataset, we conduct the first systematic empirical study to evaluate the impact of requirement ambiguity on LLM-based code generation. Our results demonstrate that ambiguity consistently degrades the performance of all evaluated LLMs, with the most pronounced negative effects observed in highly advanced models. Furthermore, we observe that LLMs frequently produce functionally divergent implementations for the same ambiguous requirement and lack the capability to identify or resolve such ambiguity autonomously. These findings reveal a significant performance gap between clear and ambiguous requirements, underscoring the urgent need for ambiguity-aware techniques in the next generation of automated software engineering tools. The Orchid benchmark is publicly available at https://huggingface.co/datasets/SII-YDD/Orchid.

</details>


### [239] [SpecSyn: LLM-based Synthesis and Refinement of Formal Specifications for Real-world Program Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21570)
*Lezhi Ma,Shangqing Liu,Yi Li,Qiong Wu,Han Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SpecSyn是一种基于大语言模型(LLM)的程序规格自动生成方法，通过程序分段和基于语义不等价变异的规格增强机制，解决了现有方法在处理大型程序和规格强度评估方面的不足，在真实程序验证任务中表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM驱动的规格生成方法存在两大局限：缺乏处理大规模输入程序的有效策略，以及缺乏评估和保证生成规格强度的机制。这些局限阻碍了其在真实程序验证任务中的适用性。

Method: SpecSyn首先将输入程序分解为独立片段，随后通过迭代规格生成过程分别处理；创新性地引入基于语义非等价程序变异和变体判别的规格细化机制，以评估和增强生成规格的语义强度。

Result: 实验表明SpecSyn保持超过90%的高精确率和超过75%的出色召回率，显著优于现有LLB方法。进一步评估中，SpecSyn成功处理了1365个目标属性中的1071个。

Conclusion: SpecSyn通过程序分段和语义增强机制，有效解决了LLM规格生成在真实程序验证中的适用性问题，为自动化程序验证提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Program verification is a formal technique to rigorously ensure the correctness and fault-freeness of software systems. However, constructing comprehensive interprocedural specifications for full verification obligations is time-consuming and labor-intensive, giving rise to automated specification generation approaches. Despite the significant advancements in these approaches brought by Large Language Models (LLMs), existing LLM-empowered approaches still suffer from significant limitations: they lack effective strategies for handling sizable input programs, and are typically equipped with no mechanisms to evaluate and guarantee the strength of the generated specifications. The limitations impair their ability to extract precise specifications from real-world complicated programs to support the verification of target properties, thereby hindering the applicability of existing approaches in verification tasks on real-world programs. To remedy this gap, we propose SpecSyn, a novel LLM-based specification generation method. SpecSyn first decomposes the input program into individual segments, which are handled respectively by the subsequent iterative specification generation process. Innovatively, we incorporate into the process a specification refinement mechanism based on semantic-non-equivalent program mutations and variant discrimination, assessing and enhancing the semantic strength of the generated specifications. Extensive experiments show that SpecSyn maintains high precision over 90% and outstanding recall over 75%, significantly outperforming existing LLM-based approaches. In further evaluations, SpecSyn successfully handles 1071 out of 1365 target properties for open-source programs, proving its applicability on real-world program verification tasks.

</details>


### [240] [A Metamorphic Testing Approach to Diagnosing Memorization in LLM-Based Program Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21579)
*Milan De Koning,Ali Asgari,Pouria Derakhshanfar,Annibale Panichella*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文通过结合 metamorphic testing 与 negative log-likelihood (NLL) 来更好地揭示LLM-based自动程序修复中的数据泄露问题。对Defects4J和GitBug-Java应用语义保持变换构建变体基准，评估7个LLM后发现所有模型在变换后基准上的成功率显著下降（-4.1%至-15.98%），且下降程度与NLL强相关，证明联合方法能提供更强可靠的数据泄露证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可能记忆预训练数据中的bug修复，导致评估基准与预训练数据重叠时产生数据泄露，造成性能估计膨胀。需要更好的方法来揭示数据泄露问题。

Method: 对Defects4J和GitBug-Java两个数据集应用语义保持变换构建变体基准测试，评估7个LLM在原始版本和变换版本上的修复成功率，分析性能下降与NLL之间的关系。

Result: 所有评估的最先进LLM在变换后的基准测试上都表现出显著的补丁生成成功率下降（从GPT-4o的-4.1%到Llama-3.1的-15.98%不等），且性能下降与原始基准测试上的NLL强相关，表明模型在更可能记忆的实例上表现更好。

Conclusion: 结合MT与NLL提供了更强且更可靠的数据泄露证据，同时 metamorphic testing 可帮助缓解LLM-based APR评估中的数据泄露问题。

Abstract: LLM-based automated program repair (APR) techniques have shown promising results in reducing debugging costs. However, prior results can be affected by data leakage: large language models (LLMs) may memorize bug fixes when evaluation benchmarks overlap with their pretraining data, leading to inflated performance estimates. In this paper, we investigate whether we can better reveal data leakage by combining metamorphic testing (MT) with negative log-likelihood (NLL), which has been used in prior work as a proxy for memorization. We construct variant benchmarks by applying semantics-preserving transformations to two widely used datasets, Defects4J and GitBug-Java. Using these benchmarks, we evaluate the repair success rates of seven LLMs on both original and transformed versions, and analyze the relationship between performance degradation and NLL. Our results show that all evaluated state-of-the-art LLMs exhibit substantial drops in patch generation success rates on transformed benchmarks, ranging from -4.1% for GPT-4o to -15.98% for Llama-3.1. Furthermore, we find that this degradation strongly correlates with NLL on the original benchmarks, suggesting that models perform better on instances they are more likely to have memorized. These findings show that combining MT with NLL provides stronger and more reliable evidence of data leakage, while metamorphic testing alone can help mitigate its effects in LLM-based APR evaluations.

</details>


### [241] [DryRUN: On the Role of Public Tests in LLM-Driven Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21598)
*Kaushitha Silva,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出DryRUN框架，通过让大型语言模型自主生成输入样本并模拟执行轨迹来替代传统依赖人工标注测试用例的代码生成方法，解决现有方法在无公开测试用例场景下的局限性，并在LiveCodeBench v6数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码生成框架依赖人工提供的公开测试用例进行调试和仿真，导致两个核心问题：(1) 人工标注测试用例耗时费力，构成软件开发生命周期中的瓶颈；(2) 过度依赖公开测试导致"过度自信差距"，模型在隐藏测试上表现不佳。这限制了现有方法仅适用于精心策划的竞赛编程基准测试，难以应用于真实软件工程场景。

Method: 提出DryRUN框架，核心创新包括：(1) 允许LLM自主生成有效输入样本；(2) 模拟执行轨迹进行自纠正；(3) 迭代式规划-生成-执行循环。关键洞见是外部输入样本并非代码生成的必要条件。

Result: 在LiveCodeBench v6数据集（2025年3月之后）上的评估表明，DryRUN在完全不使用公开测试用例或外部执行反馈的情况下，达到了与依赖公开测试的SOTA框架CodeSIM相当的性能，同时降低了输出token消耗。

Conclusion: DryRUN成功证明了LLM可自主生成有效输入并通过执行模拟实现自纠正，为无人工标注测试用例场景下的代码生成提供了可行方案。降低token消耗的特点使其具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Multi-agent frameworks are widely used in autonomous code generation and have applications in complex algorithmic problem-solving. Recent work has addressed the challenge of generating functionally correct code by incorporating simulation-driven planning and debugging, where language models trace execution steps to verify logic. However, these approaches depend on human-provided public test cases to ground the debugging and simulation loop. Manually authoring comprehensive input-output examples is a labor-intensive bottleneck in the software development lifecycle. Because ground-truth input-output examples are rarely available prior to implementation in real-world software engineering, this dependency restricts methods to curated competitive programming benchmarks. Furthermore, we identify that reliance on these public tests induces an ``overconfidence gap,'' causing frameworks to overfit to simplistic examples and fail on hidden evaluations. In contrast, we observe that external sample inputs are not strictly necessary for code generation. We demonstrate that large language models can autonomously generate valid inputs and simulate execution traces to self-correct. Consequently, we develop DryRUN, a framework that eliminates the need for ground-truth samples by allowing the LLM to iteratively plan, autonomously generate its own inputs and simulate execution, mitigating algorithmic overconfidence. Evaluations on the LiveCodeBench v6 dataset (post-March 2025) demonstrate that DryRUN matches performance against CodeSIM, a state-of-the-art and public-test-dependent framework, while operating entirely without public test cases or external execution feedback while reducing output token consumption.

</details>


### [242] [Verifying Machine Learning Interpretability Requirements through Provenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21599)
*Lynn Vonderhaar,Juan Couder,Daryela Cisneros,Omar Ochoa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出利用ML溯源数据验证机器学习可解释性非功能需求的方法。通过保存模型和数据溯源信息，将难以测量的可解释性需求转化为可量化的功能需求进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML工程领域面临重大挑战：虽然现有文献定义了ML的可解释性要求，但可解释性本质上是一个不可测量的需求，导致无法明确确认模型是否满足其可解释性要求，这使得NFR验证成为难题。

Method: 提出利用ML溯源数据验证可解释性需求的方法，指导ML工程师保存各类模型和数据溯源信息，使模型行为透明可解释。这些溯源数据构成可量化的功能需求(FRs)，通过验证这些FRs来间接验证可解释性NFR。

Result: 通过保存模型和数据的溯源信息，将可解释性这一NFR转化为可验证的量化功能需求，建立起可解释性验证的基础框架。

Conclusion: 该研究贡献了一种验证ML模型可解释性NFR的方法，通过保存模型和数据溯源数据，将可解释性从不可测量的需求转化为可量化验证的功能需求。

Abstract: Machine Learning (ML) Engineering is a growing field that necessitates an increase in the rigor of ML development. It draws many ideas from software engineering and more specifically, from requirements engineering. Existing literature on ML Engineering defines quality models and Non-Functional Requirements (NFRs) specific to ML, in particular interpretability being one such NFR. However, a major challenge occurs in verifying ML NFRs, including interpretability. Although existing literature defines interpretability in terms of ML, it remains an immeasurable requirement, making it impossible to definitively confirm whether a model meets its interpretability requirement. This paper shows how ML provenance can be used to verify ML interpretability requirements. This work provides an approach for how ML engineers can save various types of model and data provenance to make the model's behavior transparent and interpretable. Saving this data forms the basis of quantifiable Functional Requirements (FRs) whose verification in turn verifies the interpretability NFR. Ultimately, this paper contributes a method to verify interpretability NFRs for ML models.

</details>


### [243] [Can Large Language Models Assist the Comprehension of ROS2 Software Architectures?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21699)
*Laura Duits,Bouazza El Moutaouakil,Ivano Malavolta*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究评估了9种大语言模型在帮助理解ROS2机器人软件系统架构方面的能力，通过1230个问题测试发现平均准确率达98.22%，Gemini-2.5-pro表现最佳(100%准确率)，表明LLM在辅助ROS2架构理解方面具有巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROS2是机器人软件开发中最常用的框架，其架构高度复杂，包含数千个去中心化通信的组件。开发者理解这些复杂系统的架构信息面临挑战，需要探索LLM是否能辅助完成此类任务。

Method: 采用受控实验法：(1)设计通用算法系统生成架构相关问题；(2)对3个规模递增的ROS2系统，向9个LLM发送1230个提示；(3)通过运行和监控ROS2系统建立真值作为评估基准；(4)定性分析LLM提供的解释质量。

Result: 几乎所有问题都被正确回答(平均98.22%)。Gemini-2.5-pro表现最佳(100%准确率)，其次是o3(99.77%)和Gemini-2.5-flash(99.72%)，最差的是GPT-4.1(95%)。1230个问题中仅300个错误，其中249个来自最复杂系统。解释连贯性得分从"服务引用"的0.394到"通信路径"的0.762不等。ChatGPT-4o困惑度最低(19.6)，o4-mini最高(103.6)。

Conclusion: LLM在辅助ROS2开发者理解软件架构的非平凡方面具有巨大潜力，但开发者应意识到LLM的内在局限性和不同表现差异，在使用时需加以考虑。

Abstract: Context. The most used development framework for robotics software is ROS2. ROS2 architectures are highly complex, with thousands of components communicating in a decentralized fashion. Goal. We aim to evaluate how LLMs can assist in the comprehension of factual information about the architecture of ROS2 systems. Method. We conduct a controlled experiment where we administer 1,230 prompts to 9 LLMs containing architecturally-relevant questions about 3 ROS2 systems with incremental size. We provide a generic algorithm that systematically generates architecturally-relevant questions for a ROS2 system. Then, we (i) assess the accuracy of the answers of the LLMs against a ground truth established via running and monitoring the 3 ROS2 systems and (ii) qualitatively analyse the explanations provided by the LLMs. Results. Almost all questions are answered correctly across all LLMs (mean=98.22%). gemini-2.5-pro performs best (100% accuracy across all prompts and systems), followed by o3 (99.77%), and gemini-2.5-flash (99.72%); the least performing LLM is gpt-4.1 (95%). Only 300/1,230 prompts are incorrectly answered, of which 249 are about the most complex system. The coherence scores in LLM's explanations range from 0.394 for "service references" to 0.762 for "communication path". The mean perplexity varies significantly across models, with chatgpt-4o achieving the lowest score (19.6) and o4-mini the highest (103.6). Conclusions. There is great potential in the usage of LLMs to aid ROS2 developers in comprehending non-trivial aspects of the software architecture of their systems. Nevertheless, developers should be aware of the intrinsic limitations and different performances of the LLMs and take those into account when using them.

</details>


### [244] [Automated LTL Specification Generation from Industrial Aerospace Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21715)
*Zhi Ma,Xiao Liang,Cheng Wen,Rui Chen,Bin Gu,Shengchao Qin,Cong Tian,Mengfei Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AeroReq2LTL利用LLM自动将航空领域自然语言需求转换为形式化LTL属性，通过数据字典和模板语言两大创新解决工业需求文档中术语复杂和隐含结构问题，实验达85%精确率/88%召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言需求到形式化LTL属性的翻译高度劳动密集且易出错，需同时具备航空控制工程与形式化方法的稀缺专业知识。现有NL2LTL工具在处理工业实际需求文档时常因复杂领域术语或隐含时间逻辑结构而失败。

Method: 提出AeroReq2LTL框架，利用LLM自动生成LTL属性两大工业创新：(i)数据字典——将技术术语规范化为精确的原子命题；(ii)模板化需求语言——在翻译前显式化时间线索和逻辑关系。

Result: 在真实航空数据集上，AeroReq2LTL达到85%的精确率和88%的召回率，其输出可直接被现有验证工具消费。

Conclusion: AeroReq2LTL成功将LLM应用于航空需求的形式化验证属性生成，通过领域特定的数据字典和模板语言克服了通用NL2LTL工具的局限性，输出的LTL公式可直接用于现有验证工具。

Abstract: In the development and verification of safety-critical aero-space software, Linear Temporal Logic (LTL) has been widely used to specify complex system properties derived from requirements. However, a significant gap remains in industrial practice: translating natural language (NL) requirements into formal LTL properties is a labor-intensive and error-prone process that requires rare expertise in both aerospace control engineering and formal methods. While recent NL-to-LTL tools (e.g., NL2SPEC, NL2TL, NL2LTL) are capable of automating parts of this process, they often fail on real requirement documents in industrial settings, due to complex domain terminology or implicit temporal and logical structure. To address these challenges, we present AeroReq2LTL, a framework that automates LTL property generation for aerospace requirements using large language models (LLMs), with two key industrial innovations: (i) a data dictionary that normalizes technical jargon into precise atomic propositions; and (ii) a template-based requirement language that makes temporal cues and logical relations explicit before translation. On a real aerospace dataset, AeroReq2LTL achieves 85% precision and 88% recall in LTL generation, and its outputs can be directly consumed by existing verification tools.

</details>


### [245] [Agentic AI-assisted coding offers a unique opportunity to instill epistemic grounding during software development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21744)
*Magnus Palmblad,Jared M. Ragland,Benjamin A. Neely*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出GROUNDING.md，一种领域限定的认知锚定文档，以质谱蛋白质组学为示例，通过编码硬约束（非协商的有效性不变量）和约定参数（社区共识默认值），确保AI生成的代码在科学有效性层面符合领域标准，使非领域专家也能生成包含最佳实践的正确代码和软件工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI辅助编码从聊天式 vibe coding 演进到基于智能体脚手架的 agentic 软件开发，如何确保非领域专家借助AI生成的代码、工具和软件能够满足领域特定的科学正确性标准成为一个关键挑战。当前趋势是利用计划文档之外的项目和方法范围文档，但这些文档仍不足以强制执行科学有效性约束。

Method: 提出一种社区治理的、领域限定的显式认知锚定文档——GROUNDING.md。该文档将领域知识编码为两层结构：(1) Hard Constraints（硬约束）：基于经验验证的非协商性有效性不变量，用于强制科学正确性；(2) Convention Parameters（约定参数）：社区共识的默认值。这些约束优先于所有其他上下文强制执行，通过AI智能体遵循指南比人类更容易的特性，确保代码生成在底层就内置最佳实践。

Result: GROUNDING.md框架使非领域专家能够生成在科学有效性层面可靠的代码、工具和软件，同时为软件开发者、代码审查者和使用者提供信心保障。该方法利用AI智能体的可引导性优势，为组织提供一种将领域专家保持在工作流程中的机制，以应对民主化定制软件生成时代的挑战。

Conclusion: 通过领域限定的认知锚定文档，AI辅助编程可以在保持科学有效性的同时实现软件生成的民主化。社区治理模式确保文档随领域发展而更新，智能体架构使得约束执行比人工遵守更可靠，为未来定制软件解决方案的生成提供了质量保障框架。

Abstract: The capabilities of AI-assisted coding are progressing at breakneck speed. Chat-based vibe coding has evolved into fully fledged AI-assisted, agentic software development using agent scaffolds where the human developer creates a plan that agentic AIs implement. One current trend is utilizing documents beyond this plan document, such as project and method-scoped documents. Here we propose GROUNDING.md, a community-governed, field-scoped epistemic grounding document, using mass spectrometry-based proteomics as an example. This explicit field-scoped grounding document encodes Hard Constraints (non-negotiable validity invariants empirically required for scientific correctness) and Convention Parameters (community-agreed defaults) that override all other contexts to enforce validity, regardless of what the user prompts. In practice, this will empower a non-domain expert to generate code, tools, and software that have best practices baked in at the ground level, providing confidence to the software developer but also to those reviewing or using the final product. Undoubtedly it is easier to have agentic AIs adhere to guidelines than humans, and this opportunity allows for organizations to develop epistemic grounding documents in such a way as to keep domain experts in the loop in a future of democratized generation of bespoke software solutions.

</details>


### [246] [Less Is More: Measuring How LLM Involvement affects Chatbot Accuracy in Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21746)
*Krishna Narasimhan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文将“LLM参与程度”当作独立自变量，对“自然语言→Joern CPGQL查询”三种架构做受控对比：直接生成（A1）、模式约束的JSON中间表示（A2）、工具增强的智能体生成（A3）。结论是“少即是好”——结构化中间表示（A2）在全部四个开源模型上结果匹配率最高，在大模型上比直接生成高15–25个百分点，并显著优于消耗约8倍token的智能体方案；智能体所解任务集合是A2解出任务的严格子集，无任何互补覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态分析的能力并不缺，瓶颈在可用性：Joern 的 CPGQL 是带 Scala 风格方法链、图遍历语义与陌生算子名的冷门 DSL，训练语料中极少出现，开发者难以手写查询。LLM 具备把自然语言意图“近似翻译”为结构化产物的能力，但它是随机近似器而非忠实翻译器，在欠表示的形式化语言上会产出语法合法却语义错误的输出。已有系统（IRIS 仅让LLM做污点源/汇分类、QLCoder 允许智能体自由编译运行、MoCQ 压缩CodeQL子集）在“把多少工作委托给LLM”上各取一点，却无人把委托程度作为值得研究的变量。此外 Anand et al. (2024) 的注意力/隐藏表示分析显示，代码LLM只编码语法token内部与标识符内部的关系，无法可靠编码二者之间的跨类别关系——而这正是生成分析查询所必需的能力。因此本文提出的问题不是“要不要用LLM”，而是“应给LLM多大自由度”：输出空间约束得越紧、越良类型，出错空间越小。

Method: （1）基准：自建20个自然语言→CPGQL任务，按复杂度分三档——结构查询7个（单次AST/类型层级遍历）、数据流查询7个（reachableBy/reachableByFlows）、复合查询6个（控制流+数据流联合推理）；目标为两个真实Java项目 Apache Commons Lang（9题）与 OWASP WebGoat（11题）；20条ground truth均人工编写并经 Joern 4.0.488 执行验证，基准内容与提示示例均不进入提示词（示例取自官方文档）以防泄漏。
（2）三架构：①A1 直接生成——LLM在CPGQL语法参考、Joern约定与文档示例辅助下直接产出CPGQL字符串，执行失败则把错误回灌重试（≤3次）；②A2 结构化中间——LLM只输出符合固定模式的JSON（5类查询类型、10种输出列、3类流端点），由手写、带22个单元测试的确定性 mapper 转成CPGQL，LLM完全不见查询语法；③A3 工具增强智能体——通过HuggingFace function calling 暴露 find_methods/find_calls/trace_data_flow/find_reachable_by/run_custom_query 五个工具，按ReAct循环最多10步、输出文本答案。
（3）模型与设计：4个开源权重模型，2×2设计（Llama 3.3 70B、Qwen 2.5 72B；Llama 3.1 8B、Qwen 2.5 7B），temperature 0、随机种子42/43/44三次重复，每模型×架构60次试验，共720次（有效660次，A3的Llama 70B因HuggingFace限流与工具调用解析失败排除）；三架构共享统一重试预算以隔离架构变量。
（4）指标：结果匹配率（与ground truth在同一CPG上执行后归一化比对，最主指标）、精确字符串匹配（仅A1/A2适用）、总token消耗、LLM调用次数、执行成功率（各架构定义不同，A3仅表示是否产出最终答案）。

Result: ①A2在每个模型上均取得最高结果匹配率，排序恒为 A2 > A1 > A3。大模型增益显著：Qwen 72B 58.3% vs A1 43.3%（+15.0pp）、Llama 70B 55.0% vs 30.0%（+25.0pp）；小模型仅+3.3pp（Qwen 7B 35.0% vs 31.7%）与+5.0pp（Llama 8B 35.0% vs 30.0%）。
②模型规模与架构存在交互：大模型在A2上执行成功率100%（每次输出都是可映射的合法JSON）；小模型执行成功率骤降（Qwen 7B 65.0%、Llama 8B 53.3%），35–47%的输出在JSON解析或模式校验阶段即失败，形成天花板——架构本身正确但模型无法稳定产出受约束输出。相反A1在小模型上执行成功率近乎100%，因为Joern的“可运行”门槛低于“符合类型化模式”门槛，故小模型写出能跑但结果错的CPGQL。
③A3表现最差且成本最高：Qwen 72B 25.0%、Qwen 7B 15.0%、Llama 8B 15.0%；在Qwen 72B上比A2低33pp、平均token消耗为A2的8倍（中位数4倍，分布跨度14倍：3081–42790，IQR 4768–21883超过A1+A2全距）、平均延迟32.1秒 vs A1/A2的5秒内，且8/60次试验触顶10步未收敛。失败模式有三类：选中部分相关的大结果集后推理错误、陷入探索性循环（为收集上下文而非作答）、拿到正确中间结果却在组装最终答案时误读/重排。
④无互补覆盖：Qwen 72B上A2至少解出20题中的13题，A3只解出其中6题且不含任何额外题目；Qwen 7B与Llama 8B上A2各解7题、A3各解其3题；另有6题（C02、C05、D02、D07、S03、S05）三架构全部失败，涉及冷门CPGQL算子或多跳推理。该严格子集关系在全部有干净数据的模型上成立，故组合系统无法为高成本提供理由。
⑤分层难度因架构而异（Qwen 72B）：A1下复合题(44.4%)反高于纯数据流(33.3%)，说明结构成分提供了锚定；A2下数据流题最易(71.4%)，因模式显式刻画了流起点/终点，任务退化为分类，而复合题最难(44.4%)，反映多字段联合指定的困难；A3在小模型上只能解结构题（Qwen 7B结构42.9%，数据流与复合均0%），说明多步工具调用在需要跨调用协调信息时崩溃。
⑥方法论结论：精确字符串匹配低估真实正确性——Qwen 72B在A1上精确匹配26.7%而结果匹配43.3%，差距16.6pp，因LLM常写出语义等价但路径不同的合法查询；DSL生成评测应执行并比对结果，而非比对字符串。
⑦解释性假说（作者明示未做实验隔离）：A2把任务从“代码生成”转为“分类/抽取”，避开Anand et al. 揭示的跨类别关系编码缺陷；其增益量级与MoCQ（削减DSL表面）、IRIS（限制为分类）所得相当，三方独立系统得到同一架构原则，相互佐证。

Conclusion: 在形式化结构良好的领域（模式化查询语言、确定性执行），应把LLM的职责压缩为自然语言理解与“填充良类型模式”，把查询构造交给确定性代码：A2以极低token成本取得最高正确率，并优于无约束生成与迭代式工具使用。智能体式工具编排不仅更差、更贵，还无互补覆盖，其原因被归因于逐步误差累积（平均4.8步、即便单步90%准确率也仅约59%端到端成功率）与过度自主带来的“探索不收敛”。该原则不限于CPGQL：任何具备形式模式、类型化查询语言与确定性执行能力的领域都可采用同一分解。其关键前提是“有效查询空间能被足够紧凑的模式刻画”，此前提并非在所有模型规模下都成立——小模型的瓶颈从查询正确性转移为模式合规性，可通过guided decoding/受约束生成在token层面强制JSON模式合规来缓解（列为未来工作）。有效性威胁亦被明确讨论：基准由作者自建（仅20题）、A2的mapper为手写故其覆盖率定义了A2表达上限、A3因HuggingFace解析器而非MCP实现导致Llama 70B数据缺失且A3结果比对本身噪声更大、A3与A1/A2执行成功率定义不可直接比较、全部实验temperature=0。基准与全部实验数据（660次试验输出、mapper与测试套件、复现脚本）已公开以支持复现与扩展。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to make static analysis tools accessible through natural language, yet existing systems differ in how much they delegate to the LLM without treating the degree of delegation as an independent variable. We compare three architectures along a spectrum of LLM involvement for translating natural language to Joern's query language \cpgql{}: direct query generation (\approach{1}), generation of a schema-constrained JSON intermediate representation (\approach{2}), and tool-augmented agentic generation (\approach{3}). These are evaluated on a benchmark of 20 code analysis tasks across three complexity tiers, using four open-weight models in a 2\(\times\)2 design (two model families \(\times\) two scales), each with three repetitions. The structured intermediate representation (\approach{2}) achieves the highest result match rates, outperforming direct generation by 15--25 percentage points on large models and surpassing the agentic approach despite the latter consuming 8\(\times\) more tokens. The benefit of structured intermediates is most pronounced for large models; for small models, schema compliance becomes the bottleneck. These findings suggest that in formally structured domains, constraining the LLM's output to a well-typed intermediate representation and delegating query construction to deterministic code yields better results than either unconstrained generation or iterative tool use.

</details>


### [247] [Generalizing Test Cases for Comprehensive Test Scenario Coverage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.21771)
*Binhang Qi,Yun Lin,Xinyi Weng,Chenyan Liu,Hailong Sun,Gordon Fraser,Jin Song Dong*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TestGeneralizer是一个从单一初始测试自动生成全面测试用例的框架，通过理解隐式需求、生成场景模板并实例化，最后生成可执行测试。实验在12个Java项目中相比ChatTester提升31.66%（突变覆盖）和23.08%（LLM评估）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动化测试生成过度关注代码覆盖率，忽视源自隐式需求的测试场景；开发者编写的测试虽反映需求但覆盖有限，导致重要测试常被延迟添加。

Method: TestGeneralizer采用三阶段流程：(1)增强需求和场景理解；(2)生成场景模板并实例化；(3)生成并精炼可执行测试用例。在12个开源Java项目上评估。

Result: 相比ChatTester，突变测试场景覆盖率提升31.66%，LLM评估场景覆盖率提升23.08%。

Conclusion: 通过利用初始测试揭示隐式需求，TestGeneralizer能有效生成更全面的测试场景覆盖，验证了需求导向测试生成的可行性。

Abstract: Test cases are essential for software development and maintenance. In practice, developers derive multiple test cases from an implicit pattern based on their understanding of requirements and inference of diverse test scenarios, each validating a specific behavior of the focal method. However, producing comprehensive tests is time-consuming and error-prone: many important tests that should have accompanied the initial test are added only after a significant delay, sometimes only after bugs are triggered. Existing automated test generation techniques largely focus on code coverage. Yet in real projects, practical tests are seldom driven by code coverage alone, since test scenarios do not necessarily align with control-flow branches. Instead, test scenarios originate from requirements, which are often undocumented and implicitly embedded in a project's design and implementation. However, developer-written tests are frequently treated as executable specifications; thus, even a single initial test that reflects the developer's intent can reveal the underlying requirement and the diverse scenarios that should be validated. In this work, we propose TestGeneralizer, a framework for generalizing test cases to comprehensively cover test scenarios. TestGeneralizer orchestrates three stages: (1) enhancing the understanding of the requirement and scenario behind the focal method and initial test; (2) generating a test scenario template and crystallizing it into various test scenario instances; and (3) generating and refining executable test cases from these instances. We evaluate TestGeneralizer against three state-of-the-art baselines on 12 open-source Java projects. TestGeneralizer achieves significant improvements: +31.66% and +23.08% over ChatTester, in mutation-based and LLM-assessed scenario coverage, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.CY'></div>

# cs.CY [[Back]](#toc)

### [248] [Votiverse: A Configurable Governance Platform for Democratic Decision-Making](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20863)
*Diego Macrini*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: Votiverse is an open-source governance platform that formalizes and implements liquid democracy, featuring transitive/revocable topic-specific delegations, a governance awareness layer for decision context, and a prediction-tracking accountability system. It treats traditional representative democracy as one specific configuration rather than a default, enabling organizations to explore the full spectrum of democratic possibilities.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing democratic systems occupy a narrow, largely unquestioned band of the possible governance spectrum, chosen under historical constraints that no longer apply. There is a need for configurable governance mechanisms that go beyond the binary of direct participation or fixed-term representation, particularly to address known failure modes of both pure direct democracy and traditional representative democracy.

Method: The authors formalized a governance model based on liquid democracy with three core innovations: (1) a transitive, revocable, topic-specific delegation mechanism where direct votes always override delegations; (2) a governance awareness layer providing real-time contextual reporting on the delegation network; and (3) a prediction-tracking accountability layer that records proposals, promises, and actual outcomes. The platform architecture was implemented as open-source software for organizations of any size to deploy.

Result: The paper provides a complete formalization of the Votiverse governance model, positions it within existing literature on liquid democracy and participatory decision-making, addresses known failure modes, and delivers a working open-source implementation that enables organizations to explore alternative governance configurations.

Conclusion: Votiverse demonstrates that flexible, technology-mediated governance is feasible and can be formally modeled. By treating traditional representative democracy as a specific configuration rather than a default, it opens the design space for governance innovation. The open-source implementation validates the theoretical framework and provides a working platform for communities to experiment with alternative democratic structures.

Abstract: Democracy is not a single mechanism. It is a space of possible configurations -- a spectrum stretching from pure direct participation to full delegation of authority. The systems we live under today occupy a narrow band of that spectrum, chosen centuries ago under constraints that no longer apply, and rarely questioned since. Votiverse is a platform for exploring the rest of that space. It provides organizations, communities, and institutions of any size with a configurable governance engine. Participants can vote directly, delegate their vote to trusted individuals by topic, or operate under any hybrid arrangement their group defines. Delegations are revocable, topic-specific, and transitive. A direct vote always overrides a delegation. In this model, traditional representative democracy is not the norm -- it is an edge case: the configuration you get when delegation is forced, universal, non-specific, and irrevocable for a fixed term. Votiverse introduces two structural innovations. First, a governance awareness layer -- a built-in system that monitors the delegation network and delivers contextual, progressive-disclosure reporting to participants at the point of decision. Second, a prediction-tracking accountability layer. Proposals carry falsifiable predictions. Outcomes are recorded. Over time, the platform builds a collective memory of what was decided, what was promised, and what actually happened. Together, these layers transform voting from a momentary act into an ongoing process of collective learning. This paper formalizes the governance model, situates it within existing work on liquid democracy and participatory decision-making, addresses known failure modes, and describes the architecture of the platform. The core platform has been implemented and released as open-source software.

</details>


### [249] [Sibling Rivalry in the Ivory Tower: Mass Science, Expanding Scholarly Families, and the Reshaping of Academic Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20864)
*Likun Cao,Jie Hua,James Evans*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究将人口学中的出生顺序概念应用于学术谱系分析，发现同一导师的后续博士生在职业成就上系统性低于早期学生，揭示了认知刺激减少和学术生态位狭窄是主要机制，为理解大众科学时代的科学分层提供了新框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要依赖马太效应框架解释科学分层，但在大众科学和团队研究主导的时代该框架日益受限；尽管科学家共享机构和学术传承，但职业成就差异仍存在大量未解释的变异，因此需要引入人口学视角的新分析框架。

Method: 采用人口学概念（出生顺序）应用于学术谱系分析，基于超过100万名美国博士毕业生的数据，检验同一导师指导序列对学生职业成就的影响，并通过机制分析验证认知刺激和学术生态位两个关键路径。

Result: 发现同门后来学生在多个成就维度上系统性地低于早期学生；机制分析表明，尽管导师对所有学生投入资源相当，但后来学生获得的成熟学者认知刺激较少，且在同辈分化压力下倾向于更狭窄的学术生态位，这两个因素共同限制了其智力发展和后续成功。

Conclusion: 该研究通过引入人口学框架，为理解科学分层提供了新视角，揭示了学术谱系中的"出生顺序效应"如何系统性地影响职业成就，为科学社会学和更广泛的社会认知系统分析开辟了新路径。

Abstract: This paper investigates mechanisms underlying scientific stratification in the transition from elite to mass science. Existing scholarship has examined stratification through the Matthew effect framework, but this approach is increasingly limited as mass, team-based research becomes dominant. While scientists now share institutions and lineages, substantial career outcome differences remain unexplained. We propose integrating demographic concepts into science studies. Drawing parallels between biological families and scholarly lineages as fundamental reproductive units, we adapt the birth order concept to examine how doctoral student sequence within a lineage shapes career trajectories. Using data on over one million U.S. doctoral graduates, we find that later students of the same advisor systematically underperform earlier ones across multiple achievement dimensions, both short and long term. Examining underlying mechanisms reveals that although advisors invest comparable resources in all students, later students receive less cognitive stimulation from mature scholars than peers and specialize in narrower niches under peer differentiation pressure. Both of these factors constrain intellectual development and subsequent success. By introducing a demographic framework, this paper offers new perspectives on scientific stratification and demonstrates how demographic concepts can fruitfully analyze broader social and epistemic systems.

</details>


### [250] [Advances in Art: Orthogonal Disruption and the Beauty in Schematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20865)
*Sergio Alvarez-Telena,Marta Diez-Fernandez*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出"正交艺术"(Orthogonal Art)这一新兴艺术学科，旨在与人工智能形成辩证对立而非为其服务，以技术原理图为核心创作媒介，同时具有跨学科教育功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人工智能技术快速发展并渗透艺术创作领域，现有研究多聚焦于AI辅助增强型创意实践。本文作者认为需要一种与AI在生成空间和概念空间上正交（即不相交、不重叠）的艺术实践，以回应而非服务AI技术革命。

Method: 1) 提出正交艺术的理论框架，定义其本质特征；2) 以技术原理图作为主要创作媒介进行实例化实践；3) 将艺术实践与原理图逻辑、算法结构相结合，建立人文科学与工程、哲学的跨学科桥梁。

Result: 建立了正交艺术的理论框架，并提供技术原理图艺术实践的具体示范。

Conclusion: 正交艺术作为AI时代的新兴艺术学科，具有双重价值：一方面在创作层面占据AI无法触及的空间，另一方面在教育层面为理解增强机器系统提供可及的入门途径，实现艺术、工程与哲学的跨学科素养整合。

Abstract: This paper introduces Orthogonal Art, a proposed artistic discipline that emerges in dialectical response to artificial intelligence rather than in service of it. Unlike AI-augmented creative practices, Orthogonal Art is structurally defined by occupying the generative and conceptual spaces that current AI systems cannot access. As a founding instantiation of this framework, the paper presents a novel artistic practice in which technical schematics serve as the primary medium. A significant secondary contribution is the pedagogical dimension of the work: by grounding artistic practice in schematic logic and algorithmic structure, the framework provides an accessible entry point into the advanced field of Augmented Machines systems, enabling cross-disciplinary literacy within Humanities at the intersection of art, engineering, and philosophy.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond the Binary: Motivations, Challenges, and Strategies of Transgender and Non-binary Software Engineering Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20866)
*Isabella Graßl*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本研究通过13名跨性别和非二元性别软件工程学生的半结构化访谈，探究其在高等教育中的经历。研究发现性别认同对这些学生选择软件工程起间接作用，主要动机包括远程工作的吸引力和创造更具包容性技术的愿望。虽然参与者未报告大学内的直接歧视，但普遍遭遇言语侮辱、评判、不容忍和敌意，主要源于社会文化规范和缺乏代表性。研究呼吁高校提供更多制度性支持、结构性变革和Representation。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注专业软件工程领域中的性别少数群体，但对其教育阶段的经历关注不足。教育阶段是确保未来科技劳动力平等机会、预防排斥的关键时期，因此有必要探究跨性别和非二元性别学生在软件工程教育中的独特挑战与需求。

Method: 采用半结构化访谈法，对全球13名跨性别和非二元性别软件工程专业学生进行深度访谈，研究聚焦三个维度：入学动机、遭遇的障碍、以及提升学术环境包容性的策略。

Result: 研究发现性别认同对这些学生选择软件工程的决策起间接作用，主要影响因素包括远程工作的吸引力以及创造更具包容性技术的个人愿望。参与者虽未报告校内直接歧视，但普遍经历言语侮辱、评判、不容忍和敌意等消极体验，这些因素对其心理健康造成负面影响。这些挑战主要源于社会文化规范和Representation不足。尽管面临诸多障碍，学生仍坚定地选择继续学习，同时呼吁高校提供更多制度性支持、结构性变革和增强Representation。

Conclusion: 研究提出具体建议，强调高校应采取系统性措施支持所有学生，无论其性别认同如何，包括完善制度支持机制、推动结构性变革、以及增加跨性别和非二元性别群体的Representation。

Abstract: When software is designed by people from diverse identities and experiences, it is more likely to be inclusive and address a broader range of user needs. However, for transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, the path to becoming such creators may be marked by unique challenges. While existing research explores gender minorities in professional software engineering, limited attention has been given to their educational journey, a key phase for ensuring equal opportunities and preventing exclusion in the tech workforce. This study aims to address this gap by investigating the experiences of transgender and non-binary students in software engineering, with a particular focus on their motivations for entering the field, the obstacles they encounter, and potential strategies for fostering greater inclusivity within their academic environments. Based on 13 semi-structured interviews with transgender and non-binary students across the globe, we found that gender identity plays an indirect role in their decision to pursue software engineering. Key factors include the appeal of remote work and a personal desire to create more inclusive technologies. Although the participants did not report direct discrimination within their universities, many described experiencing verbal insults, judgment, intolerance, and hostility, all of which negatively impacted their mental health. These challenges often stem from socio-cultural norms and a lack of representation. Despite these obstacles, the students remain committed to their choice of study but call for greater institutional support, structural changes, and increased representation. From these findings, we suggest concrete steps to support students, regardless of gender identity.

</details>


### [252] [Preserving Decision Sovereignty in Military AI: A Trade-Secret-Safe Architectural Framework for Model Replaceability, Human Authority, and State Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.20867)
*Peng Wei,Wesley Shu*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文是一篇概念性/架构性论文（非实证研究），主张军事AI的核心战略问题不是"能否获得最强模型"，而是"决策主权"（decision sovereignty）——国家能否在分析模块来自商业供应商的情况下，仍保有对决策政策、任务路由、版本管理、约束与拒答逻辑、审计留痕与最终行动批准的权威控制。作者以2026年Anthropic与美国国防部争议、Maven项目历史及美/NATO/英情报界治理指引为动机，提出"贸易秘密安全"的Energetic Paradigm（EP）公开架构规范：供应商模型仅作可替换的分析组件，路由、约束、日志、升级与行动授权由国家持有的编排层掌控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿AI供应商与国家安全客户的关系，使一个结构性新问题显性化：一旦私营治理的模型嵌入军事工作流，供应商不仅影响技术性能，还能影响系统"可被使用"的运行边界条件（如拒答逻辑、安全护栏、更新节奏、服务撤回）。作者列举三条动因链：其一，2026年3月Anthropic—五角大楼公开争议显示私人供应商的安全边界被解读为国家行动的约束，五角大楼一度将Anthropic列为供应链风险；其二，Project Maven使AI驱动的情报处理制度化，并经NATO采购扩散为军事信息与决策基础设施；其三，既有公共部门采购指引虽强调版本控制、回滚权、供应商文档与部署前评估，但若系统核心逻辑集中于外部模型，架构性依赖依旧存在。现有文献多讨论节奏加速、不透明性、偏见、信任校准、问责缺口与决策委托漂移，而较少显式处理"供应商边界控制"（supplier boundary control）这一类别，因此需要专门定义与建模。

Method: 纯概念与架构论证，无实验。核心方法由五部分组成：(1) 定义"决策主权"为六项能力：政策主权（哪些输出可支撑哪些行动）、路由主权（任务在模块/回退/复核间的分配）、版本主权（替换、回滚、钉版、升级时机）、约束主权（拒答逻辑、升级阈值、使用边界）、审计主权（日志、溯源、可解释记录）、行动主权（建议采纳与致命/强制效果的最终批准），并明确其与"人在回路"的区别（名义在环≠主权）。(2) 建立供应商诱导边界控制的五路径威胁模型：政策注入、版本漂移、撤回杠杆、审计不对称、经由便利化的规范性漂移；作者强调这些路径不需要恶意意图，是普通商业治理与异常军事责任的结构性交互结果。(3) 给出六层公开架构：输入与溯源层／可替换分析模型层／主权编排层（路由、回退、置信门、升级、接口归一）／约束与复核层（政策校验、人工复核、红队触发、域限制由国家方所有）／授权层（重大后果行为不得仅凭模型输出自动执行，须指定人员或机构批准）／审计与回滚层（记录模型、版本、提示、上下文边界、规则触发、人工干预、行动结果）。核心原则为"模块化不对称"：模型可以商业供应且是最优，但不得成为系统最终仲裁者。(4) 提出"可替换性优于依赖性"原则（输出归一为通用schema、红线与授权阈值不嵌入厂商提示与厂商审核层、必须定义降级与失败模式、采购逻辑由消除依赖转向阻止依赖演化为"决策俘获"）与"人类权威作为系统属性"的四属性：可争辩性、可追溯性、升级结构、非自执行。(5) 给出七步公开安全操作序列，并以表1对"以模型为中心的集成"与"以主权为中心的集成"在九个维度上做风格化对比（边界来源、模型替换、撤回时失效模式、版本治理、可审计性、人类权威、联盟互操作性、战略主权等）。

Result: 得出的是一组可检验性有限的架构性主张而非量化结论：军事AI主权更多取决于"决定模型如何被使用、约束、替换与覆盖"的架构所有权，而非对每个模型的所有权；"有意义的人类控制"必须被实例化为架构属性（选项菜单、拒答与默认值本身不受供应商治理支配），否则人工监督在速度压力下会失效；负责任AI在国防领域应被理解为架构性要求而非规范性清单（满足伦理清单的单一外部模型依赖系统仍可能缺乏主权控制）；采购应把"可替换性"作为核心绩效指标，并扩展为"主权测试"——若某供应商改政策、撤服务或未通过认证，任务能否继续；联盟互操作性可通过"政策层标准化而非模型层标准化"实现（盟友不需同一模型，只需溯源、约束、审计日志、人工授权接口兼容）；该框架与零信任/高保障LLM部署（可追溯、 Compartmentalization、持续监督）及人机信任校准文献相衔接，使信任可被分散与限界。论文自述三项贡献：决策主权定义、供应商边界控制威胁模型、公开的贸易秘密安全架构规范；并列出四项后续研究任务（决策主权的经验性测量指标如替换韧性/政策层稳定性/审计完备性、主权编排与联盟规则及采购法的法律分析、组织层面的训练-条令-编制模型、降级通信与对抗欺骗与云中断条件下的评估）。

Conclusion: 军事AI的战略问题不只是"谁有最好的模型"，而是"当模型成为决策基础设施的一部分时，谁保有控制权"。商业模型进入军事工作流后，供应商治理会逐步塑造主权行动空间，这是战略设计问题而非合约不便利。核心论点（较窄且可辩护）：军事AI的主权性不取决于拥有每一个模型，而取决于拥有决定模型如何被使用、约束、替换与覆盖的架构。若成立，未来军事优势不仅取决于获取前沿AI能力，更取决于把这种能力嵌入体系而不交出决策边界的制度能力。局限：纯概念性、无实证验证、不公开EP内部方法与基准数据、不主张自主致命决策、亦不宣称架构重构可消除全部风险；此外EP自我定位与"贸易秘密安全"的取舍使该规范难以被第三方核验，部分结论依赖2026年争议的公开报道。

Abstract: Recent events surrounding the relationship between frontier AI suppliers and national-security customers have made a structural problem newly visible: once a privately governed model becomes embedded in military workflows, the supplier can influence not only technical performance but also the operational boundary conditions under which the system may be used. This paper argues that the central strategic issue is not merely access to capable models, but preservation of decision sovereignty: the state's ability to retain authority over decision policy, version control, fallback behavior, auditability, and final action approval even when analytical modules are sourced from commercial vendors. Using the public Anthropic--Pentagon dispute of 2026, the broader history of Project Maven, and recent U.S., NATO, U.K., and intelligence-community guidance as a motivating context, the paper develops a trade-secret-safe architectural formulation of the Energetic Paradigm as a layered, model-agnostic command-support design. In this formulation, supplier models remain replaceable analytical components, while routing, constraints, logging, escalation, and action authorization remain state-owned functions.
